Raw results, real battles, one-off checks, commands: `analysis/meta_1.3/`, `analysis/meta_1.4/` (README in each). Quote game version with ANY meta claim, the two differ.
- Data: `units.json` = 1.4.6, `units_1.3.11.json` = 1.3.11 + mod tag `game.1.3.11.mod.25`. NEVER rebuild `units.json` while the game is on 1.3 (writes 1.3 data under a 1.4 label). 1.3 run = `EU5_UNITS=<units_1.3.11.json>` + `--rules 1.3` (`S.RULES_13`). Sims always use every core at the lowest priority (`engine.js`), game running or not. `EU5_THREADS=n` only caps workers.
- 1.3 (read from exe, 30 of 33 real battles): no REGIMENT_SIZE morale factor, only W frontage per section may engage, melee picks engaged enemies first, 2-hour ticks. Initiative caps at effective 5. User campaign is limited by manpower, not gold: quote equal manpower. Meta: heavy cavalry to age III; age IV half heavy half light + ~10% cannons; age V 20li 40hc 20lc 20art; age VI 20hi 40li 40art. Equal regiments moves to infantry at age V. Cannons are cheapest third category for combined arms, past 20% they lose. Wars agree with battles.
- 1.4 (rerun 2026-10-31 with the engage limit + engaged-first targeting, which 1.4 shares with 1.3; `ENGAGE_W` in engine.js, older runs in `results/pre_fix`): pure cavalry to age III. Mixed wins battles from age IV only via combined arms (cliff at 50%). Pure cavalry wins WARS to age V. Levy infantry is worth about 0.4 of a regular at ages II-III, 0.6-0.7 after. Docs sections 1 to 11a and 16 quote runs from before the fix, not rerun.
- D009 Maghreb cavalry (Sanhaja, Zenata, Tuareg) raises 3% of tribesmen, vanilla 10% (only land levies on tribesmen above 2%): `TRY_REPLACE:` in `in_game/common/levies/abm_d009_maghreb_levies.txt`, rate `abm_levy_maghreb_cavalry_size` in `main_menu/common/script_values/abm_levy_values.txt`. Whole levy Accepted vs a plain country of same population: 1.5x / 1.0x / 0.9x at ages I-III (vanilla 5.2x / 2.1x / 1.6x). Ars Belli regiment size nerf covers steppe hordes.
- TRY_REPLACE needs the full body (REPLACE resets the entry) and copies a DLC block, listed in `replaced_files.txt`. Levies merge via jomini gamedatabase.h: sort by prefix, so plain entries load first whatever the file order; TRY_ skips a missing key silently (right for players without the DLC), plain REPLACE logs an error. Check a size in a real save after changing it: a clean log does not prove the entry bound.

- Dice timing fixed 2026-10-31: 1.4 compares the phase counter first, so re-rolls fall on days 4, 5, 9, 13, not 4, 8, 12. 1.3 was already right, its results are unchanged. The 1.4 meta rerun was stopped after six runs (`analysis/meta_1.4/results/dice_fix_partial`), the rest of `results/` and section 11b still predate the fix.

### 3q. Battle length and armies leaving a battle (2026-10-31)
- Rules, table, side effects: `EU5 Combat Mechanics (1.4.4).md` 11d. Scripts, raw results, commands: `.tools/scripts/battle_sim/analysis/battle_length/`. Model: `simulateScenario(scn, { rotate })`.
- Leaving a battle is a plain move order, free, gated only by the BATTLE's age (`MINIMUM_COMBAT_DAYS` + `retreat_delay`). No script hook fires when an army leaves. Don't re-investigate.
- Slower armies barely help. ACTIVE since 2026-11-01 in `01_abm_defines.txt`: both land damage x2, `LAND_MORALE_DAMAGE_MODIFIER` 0.5, `LAND_STRENGTH_DAMAGE_MODIFIER` 0.08. Battles about 40% shorter, infantry relay 58%, cavalry relay 96% with the slower armies and heavy infantry 2.25. Before release rerun focus and army balance.
- The first version of this (2026-10-30) ran without the engage limit and said infantry relay wins 45% and damage x2 is enough. Wrong: 97%, and x2 leaves 61%. Those runs are in `results/pre_fix`.
- Hourly ticks cannot return in a mod: combat doc section 13.

### 3t. Country simulator levies, Castile against Morocco (2026-10-31)
- `location_sim` now picks levies as the game does: DLC levies, cultures, `country_allow`, `allow`. A condition it can't read is listed by `levy_report.js`, none are left. Rows rounded down give Morocco's top-bar total: 8,517 exactly on 1338.4.8, 7,703 vs 7,701 on 1337.9.26. All 21 rows of Tadla match too.
- Both `from_save.js` load DLC data. The battle one also gained reforms, religion and `--no-general`; without religion, damage against other faiths never applied in save-based battles.
- Army Levies Recovery panel = save `army_levies`. Its percentage feeds the rows, its men do not: `max` is the levy at the last raise (Location doc section 4).
- Levy regiments of one unit type merge across provinces, so a stack is full regiments plus one partial.
- Study: `.tools/scripts/battle_sim/analysis/castile_morocco/`. `battle_sim/progress.js` prints % and time left for long runs.

### 3u. Levy focus against Standing Forces (2026-10-31)
- `.tools/scripts/battle_sim/analysis/levy_vs_standing/` (README). A country against itself after 10, 30, 50 years of economy, armies at 75% of monthly manpower plus the whole levy. Levy wins: Castile 89/62/60, Morocco 99/99/99.
- Applied by the user's call: Nobles Levy Size +200% to +100%, Non-Culture Levy Size +25% to +15%, Levy Size stays +20%. Castile 83/52/59, Morocco 93/92/94, so Levy is still the stronger pick, most of all for Morocco. Released in game.1.4.6.mod.27.
- On wooded hills Morocco is about even, 45/47/48, because frontage drops from 10 to 5. Terrain modifiers alone cost about 10 points; frontage alone takes it to 41/49/52 (`terrain_split.js`). Frontage counts regiments, and most of its extra levy regiments are 200-man cavalry.
- Not taken: culture bonus off, size +10%, nobles +100% together give Castile 68/43/47, Morocco 48/41/50.
- `economy.js` can value manpower (`@mv`, `@from`) and `--unlock` advances. Armories first thing put Castile in debt; best for year 50 is economy to year 30, then armories.
- Open: Morocco's manpower, save 39 a month, simulator 15.

### 3v. Iberia MP playtest changes (2026-11-01, on update/1.4, not played yet)
- Test ran Workshop game.1.4.6.mod.27, game files same as update/1.4 then. Saves `MP_GRA_*_2daa983e-*`. User = GRA.
- Levy focus: Designated Instruction +30% -> +15% (`regular_levy_training` in focus_data.js).
- Maghreb rate `abm_levy_maghreb_cavalry_size` 3% -> 2%.
- Tribal size: `in_game/common/unit_types/abm_tribal_levy_sizes.txt`, INJECT/TRY_INJECT `max_strength = 0.2` on six units. Stat lines ADD, 0.2 + 0.2 = 400 men (vanilla steppe horde 0.2 + 0.4 = 600). FIRST INJECT in unit_types: check in game, regiment tooltip must show 400. `a_steppe_horde` left alone (mod's own 200), `a_chieftains` left alone (nobles).

- Event timeout: `NGame.EVENT_MP_TIMEOUT_MONTHS` 3 -> 5. One define for all events. Read only in CEventWindow constructor (`0x147c1bcd0`): date window created + months. Expiry check `0x147c1dd60`. Code does not explain 1.5 month reports. `NDiplomacy.AUTO_TIME_OUT_MONTHS` 2 is diplomacy offers, left alone.
- Strait: `in_game/events/DHE/flavor_cas_rio_salado.txt` same-name copy, calls to .3 (Granada) and .4 (Portugal) removed, events stay defined. Refuse cost -20 prestige -20 legitimacy, not -50 stability. Situation's own Force Tribute and Demand War Entry kept, Morocco's `mor_vassalize_granada` kept.

One check added to `vassalize_granada`'s allow, `c:GRA ?= { is_ai = yes }`, tooltip `abm_bfts_granada_is_ai`. The button stays and greys out. An outright removal with a copied panel was tried first and dropped the same day. Event .31 and Morocco's action unchanged. Against a played Granada Castile's situation win needs Granada conquered. Check in game: with a played Granada the button greys out and names that reason. If not, the copy did not bind.
- Speed: ARMY 0.9 -> 0.675, NAVY 3.525 -> 2.64375. Numbers mine, user gave none.
- Battles: SHORTER, both land damage x2, the user's pick. `to change.txt` says longer; x2/3 and then morale x4 + strength x3 were tried first and reversed the same day. 30 v 30: infantry II 34 to 21 days, cavalry II 39 to 23, infantry V 19 to 13. `engine.js` holds the mod's modifiers, battle_length scripts reset to vanilla.

- Big stacks: morale x4 + strength x3 was dropped because infantry V 7 armies v 5 won only 49% (vanilla 100%), with 56% of the loser's reserve unused. Now, both x2 and heavy infantry 2.25: 6 v 6 44 days v 69, 7 v 5 81%, 7 v 6 69%, mixed age V 91%, infantry II 100%, cavalry 99%. Combat doc 11d, `big_battles.js`, `big_settings.js`. Not tested in game.
- Heavy infantry combat speed 2 -> 2.25: `in_game/common/unit_categories/abm_army_heavy_infantry.txt`, REPLACE with the full block, the user's number. Age V stacks stall because reserves join at combat speed x 0.1 a day. Infantry V 7 v 5 at 2 / 2.25 / 2.5 / 3: 64 / 81 / 91 / 100%. Light infantry needs nothing, 98-100%. Holding armies next door is worse. `reserve_speed.js`. REPLACE binds in unit_categories: 58 of 67 armies with auxiliaries in the 1371 save match the mod's 7000 storage. Check in game: a Musketeer tooltip shows 2.25.
- Public battle sim page NOT republished: players on mod.27 until next release. Republish at release.

- Castile against Morocco after 2%, 400 men and both x2: start 40/11 to 34/8, as they stand 95/68 to 98/78, full levies 61/23 to 77/42. castile_morocco README.

### 3r. Lend Army to War Leader (`lend_unit_to_ally`) (2026-10-31, not changed)
- Scripted interaction, in `country_interactions/lend_unit_to_ally.txt`, so `ai_will_do` can be edited. Same-name file, not `INJECT`.
- The real block is levies. The army list refuses any army with a levy, mercenary or prisoner regiment; vanilla's comment says the lend silently no-ops on levies. Three 1.4 saves: 5 of 76, 8 of 76, 9 of 96 subject armies can be lent, 2-4% of regiments (`analysis/meta_1.4/subject_armies.js`). Liberty desire is not it: 342 of 354 subjects are under 25.
- AI rules: subject case +80 needs loyalty 50+, liberty desire under 25, strength under 0.45 of the overlord. -1000 if the actor's `lowest_war_score` < -15, so a subject of a losing overlord never lends. Checked every 30 days, 6-month cooldown.
- Levies cannot be lent whatever the AI wants. The route that avoids this is `appanage_request_levies`: overlord-side, moves 12 months of the subject's manpower. Vanilla: appanages only, subject not at war.

### 3s. Movement spread crash (1.3.11 only)
- Crash: AV on `Network/Session thread`, top frame `ffxFsr2ResourceIsNull (+ 18700188)` = eu5+0x4b0b0cc, daily tick, `CMovement` spread (movement.cpp). NOT disease. Found 2026-10-30, 1568 MP game, Lutheranism movement. Two machines die same tick, same table slot. Reload no help.
- Why: `FUN_144b0ab90` loops over regiments that carry a movement (X, CMovement+0x178 set, 1595 ids, save key `#39a1`), keeps a pointer to X's slot in a hash map, then loops X's army mates (Z) and inserts Z in the same map. An insert can grow the map; the old table is freed and the next Z read uses a stale pointer. Only unsafe site found. Location -> unit inserts refetch the pointer, safe.
- Trap: the set vtable says `CLocationSet` but ids are SUB-UNIT ids (save `#31dd`), high byte = generation.
- Fix on the 1.3 line only: `NSpreadable.ESTIMATED_MIXING_WITHIN_UNITS = 0` in `loading_screen/common/defines/01_abm_defines.txt`, on branch `release/game.1.3.11.mod.25-crash-fix`. Side effect: regiments catch a movement only from the location. 1.4.6 reads X once before the loop, bug gone, so 1.4 has no such define.

### 4. Economy & Town Setups
- Custom building setups for different cultures/regions in `in_game\common\town_setups\00_default.txt`.
- Tweaks to prices and societal values.
- **`merchant_republic` availability (2026-10-27)** - `REPLACE:merchant_republic` lives in `in_game\common\government_reforms\abm_gov_reforms_europe.txt` (it was general.txt earlier - check there if it moves again). `potential` is now two branches: (a) `religion.group = religion_group:christian` AND `original_capital` in western or eastern Europe, no societal requirement; (b) `original_capital` in north_africa or middle_east with ANY religion, but `>= 50` on `serfdom_vs_free_subjects` (free subjects), `aristocracy_vs_plutocracy` (plutocracy), `individualism_vs_communalism` (individualism) and `capital_economy_vs_traditional_economy` (capital economy). Societal values run `SOCIETAL_VALUE_MIN = -100` / `MAX = 100` (`loading_screen\common\defines\00_defines.txt`), so positive = the right-hand term of the pair - that is what makes "50+ plutocracy" mean `aristocracy_vs_plutocracy >= 50`.

### 3m. Map modes: Bailiffs, Create Town, Create City (2026-10-28)
- **Files:** `in_game/gfx/map/map_modes/abm_map_modes.txt` (`abm_bailiffs` in economy group 2 next to Max Control; `abm_create_town` + `abm_create_city` in economy group 1 next to Location Rank), rules in `in_game/common/scripted_triggers/abm_map_mode_triggers.txt`, thresholds in `script_values/abm_map_mode_values.txt` (`abm_bailiff_min_tax_base = 8`, `abm_town_rich_rgo_tax_base = 15`; the loc quotes both numbers), loc `main_menu/localization/english/abm_map_modes_l_english.yml` (`mapmode_<key>_name` + `MAPMODE_<KEY>`, legend + tooltip keys). Vanilla 1.4.4 map modes are pure script (`map_color`, `secondary_map_color` = stripes, `tooltip_key`, `legend_key`; root = location, `scope:actor` = viewing country); the `@data_*` gradient constants are file-local to vanilla `map_modes.txt`, so ours spell out the values. Only the actor's own locations are coloured. **Advanced Create Town** (`abm_advanced_create_town`, same category and index, added 2026-10-30) sits beside Create Town, which stays for comparison: tiers come from the RGO cut and the province's food, see "Advanced Create Town map mode" in `EU5 Location Mechanics (1.4.4).md`. Its `abm_adv_town_*` values, triggers and `ABM_ADV_TOWN_MM_*` loc share nothing with the old mode except `abm_has_high/medium_value_raw_material`, `abm_has_food_8_raw_material` and the urban/cannot tooltips. `urbanise.js` repeats the tier in JavaScript as `adv tier`; keep both in step.
- **Bailiffs:** light blue = has one; green = rural, not overseas, integrated or core, tax base >= 8; yellow = same but tax base < 8 with a high-value raw material; dark green = worth it but still conquered/colonized; red = eligible but not worth it; blue-black = town/city/overseas.
- **Create Town** (rural only; a town loses the rural +100% RGO size + food bonus, -10% food): light green = cheap goods (clay, sand, stone, medicaments); green = medium goods (price 1.5-2: lumber, coal, tin, lead, fiber, saltpeter, wine, beeswax, fur) or food 3-4 (olives, fruit, wild game); yellow = food 5 (fish, millet, legumes) or medium goods with tax base >= 15 (big RGO a town would halve); red = food 8+ (rice, wheat, maize, potato, livestock), last; dark red = high-value goods, keep rural; black stripes = population < 5 (thousand); blue-black = already urban / cannot upgrade.
- **Create City** (towns only; a city keeps the RGO, -20% food instead of -10%, so only food matters): light green = no food; green = food 3-4 (incl. wool); yellow = food 5; red = food 8+; black stripes = population < 30; light blue = city/megalopolis; blue-black = rural / cannot upgrade.
- **Goods lists** are explicit (from vanilla default prices / food values; no script accessor for `default_market_price` was verified): high value = default price >= 2.5 (29 goods incl. wool, incense, iron, silver, gold, saffron, dyes). Retune the lists if goods change in a game update.
- **Or Qapi province RGO decision (2026-10-19):** `abm_develop_or_qapi_rgos` (economy decisions) sets `kezlev` -> stone only. `kalay`, `aqmecit`, `jurchi` and `or_qapi` are not touched (kalay keeps wool, aqmecit horses, jurchi fiber_crops, or_qapi salt). Gate `abm_can_develop_or_qapi_rgos` in `in_game\common\scripted_triggers\abm_pontic_steppe.txt`: every owned land location in `or_qapi_province` must pass `abm_location_settled_and_loyal` (full core, `integration_level = core`; owner is a monarchy/theocracy/republic and non-pagan; dominant culture primary/accepted; dominant religion = owner's). **One use per campaign, globally:** `only_once = yes` plus the global flag `abm_or_qapi_rgos_developed` (`set_global_variable` in the effect, `NOT = { has_global_variable = ... }` in `potential`).
- **Gothia RGO decision (2026-10-19, revised 2026-10-20):** `abm_develop_gothia_rgos` sets `qirq_yer` -> coal, `qarasuvbazar` -> coal, `lusta` -> lumber, `theodoro` -> lumber. `gothia_province` = theodoro qirq_yer lusta qarasuvbazar crimean_mountains (crimean_mountains is wasteland, filtered by `has_owner`/`is_land`). Shared `abm_location_settled_and_loyal` gate, requires owning all three targets, campaign-global `only_once` + `abm_gothia_rgos_developed`. Province is split at start: FEO owns theodoro/lusta (passes), CRI owns qirq_yer/qarasuvbazar (steppe_horde - fails the government rule until it settles).
- **Cafa RGO decision (2026-10-18):** `abm_develop_cafa_rgos` sets `soldaia` -> lumber, `vosporo` -> iron, `kaffa` -> dyes; `solkhat` and `seyitler` keep vanilla goods (they were dropped on 2026-10-20). The location is `kaffa`; the province is `cafa_province` = kaffa soldaia vosporo seyitler solkhat. Same gate and campaign-global `only_once` + `abm_cafa_rgos_developed`; requires owning all three targets. Caveat: `lumber` at soldaia (grasslands) fails lumber's `location_potential`, but `change_raw_material` does not validate.
- **Three provincial RGO decisions share one shape:** `abm_develop_or_qapi_rgos` / `abm_develop_cafa_rgos` / `abm_develop_gothia_rgos` all live in `in_game\common\decisions\abm_pontic_steppe_decisions.txt`, loc in `in_game\localization\english\abm_pontic_steppe_l_english.yml`, all three provinces are in `crimea_area` -> `steppes_region` (there is no `pontic_steppe` region and no `crimea_province`). `REPLACE:`/`INJECT:` do not work for events or decisions - decisions are plain new keys, which is why no vanilla file is touched. All three downgrade the RGO to level 1 before changing it (same `change_max_raw_material_workers = { value = rgo_workers subtract = 1 min = 0 multiply = -1 }` idiom as the steppe action). **The three decisions are FREE** (2026-10-20) - no price, no stability/political-influence gates, no `add_gold`; only the generic action charges. The 'cost per location' paragraph in the steppe-action entry below applies to `abm_reorganize_pastoral_rgo` only.
- **Seven historical mineral decisions (2026-10-25):** `in_game\common\decisions\abm_steppe_minerals_decisions.txt`, loc `in_game\localization\english\abm_steppe_minerals_decisions_l_english.yml`. `abm_develop_luhan_rgos` (luhansk, mityakinskaya -> coal), `abm_develop_bakhmut_rgos` (kreminna, lysychansk -> coal), `abm_develop_tana_rgos` (kalmius -> coal), `abm_develop_izium_rgos` (barvinkove -> iron), `abm_develop_kursk_rgos` (oskol -> iron), `abm_develop_belgorod_rgos` (muzhech -> iron), `abm_develop_donetsk_rgos` (khotmyzh, kotelva -> lumber). All free, `only_once` + per-decision global flag, RGO reset to level 1 before the swap; gate is the new parameterised `abm_can_develop_province_rgos = { PROVINCE = province:<x> }` in `abm_pontic_steppe.txt` (scope parameter used as block root - same as vanilla `crusade_target_relations_valid_trigger` with `$ACTOR$ = { ... }`). Starting owners: luhan_province CRI+GLH, bakhmut_province CRI+ZAZ+GLH, tana_province CRI (+GAZ tana), izium_province ZAZ+GLH, kursk_province BRY+GLH(oskol), belgorod_province BRY+GLH(korocha), donetsk_province ZAZ alone. Government types: CRI/GLH steppe_horde (fail `abm_location_settled_and_loyal`), ZAZ republic, BRY monarchy - so in practice only donetsk_province (ZAZ, republic, owns all six) can be done without conquest/reform. Note the locations are NOT where the province names suggest: `donetsk_province` holds Kharkiv/Poltava towns, and the location `kalmius` is inside `tana_province`.
- **Caucasus & Ural non-ferrous metals (2026-10-25):** four more decisions appended to `abm_steppe_minerals_decisions.txt` (loc in the same yml), all free / `only_once` / per-decision global flag / RGO reset to level 1 / gated on `abm_can_develop_province_rgos`: `abm_develop_majar_rgos` (majar_province, `yessentuq` -> copper), `abm_develop_terek_rgos` (terek_province, `naurskaya` -> copper), `abm_develop_stavropol_rgos` (stavropol_province, `shad_kirman` -> lead) and `abm_develop_taskala_rgos` (taskala_province, `taskala` -> copper + `chertanla` -> tin). That gives 3 copper, 1 lead and 1 tin, all inside `majar_area` / `lower_yik_area` as requested (astrakhan_area has no defensible metal deposit, so nothing was placed there). Rationale: Caucasus foothills copper/lead-zinc (Sadon-type argentiferous galena) for majar/terek/stavropol, and the Kargaly copper field plus Kazakh-margin tin on the Orenburg/Ural edge for taskala. Copper, tin and lead all exist as `goods:copper` / `goods:tin` / `goods:lead` and **none of them define `location_potential`**, so any terrain can take them with no error-log risk. 1337 owners: GLH owns all five locations (GLH is `steppe_horde`, so the decisions are dormant until it settles); `taskala` and `chertanla` are also partly NOG. Watch the `goods:` id gotcha: the raw-material file declares `lumber  = {` with two spaces, which breaks naive `^([a-z_]+) = \{` validators - it is a real good.

- **Steppe pastoral RGO reorganisation (2026-10-19):** two objects. `abm_reorganize_pastoral_rgo` in `in_game\common\generic_actions\abm_steppe_rgo_conversion.txt` (type `owncountry`, three chained `select_trigger`: province `source = actor`, location `source = target_province` + `source_flags = only_actual_locations`, goods `interaction_source_list` over `every_goods`; effect `change_raw_material = scope:target_good` on `scope:target_location`), plus `abm_reorganize_pastoral_rgo_decision` in `abm_pontic_steppe_decisions.txt` whose option does `fire_generic_action = { type = abm_reorganize_pastoral_rgo actor = root }`. **`owncountry` generic actions have NO automatic UI** - the Action modding wiki lists GUI fetch functions for parliament (`GovernmentView.GetParliamentActions`), religious (`CountryReligionLateralView.GetReligiousActions`), religiousfaction, internationalorganization, situation and disaster, but none for `owncountry`, and the Decisions lateral view only renders `DecisionItem`s under its 7 `decision_category` filters (a generic action has no category). **`fire_generic_action` is the vanilla-blessed 'prompt the player' bridge** - `on_religion_changed` in `on_action\_hardcoded.txt` uses it with no `is_ai` guard and the comment 'make sure that the country is prompted to join another AP'. So the decision supplies the UI and the action supplies the pickers, with no GUI file touched (a `right_panel.gui` button version was written and then reverted). Gates in `abm_pontic_steppe.txt`: `abm_steppe_pastoral_province` (province scope, takes a `COUNTRY` parameter so it works from both scopes - `{ COUNTRY = scope:actor }` in the action, `{ COUNTRY = root }` in the decision; `region = region:steppes_region`, no owned land location failing `development >= 10` + `abm_steppe_pastoral_location` (the old `province_average_development >= 20` was unreachable: the highest steppe province is cafa at 16.75)), `abm_steppe_pastoral_location` (`integration_level = core` + owner = $COUNTRY$ + majority primary/accepted culture + majority owner religion + `abm_steppe_pastoral_good`), `abm_steppe_pastoral_good` (wool horses millet legumes fiber_crops livestock wheat) and `abm_steppe_target_good` (same seven + wine + sand - **lumber was removed on 2026-10-25**, it has its own action). Per-good restrictions live in the goods `select_trigger`'s `visible`: sand needs `scope:target_location = { is_coastal = yes }`. (Lumber used to be here too; it was split into its own action on 2026-10-25 - see the timber entry below.)  `$PARAM$` can carry a scope: vanilla's `has_pentarchy_modifier` does `switch = { trigger = $location$ }` and is called `{ location = scope:target_pentarchy }`. `sand` is hidden unless the *picked location itself* is coastal (`scope:target_location = { is_coastal = yes }`); the location's current good is excluded. The effect resets the working to level 1 with the vanilla `change_max_raw_material_workers = { value = rgo_workers subtract = 1 min = 0 multiply = -1 }` idiom (same as `coal_prospecting` / `columbian_exchange`), applied BEFORE `change_raw_material`. 'Sturdy grain' is the en name of `millet`. `province_average_development` is a province trigger (`province_triggers.txt`) while `development` is the province attribute column for list display - don't swap them. `cache_targets` must NOT be set on the goods stage (list depends on the chosen location). Cost per location changed: 10 stability + 10 political influence (in the price object) + 4x the location's wealth in gold. The gold cannot live in the price object - `price_modifier` multiplies the WHOLE price (proof: vanilla `remove_accepted_culture` is `stability = 2` and its action's modifier is culture% x100, so stability scales too), so gold is charged in the effect: `add_gold = { value = -100 subtract = { value = <location>.development multiply = 10 } max = -100 min = -300 }`, i.e. 100 gold floor + 10 per point of DEVELOPMENT, capped at 300. Development was chosen over `location_potential_tax_base` (the UI's 'wealth') because wealth values are tiny and flat (vanilla disaster payments multiply it by 7-20 to reach meaningful sums), while development is the number that also caps RGO size and spans 1-25 across the steppe (dev 10 -> 200g, dev 20+ -> 300g cap). Negative-value clamping gotcha: with a negative running value the cost cap is `min` and the floor is `max`. 'Location wealth' = `location_potential_tax_base` - that is what `Location.GetTotalPossibleTaxBase` shows next to the wealth icon in `location_tooltips.gui`, and what the `province_location_wealth` province column sums. `political_influence_severe_penalty` is -10 and `stability_severe_penalty` is -12, so -10 is a normal vanilla magnitude; stability is a -100..100 scale in EU5, not EU4's -3..+3. Once per location: the effect sets the location variable `abm_steppe_rgo_converted`, the location `visible` excludes it and the province gate requires at least one unconverted location. It is deliberately ours-only - the Columbian Exchange never reads it, so CE can still change an already-reorganised location. Loc: `in_game\localization\english\abm_steppe_rgo_conversion_l_english.yml`.

- **Timber working, separate from the pastoral action (2026-10-25):** `abm_establish_timber_working` (generic action, `owncountry`) + `abm_establish_timber_working_decision`, in the same three files as the pastoral pair (`in_game\common\generic_actions\abm_steppe_rgo_conversion.txt`, `...\decisions\abm_pontic_steppe_decisions.txt`, `...\prices\abm_steppe_rgo_conversion.txt`, loc in `...\localization\english\abm_steppe_rgo_conversion_l_english.yml`). **Why separate:** the user wanted lumber to cost 1500 gold instead of the pastoral 100-300, and to be capped per province and per area. **Cost:** `price:abm_timber_working_price` = `stability = 10`, `political_influence = 10`, `gold = 1500`. Gold can live directly in the price object here because there is no `price_modifier` to scale the other two. The decision's `allow` mirrors it with `gold >= 1500`, `stability >= 10`, `political_influence >= 10` so the button greys out instead of silently failing. **Same gates as the pastoral action:** province picker `visible` uses `abm_steppe_pastoral_province = { COUNTRY = scope:actor }`; location picker uses `abm_steppe_pastoral_location`, `is_land`, `NOT = { has_variable = abm_steppe_rgo_converted }`, and `OR = { has_river = yes is_adjacent_to_lake = yes }`. **One lumber per province:** the province picker adds `NOT = { any_location_in_province = { raw_material = goods:lumber } }` (belt-and-braces - `abm_steppe_pastoral_province` already fails once a location stops being pastoral). **Two area caps** (final, 2026-10-25): one `area = { ... }` block in the location picker holds two iterators that AND together - `any_location_in_area = { raw_material = goods:lumber count < 6 }` (six lumber locations per area in all; any lumber counts, including 1337 lumber) and `any_location_in_area = { owner = scope:actor has_variable = abm_timber_working count < 3 }` (three of them ours). Two `any_location_in_area` in one block is fine - duplicate iterators AND. The effect stamps `set_variable = { name = abm_timber_working value = yes }` on the converted location alongside `abm_steppe_rgo_converted`. A dedicated marker is used instead of counting `raw_material = goods:lumber` so that vanilla lumber - and land of the same country conquered later - does not eat into the allowance. Note the allowance is ownership-based: losing a working frees a slot. A bare `area = { ... }` block switches scope from a location to its area - vanilla proves it in `random_event.txt` (`capital ?= { area = { any_location_in_area = { ... } } }`) and `treaty_of_tordesillas.txt`. `count < N` inside an `any_*` iterator is vanilla (`country_triggers.txt` uses `count < 6` in `any_international_organization_member`). Effect = the same RGO-level reset idiom, `change_raw_material = goods:lumber`, then `set_variable = { name = abm_steppe_rgo_converted value = yes }` (shared with the pastoral action, so a location converted by either is closed to both). Lumber is no longer in `abm_steppe_target_good`, so the pastoral goods picker cannot offer it. The five **fixed** decisions that set lumber (`soldaia`, `lusta`, `theodoro`, `khotmyzh`, `kotelva`) are unaffected - they call `change_raw_material = goods:lumber` directly.

- **Gazaria's break with Genoa, as decisions (2026-10-25, converted from events the same day):** `in_game\common\decisions\abm_genoa_gazaria_decisions.txt` holds `abm_gazaria_independence` and `abm_gazaria_culture_choice`, both `decision_category = country_specific_decisions`, `only_once = yes`, `ai_will_do = 0`, loc in `abm_genoa_gazaria_l_english.yml` (9 keys). **The event versions were deleted** (`in_game\events\abm_genoa_gazaria_events.txt` and `in_game\common\on_action\abm_genoa_gazaria_on_actions.txt`, which also redefined `on_game_start` - now gone). **Why decisions win:** the event needed `is_human = yes` on BOTH GAZ and the GEN overlord, so a solo Gazaria player never saw the choice; a decision with `tag = GAZ` + `is_human = yes` works whoever runs Genoa. `abm_gazaria_independence` potential = `tag = GAZ`, `is_human = yes`, `is_subject = yes`, `overlord ?= { has_or_had_tag = GEN }`; options do `overlord ?= { cancel_subject = root }` (plus `create_alliance = { target = c:GEN }` behind a `country_exists = c:GEN` guard, because `has_or_had_tag` can match a GEN that no longer exists). GAZ starts as GEN's vassal - `main_menu/setup/1337/12_diplomacy.txt`: `dependency = { first = GEN second = GAZ subject_type = vassal }`. `abm_gazaria_culture_choice` (revised 2026-10-27) potential = `tag = GAZ`, `is_human = yes`, `NOT = { is_subject = yes }`, `culture = culture:ligurian`, **`owns = location:theodoro` and `owns = location:lusta`** - it only appears once the Gothic heartland has actually been conquered. **Options:** a = stay Ligurian (no mechanical change, sets `abm_gazaria_stayed_ligurian`), b = `change_culture = culture:gothic_culture` + `add_accepted_culture = culture:ligurian`. **Option c (Pontic Greek) is COMMENTED OUT as of 2026-10-28** - the block is preserved in the file with `#` prefixes and a note, because the Pontic culture legacy (sharing TRE's advances) is not implemented, so offering the culture without its inheritance would be a trap. The two loc keys `abm_gazaria_culture_choice.c` / `.c.tt` were deliberately left in place so restoring it needs no localization work. Still `only_once`, so Ligurian is a deliberate choice rather than a default the player ignores. `owns = location:X` is vanilla (`decisions/flavor_decisions_pol.txt` uses `owns = location:krakow`). Original gate was: independence is expressed as 'not a subject of anyone', not a flag, so peaceful release works too. Options: stay Ligurian (only sets `abm_gazaria_stayed_ligurian`, harmless but a real effect - avoid empty effect blocks) or go Gothic. **Culture ids: `ligurian`** (`cultures/italian.txt`, `italian_group`) **and `gothic_culture`** (`cultures/greek.txt`, `greek_group`) - note the `_culture` suffix, there is no bare `gothic`. the shift mirrors vanilla's `change_primary_culture` action (`change_culture` then `add_accepted_culture = culture:ligurian`), **but deliberately does NOT call `grant_culture_bonus_advances`** - that effect (see the vanilla comment in `cabinet_actions/merge_culture_group.txt`: 'Grant the bonus advances of the primary culture') hands over the whole culture-locked advance set instantly, bypassing the `can_change_primary_culture` check, the price and the 10-year cooldown. Removed 2026-10-25 after it was found to grant all six Theodoro `feo_*` advances to Gazaria in one click. Changing culture still makes those advances researchable, which is the intended cost. **Chosen culture group matters:** `gothic_culture`, `greek_culture` and `pontic_greek_culture` are all in `greek_group`, so a Gothic-cultured country also qualifies for the `greek_group_*` advance ladder.
- **Advance gating patterns (audited 2026-10-25):** vanilla has 1647 tag-gated (`has_or_had_tag = X`), 340 individual-culture-gated (`culture = culture:X`), 198 **culture-group**-gated (`culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:X }`) and 36 mixed `OR = { has_or_had_tag = X, culture = culture:Y }` advances (e.g. `cir_tribal_elections`). Mixing a tag gate with a culture gate is therefore vanilla-idiomatic, not a bug: whoever adopts the culture gains its advances. **Gotcha found the hard way: a scan for `culture = culture:` misses the 198 culture-GROUP advances** - the Greek ladder (`greek_group_*` in `advances/culture_group_greek.txt`) is group-gated, so `greek_culture` and `pontic_greek_culture` are not in a vacuum after all. Vanilla excludes **both** BYZ and TRE from those six (`NOR = { has_or_had_tag = BYZ has_or_had_tag = TRE }`); the mod's `REPLACE:` in `abm_anatolia.txt` **drops the TRE exclusion**, so TRE now gets the greek_group ladder plus its 14 tag advances. Tag-gated counts: **BYZ 25** (all vanilla, `aqueduct_system` + `expanded_aqueduct_system` shared with ROM) and **TRE 14** (3 are mod `REPLACE:` - `tre_komnenos_legacy`, `tre_pontic_trail_mastery`, `tre_pontic_mountains`; `tre_empire_in_exile` is shared with BYZ). **FIXED 2026-10-25:** the six `feo_*` advances in `abm_steppes_ural.txt` had `OR = { has_or_had_tag = FEO culture = culture:gothic_culture }`, so a BYZ/TRE that switched primary culture to Gothic kept its tag advances AND its greek_group ladder AND gained the Theodoro set - a net gain for nothing, and cheap because all three cultures share `greek_group`. The culture branch is now `AND = { culture = culture:gothic_culture NOR = { has_or_had_tag = BYZ has_or_had_tag = TRE } }` - the same exclusion vanilla applies to `greek_group_*` - while the `has_or_had_tag = FEO` branch stays unconditional, so a Theodoro that later forms something else keeps its own ideas. GAZ and any other Gothic convert still qualify. Rejected alternative: requiring Gothic-dominant owned land, which the user judged worse than simply denying BYZ/TRE.

- **Steppe Redoubts building (2026-10-25):** `abm_steppe_redoubts` in the new `in_game\common\building_types\abm_steppe_buildings.txt`, unlocked by `abm_steppe_redoubts_advance` appended to `abm_steppes_ural.txt` (`requires = castle_advance`, `unlock_building = abm_steppe_redoubts`, `allow_children = no`), loc in the new `in_game\localization\english\abm_steppe_l_*` style file `abm_steppe_buildings_l_english.yml`. Modelled on vanilla `fortress_church` (`religion_buildings.txt`), which is the only building that hardens the *existing* fort rather than adding a fort level. **+1/+2 battle dice rolls are NOT moddable:** there is no dice/roll modifier anywhere in `main_menu\common\modifier_type_definitions\00_modifier_types.txt` (the only matches for 'roll' are `reroll_avatar_cost_modifier` and `reroll_parliament_agendas_cost_modifier`). The full location-scope defensive toolkit is `fort_level`, `minimum_fort_level`, `local_defensive`, `local_garrison_size`, `local_garrison_growth`, `local_hostile_attrition`, `local_army_attrition`, `local_forced_attrition`, `max_siege_memory`, `local_fort_maintenance_efficiency`, `hostile_movement_cost`. Chosen: `local_defensive = 0.25` (in `raw_modifier`, exactly where fortress_church puts it), `local_garrison_size = 0.25` and `local_hostile_attrition = 1` (both in `modifier`; vanilla precedent `unique_buildings.txt:3052`, and `korean_gunnery_land_defense` uses the same `= 1` - note that building also sets `max_attrition = 1`, unconfirmed whether the hostile attrition bonus is capped without it). Gates now sit together in `location_potential` (see the final-shape entry below); `region` appears 9 times inside vanilla `location_potential` blocks. `country_potential = { has_advance = ... }` is the vanilla `caravanserai` trick for hiding a building from those without the tech. **Two gotchas:** (1) buildings have **no `icon` field** - zero vanilla buildings use one; the icon comes from the production method's `icon_type = building_type` / `icon = <building id>`, so the PM points at `icon = stockade` until an `abm_steppe_redoubts.dds` exists in `main_menu\gfx\interface\icons\buildings\`. (2) `pure_tooltip_entry = pte_no_propagating_zone_of_control` is just an extra tooltip **line** (the key exists in vanilla), it does not suppress anything - fortress_church pairs it with a `# propagating_zone_of_control = no` comment. **`defender` is NOT a modifier (investigated 2026-10-26):** it is a static field on `in_game\common\topography\00_default.txt` (mountains 2; hills, plateau, wetlands, narrows 1) and `in_game\common\vegetation\00_default.txt` (woods, forest, jungle 1). **flatland and grasslands give 0**, which is exactly why the open Pontic steppe is indefensible. It is absent from `00_modifier_types.txt`, and there is no `change_topography` / `change_vegetation` effect (only the `show_event_target_topography` / `show_event_target_vegetation` UI helpers), so no building, advance, modifier or event can add to it. **No location-scope combat-quality modifier exists at all:** `discipline`, `land_morale`, every `army_*_power`, `levy_combat_efficiency_modifier` and `damage_done_*` are all `category=unit`. A building can only reach those through `capital_country_modifier`, i.e. only from the capital and globally. Building modifier blocks in vanilla: `modifier` (353 uses), `raw_modifier` (49), `capital_country_modifier` (96), `capital_modifier` (21), `foreign_country_modifier` (3), `market_center_modifier` (12), `capital_to_overlord_modifier` (1) - there is no plain country-scope block. Nearest location-scope combat levers: `local_frontage_allowed`, `local_war_score_efficiency`, `max_attrition`, and military quantity (`local_manpower`, `local_manpower_modifier`, `local_levy_size_modifier`, `local_army_levy_size_modifier`, `local_levy_recruitment_speed_modifier`, `can_recruit_regiment_in_this_location`) - none of them has vanilla building precedent except the last four. **Final shape (2026-10-26, after three revisions):** a **single** building `abm_steppe_redoubts`, buildable in all four settlement types (`rural_settlement`/`town`/`city`/`megalopolis = yes`), on flatland only, inside `region:steppes_region`, gated on `has_advance = abm_steppe_redoubts_advance`. Both the region and the topography live in `location_potential` (vanilla `italian_administration_center` gates on `region = region:italy_region` the same way) - **not** in `allow`, because `allow` is only the 'enabled' check and would merely grey the button out elsewhere, whereas `location_potential` is the 'visible' check and hides the building outside the steppe entirely. `modifier = { local_garrison_size = 0.25  local_hostile_attrition = 1 }`; `raw_modifier = { local_defensive = 0.25  pure_tooltip_entry = pte_no_propagating_zone_of_control }` - i.e. exactly vanilla `fortress_church`'s defensive shape. **`minimum_fort_level` and `propagating_zone_of_control` were considered and deliberately dropped:** the user first wanted the fort level rural/town-only, which forced a second building entry (modifier blocks cannot be conditional), and then scrapped the fort level entirely, so the split was collapsed back to one entry and the second `unlock_building`, the `_urban` loc keys and both raw_modifier lines were removed. Consequence to remember: with no `minimum_fort_level`, the building does **nothing** in an unfortified location, because `local_defensive` only modifies an existing fort. If it is ever reinstated: `minimum_fort_level = 1` + `propagating_zone_of_control = no` in `raw_modifier` is the `city_walls` pattern (a fort level without zone of control), and scoping it by settlement type requires a second building entry. Reused vanilla `construction_demand = fort_construction` and `build_time = small_fort_building`; the maintenance PM is declared inline under `unique_production_methods` (the mod's own `abm_genoese_arsenal` pattern), so no new price/PM files were needed. **All seven mod-new buildings now have loc** (added 2026-10-25): `abm_steppe_redoubts` in `abm_steppe_buildings_l_english.yml`, and the five that were displaying as raw ids (`abm_genoese_arsenal`, `abm_gazarian_arsenal`, `abm_gazarian_headquarters`, `abm_church_school`, `matenadaran`) in the new `abm_buildings_l_english.yml`. Two more raw-id holes were in the same chain and were filled too: the `abm_the_genoese_arsenal` advance (appended to `abm_genoa_gazaria_l_english.yml`) and the `abm_eastern_christian_militant_church` government reform (new `abm_government_reforms_l_english.yml`). Buildings and reforms both use plain `<id>` / `<id>_desc` keys; advances use `<id>` / `<id>_desc` too and vanilla often writes the name as `"$<building_id>$"`. Beware when auditing: **the mod's `in_game\common\advances\` directory also holds copies of vanilla advance files** (e.g. `3_fort_level.txt`), so a naive 'every non-REPLACE advance in the mod needs loc' scan reports ~2300 false positives.

- **Cossacks estate gated to Christian rulers (2026-10-26):** new `in_game\common\scripted_triggers\abm_pop_triggers.txt` with `REPLACE:is_cossacks = { ... }`. **How estate availability actually works:** an estate definition (`in_game\common\estates\00_default.txt`) carries **no** availability trigger at all - none of the eight estates has `potential`/`allow`/`country_potential`, and there is **no `add_estate` / `remove_estate` effect** anywhere in the engine. An estate simply exists while some pop belongs to it: `in_game\common\pop_types\00_default.txt` maps each pop type to estates with lines like `cossacks_estate = { is_cossacks = yes }` / `dhimmi_estate = { is_dhimmi = yes }` / `tribes_estate = { is_gaelic_clans = yes }`, and those are scripted triggers in pop scope (`scripted_triggers/pop_triggers.txt`). So gating an estate = gating the corresponding pop trigger. Vanilla `is_cossacks` required `culture = culture:cossack_culture` plus an owner whose **primary culture** is in `russian_group`, `lithuanian_group` or `polish_group`; the mod adds `religion.group = religion_group:christian` inside that `owner ?=` block (same syntax vanilla's `is_dhimmi` uses for `religion_group:muslim`), which makes the estate appear only for Christian owners and vanish automatically when the country's religion group changes. `REPLACE:` **is** supported for `scripted_triggers` - the mod already does it in `abm_alliance_overrides.txt` (`alliance_can_exist`) and `crusade_target_overrides.txt` (`is_valid_target_for_crusade`). **Also fixed a live typo:** `in_game\common\estates\abm_estates.txt` had `RELACE:cossacks_estate` instead of `REPLACE:`, so that whole override was inert; the block is byte-identical to vanilla apart from formatting, so activating it changes nothing yet - but any future edit there was silently doing nothing. The mod also ships `in_game\common\pop_types\00_abm_default.txt` with `REPLACE:soldiers`, which calls `is_cossacks = yes` too, so both paths pick up the new gate. **Final gate (2026-10-26, fourth revision):** the pop must be `cossack_culture`, and the owner must be `religion.group = religion_group:christian` **AND** `OR = { monarchy, republic, theocracy }` (excludes `tribe` and `steppe_horde`) **AND** an OR group of the three culture-group checks **plus** `AND = { has_accepted_culture = culture:cossack_culture  original_capital ?= { region = region:steppes_region } }` - Cossack acceptance only counts for a country whose original capital is in the steppe, and a steppe capital on its own is NOT sufficient. The capital check sits INSIDE that OR (the user restructured it), so it widens the gate rather than narrowing it. **`original_capital` is a real country scope link** - vanilla `advances/region_indonesia.txt` does `original_capital ?= { region = region:indonesia_region }`, and `estate_privileges/tribes_estate.txt` does `capital ?= { region = region:maghreb_region }`. Using `original_capital` rather than `capital` stops a player conjuring the estate by moving their capital into the steppe. **1337 outcome:** kyiv is the only location with a cossack_culture pop (0.416 tribesmen, Orthodox) and KIE's primary culture is `ruthenian` (russian_group), religion orthodox, government not set explicitly (defaults to monarchy) - so **Kyiv starts with the estate**, tiny as it is. ZAZ fails: `steppe_horde` government and no cossack pops, though `kichkas` is in `steppes_region`. Note `kyiv` itself is in **ruthenia_region**, not steppes_region - it only qualifies through the culture branch. **Who gets it in vanilla (audited 2026-10-26):** the mapping appears once in *every* pop type (nobles, clergy, burghers, laborers, soldiers, peasants, tribesmen, slaves), so pop type is irrelevant - only pop culture and owner primary culture matter. `cossack_culture` is itself in `russian_group`. 86 tags at 1337 have a qualifying primary culture (russian_group = 13 cultures incl. ruthenian/muscovite/novgorodian, polish_group = 5, lithuanian_group = 2). **But the estate needs cossack_culture POPS, and the 1337 setup contains exactly one: 0.416 tribesmen, Orthodox, in `kyiv`** (only 1 `cossack_culture` line in the whole of `main_menu\setup\1337\06_pops.txt`). kyiv is owned by **KIE**, whose primary culture is `ruthenian` - so **Kyiv is the only country with a Cossacks estate at game start**, and it is negligible. **ZAZ starts with `cossack_culture` as primary culture** and `flavor_zaz.txt` re-applies `change_culture = culture:cossack_culture` plus `change_religion = religion:orthodox`, but it owns no cossack pops at 1337, so the estate only becomes real once assimilation produces them. The `cossacks_reform` government reform requires `culture = culture:cossack_culture` and `country_type = army`. Vanilla had **no religion condition at all** - a Muslim or Tengri ruler of a Russian/Lithuanian/Polish-cultured state would have qualified, which is exactly what the Christian gate closes.

- **Cultural regiments (2026-10-26):** 23 unit types originally in `in_game\common\unit_types\abm_cultural_units.txt` (now 20 after the circassian removal - see below), loc in `abm_cultural_units_l_english.yml`. Each culture furnishes 2-3 historical units: crimean (horse archer, ages 2-3), mishar (melee horse, 2-3), astrakhani (cheap horse archer, 3-4), gothic_culture (defensive heavy cav 2-3 **and** defensive heavy inf 3-4), cossack_culture (raid light cav 3-5 **and** defensive light inf 4-5), ligurian (offensive heavy inf 4-5), ruthenian (balanced heavy inf 4-6), circassian_culture (all-offence light cav 4-6). **Gate (revised 2026-10-26):** `country_potential = { OR = { culture = culture:X  AND = { has_accepted_culture = culture:X  culture = { cultural_view = { target = culture:X  value >= positive } } } }  original_capital ?= { region = region:steppes_region } }`. PRIMARY culture always qualifies; an ACCEPTED culture additionally needs our primary culture's view of it at `positive` or better (enum: enemy/negative/neutral/positive/kindred). `culture = culture:X` at country scope tests the primary culture - same test vanilla uses in `can_change_primary_culture` and `advances/country_cir.txt`. **The steppe-capital check is an AND on top of the whole OR**, and it uses `original_capital` rather than `capital` so relocating the capital into the steppe does not unlock the regiments. **The 3 Ruthenian units have their own three-route gate (2026-10-26):** `OR = { culture = culture:ruthenian  AND = { has_accepted_culture = culture:ruthenian  original_capital ?= { OR = { region = region:ruthenia_region  region = region:steppes_region } } }  has_or_had_tag = KIE  has_or_had_tag = UKR }`. So Kyiv qualifies despite its capital `kyiv` sitting in `ruthenia_region`, and Ruthenia (UKR) keeps them after forming. **No `cultural_view` check on this line** - the user specified the three routes exactly and opinion was not among them. `UKR` is a real tag: formable `UKR_f` (#Ruthenia) in `formable_countries/00_formable_countries.txt`, level 3, `tag = UKR`, `regions = { ruthenia_region }`, `allow = { owns = location:kyiv }`; it is **not** a 1337 country, which is why `has_or_had_tag` rather than `tag =` is used. **1337 effect (verified):** 13 of the 14 tags whose primary culture has cultural units qualify via the cultural gate - ATR, GLH (astrakhani); CIR, CPA, HTY, HYT, KDA, KIP, TEM (circassian); CRI (crimean); FEO (gothic); GAZ (ligurian); ZAZ (cossack). KIE qualifies separately through the Ruthenian line's own gate rather than the cultural one. **`cultural_view` is a culture-scope trigger**, so it needs the `culture = { ... }` wrapper; vanilla `script_values/io_policy.txt` does `root.culture ?= { cultural_view = { target = ... value >= kindred } }`. Direction is "our primary culture's opinion of them" - the user never confirmed the direction, so this is an assumption. **Unit-type conventions confirmed from vanilla `1_uniques_for_age_*.txt` (107 units):** `copy_from = a_age_N_<category>` + `is_special = yes` + `buildable = yes` is the whole recipe; unique units do **not** set `age`, `hide = no` or `category` (I set `category` explicitly since it matches the template, but vanilla usually omits it); 229 unit types omit `construction_demand`/`maintenance_demand` and fall back to the category, so none were invented. **`max_strength` and `combat_power` on a `copy_from` unit are RELATIVE, not absolute** (vanilla `a_varangians_2` sets `max_strength = 0.1` on top of an age-2 template whose own value is 1.0 - an absolute reading would have made an elite unit 10x weaker). **Neither is used by the mod's units (2026-10-27):** the user asked that unit STRENGTH not be modified, so all 11 `max_strength` lines were stripped and no `combat_power` was ever added - the units differ only through `*_damage_done` / `*_damage_taken`, `initiative`, `flanking_ability`, `supply_weight`, `build_time_modifier` and terrain `combat`. That keeps every regiment the same size as its age template, so the cultural lines are a trade in effectiveness, never in numbers. **Calibrated value ranges from vanilla:** initiative -4..5 (median 2, integer-ish), morale_damage_done 0.05..0.33 (median 0.10), strength_damage_taken -0.50..0.20 (median -0.10), flanking_ability up to 0.30, supply_weight typically -0.25, max_strength deltas ~0.10. **Avoided on purpose:** `secure_flanks_defense` (exactly ONE vanilla use, at 0.03), `movement_speed` (median 1.0 so its semantics are ambiguous - possibly absolute - and `impact = { <terrain> = x }` is the safe relative alternative), and `frontage` (one vanilla use). **The 5 Cossack units are gated differently from the other 18 (2026-10-26):** they are an *estate* privilege, not a cultural one, so they use `country_potential = { country_has_estate = estate_type:cossacks_estate }` plus `location_potential = { any_pop = { culture = culture:cossack_culture } }` - any country holding the Cossacks estate may raise them regardless of its own culture, but only where Cossack pops actually live, a handful being enough (no majority, no `dominant_culture` check). **`any_pop` inside a unit's `location_potential` is vanilla-proven** by `a_schiltron` in `1_uniques_for_age_1_traditions.txt`, which does `location_potential = { any_pop = { OR = { culture = culture:highland culture = culture:scottish } } }`. Note `any_pop` is a generic scope iterator, so it does **not** appear in `trigger_localization/`, which made a naive 'is this trigger real' grep report a false negative. Deliberately used `culture = culture:cossack_culture` rather than the `is_cossacks` scripted trigger: `is_cossacks` also re-checks owner religion/government/culture-group/capital, which the country gate already covers, and re-using it would double-gate. **`impact` is the in-game "Movement" block (2026-10-27):** per the wiki `Unit_modding`, `combat` sets combat power in a terrain while `impact` is what the UI calls Movement; both take vegetation/topography/climate keys. **NEGATIVE `impact` = FASTER**, which is counter-intuitive and easy to get backwards. Proof from vanilla: `a_bedouin_cavalry` has `desert = -0.75, arid = -0.75, arctic = +0.25`; `a_gebirgsschutzen_infantry` has `hills = -0.1, mountains = -0.25`; `a_hesse_jager` has `forest = -0.15, woods = -0.15`; while the base `a_light_cavalry`/`a_heavy_cavalry` templates carry `jungle/wetlands/mountains = +0.10` as a cavalry penalty. **All terrain values were flattened to exactly ±0.10 (2026-10-27) at the user's request, in BOTH `combat` and `impact`** - a temporary balance simplification, so `combat`/`impact` now only ever encode direction (bonus vs penalty), never magnitude. Re-introduce graduated values only if asked. **All 23 units now carry both blocks** and every culture has a coherent movement identity: hordes/Cossack horse are `-0.25` in flatland/grasslands/sparse and `+0.15..+0.25` in hills/mountains; gothic and circassian are the mirror image (fast in hills/mountains/plateau, `+0.10` in flatland); ruthenian is fast in woods/forest; ligurian fast on flatland, slow in mountains. **The unit file was renamed by the user to `in_game\common\unit_types\abm_units_steppes.txt`** (from `abm_cultural_units.txt`); the localization is still `in_game\localization\english\abm_cultural_units_l_english.yml`. The user also hand-softened the Crimean combat penalties from `-0.15/-0.20` to `-0.10` - do not revert their combat edits when touching `impact`. **Steppe terrain profile (2026-10-27):** the 11 horde and Cossack units share one shape - a bonus on open ground and a penalty on rough ground. Open ground uses `flatland` (topography) plus `grasslands`/`sparse` (vegetation); rough ground uses `hills`/`mountains` (topography). Horde horse archers (crimean, astrakhani) additionally take `woods = -0.10` and `forest = -0.15`; mishar, the melee line, does not, since the user asked for the woodland penalty on *archers* specifically. Cossack infantry keeps its `wetlands = +0.10` (a marsh is not a hill) but now takes `hills = -0.10` / `mountains = -0.15` like the horse, matching the user's 'Cossacks units' wording - flagged to them as slightly unusual for infantry. Untouched on purpose: gothic (hills/mountains bonus - mountain anvil), ligurian, ruthenian (woods/forest bonus), circassian (hills/plateau bonus - it is not a horde tag). **Still open:** the other 18 units are plain recruitable regiments with no `location_trigger`, so they can be raised anywhere once the country-level gate passes; `has_accepted_culture` inside a unit's `country_potential` has no vanilla precedent (vanilla unit gates use `culture`/`has_culture_group`/`has_or_had_tag`), though it is a normal country trigger elsewhere; `add_estate_private_regiment` exists if the Cossack units should ever become true estate private armies instead of normal recruitment.

- **Gazaria population rebalance (2026-10-27):** `main_menu\setup\1337\06_pops.txt` is the mod's own 5.4 MB copy of the vanilla pop setup and IS the file to edit for population. Values are in **thousands**, so `size = 82.653` = 82,653 people. **`main_menu/setup/**` files have NO BOM and use CRLF** (unlike `in_game/**`, which has a BOM) - reading with `encoding='utf-8-sig'` silently strips CRLF and adds a BOM, so restore `d.replace(b'\r\n',b'\n').replace(b'\n',b'\r\n')` and drop the BOM afterwards, or the whole 5 MB file gets rewritten. Rescaled seven locations by a uniform factor so the culture/religion/class mix is preserved exactly: kaffa 82.653 -> 80, taman 17.898 -> 50, vosporo 14.928 -> 50, soldaia 12.862 -> 50, tana 12.710 -> 40, theodoro 22.913 -> 60, lusta 10.102 -> 50. 61 `size` values changed, nothing else touched. **Second pass, same day, per the user's own numbers:** kaffa -> 80k, taman/vosporo/soldaia -> 50k each, tana -> 40k, theodoro -> 60k, lusta -> 50k (uniform rescale, mix preserved). **Third pass - the one that stands:** Gazaria settles at **256.30k** in 5 locations with deliberately non-round totals (kaffa **88.117**, vosporo 48.174, soldaia 46.905, taman 43.281, tana 38.546 (kaffa raised from 79.392 on 2026-10-28, uniform rescale so its 35-group composition is unchanged)). The four non-capital Gazarian towns were REBUILT, not scaled, with an explicit 5-group mix - pontic_greek_culture/orthodox, pontic_greek_culture/catholic, gothic_culture/orthodox, gothic_culture/catholic, ligurian/catholic - spread over nobles 0.6%, clergy 1.2%, burghers 13.5%, slaves 5.2%, peasants 79.5%. The culture split differs per town so they are not clones: vosporo 52/11/19/6/12 (Greek-dominated), soldaia 36/10/29/8/17 (Gothic-leaning), taman 33/11/21/6/29 (Ligurian-heavy), tana 29/12/16/5/38 (Ligurian-dominated trading post on the Don). **kaffa keeps its diversity** as asked - its 29 original groups were rescaled to 74.0k and a 5.39k gothic_culture quarter was appended, giving 35 groups and 13 cultures (ligurian 46.5%, crimean 11.1%, circassian 8.2%, armenian 8.1%, pontic_greek 8.1%, gothic 6.8%, abkhazian 5.1%, alan, georgian, turkish, ashkenazi, venetian, ruthenian). **Rest of Crimea buffed** to 17-28k per location, composition preserved by uniform scaling: qirq_yer 26.481, qarasuvbazar 18.927, seyitler 17.364, solkhat 24.038, kalay 19.415, kezlev 22.706, aqmecit 20.163, jurchi 23.859, or_qapi 27.412. **Resulting country totals:** GAZ 265.0k (5 loc), FEO 110k (2), **CRI 489.5k (79)**, GLH 956.7k (162), TRE 377.5k (10), GEN 457.9k (21), VEN 262.6k (17), MOS 352.5k (28), KIE 795.8k (84). **Theodoro got the same rebuild (2026-10-27):** `theodoro` -> **63.247** and `lusta` -> **56.108**, both REBUILT (not scaled) on the same five-group model but Gothic-dominant: theodoro = gothic/orthodox 58%, pontic/orthodox 22%, ligurian/catholic 8%, gothic/catholic 7%, pontic/catholic 5%; lusta = 49/33/7/6/5. Pop types there are nobles 0.9%, clergy 1.4%, burghers 9.0%, peasants 88.7% (theodoro) and 0.7/1.0/7.0/91.3 (lusta) - no slaves, unlike the Gazarian ports. FEO total is now **119.355k** in 2 locations, up from 110k. **Ownership was left alone** - the user chose 250-270k rather than expanding, so no land changed hands and the `abm_develop_or_qapi_rgos` / Tana coal decisions keep working for Crimea. **Editing trap:** these location blocks are closed by `\t}` on its own line. Do NOT `rstrip()` the replaced body - it glues `\t}` onto the last `define_pop` line and the next block's regex then swallows its neighbours (this silently produced a 154k `seyitler` on the first attempt; caught by re-checking totals). GAZ's ownership at 1337 is still only kaffa+vosporo (core) and tana (integrated); every other Crimean location except theodoro belongs to **GLH**, so handing them to GAZ is politically cheap.

- **Steppe regiment unlock advances (2026-11-08):** the 23 cultural regiments had NO advance, and a `buildable = yes` unit type is only recruitable once an advance `unlock_unit`s it - so they were unobtainable. New file `in_game\common\advances\abm_units_steppes.txt` holds one `<unit>_advance` per regiment (23 advances originally, 20 now, `content_priority = 900`), each `potential` mirroring that unit's own `country_potential` (culture, or accepted culture with `cultural_view ... value >= positive`, or `country_has_estate = estate_type:cossacks_estate` for the Cossack lines) **plus `original_capital ?= { region = region:steppes_region }`**. Exceptions: the three Ruthenian advances copy the unit gate verbatim (primary culture / KIE / UKR routes carry no steppe requirement, and the accepted-culture route allows ruthenia_region OR steppes_region). `requires` points at the vanilla advance unlocking the baseline unit the regiment `copy_from`s, and every pair is same-age (verified by parsing all vanilla advances). Mapping used: age2 light cav `unlock_cavalry_advance`, age3 `unlock_light_lancers_advance`, age4 `unlock_pistoleers_advance`, age5 `unlock_hussars_advance`, age6 `unlock_light_dragoons_advance`; age2 heavy cav `unlock_heavy_cavalry_advance`, age3 `unlock_lancers_advance`; age3 heavy inf `unlock_halberdiers_advance`, age4 `unlock_pikemen_advance`, age5 `unlock_musketeers_advance`, age6 `unlock_fusiliers_advance`; age4 light inf `unlock_arquebusiers_advance`, age5 `unlock_hunters_advance`. Loc appended to `abm_advances_steppe_l_english.yml` (names reuse the unit display names, as the Italy file does).
- **Circassian cavalry removed (2026-11-10):** `abm_circassian_cavalry_4/5/6` and their three advances + loc deleted on request; the steppe set is now **20 units / 20 advances** (crimean 2, mishar 2, astrakhani 2, gothic_culture 4, cossack_culture-REGISTERED horse 3, cossack_culture-ZAPOROZHIAN foot 2, ligurian 2, ruthenian 3). Do not restore the circassian line unless asked.
- **Cossacks split by ARM, not by institution (2026-11-08, final):** the MOUNTED line is the Crown's REGISTERED Cossacks and is an ESTATE line (`country_has_estate = estate_type:cossacks_estate` + `location_potential` any_pop `culture:cossack_culture`): `abm_cossack_cavalry_3` (age 3) -> `_4` (age 4) -> `_5` (age 5). The FOOT line is the ZAPOROZHIAN host and is a CULTURE line on `cossack_culture` (the only Cossack culture in vanilla; ZAZ uses `culture_definition = cossack_culture`), standard accepted-culture + steppe-capital gate: `abm_cossack_infantry_4` (age 4) -> `_5` (age 5). Stats unchanged from the original five units (horse 0.10 str dmg / -0.10 morale taken / -0.25 supply / +2 speed; foot -0.15 morale and -0.10 strength taken). **Interim experiment reverted:** the 4 extra units (`abm_registered_cossack_cavalry_5`, `abm_registered_cossack_infantry_4/5`, `abm_zaporozhian_cavalry_4`) plus their advances and loc were all removed the same day - do not re-add them. History notes kept for reference: register created 1572, abolished 1699; registered infantry fought from the tabor; registered light horse were a minority skirmish arm cheaper than hussars; age years are traditions 1337, renaissance 1342, discovery 1437, reformation 1537, absolutism 1637, revolutions 1737.
- **`abm_settle_the_steppe` cabinet action (2026-10-27):** `in_game\common\cabinet_actions\abm_settle_the_steppe.txt`, loc in `abm_settle_the_steppe_l_english.yml`, unlocked by `abm_settle_the_steppe_advance` (appended to `abm_steppes_ural.txt`, age_3_discovery, `requires = city_building_advance`, `unlock_cabinet_action = abm_settle_the_steppe`, gated on `capital ?= { region = region:steppes_region }`). **Country gate (2026-10-27):** `potential` also requires `OR = { monarchy, theocracy, republic }` and `is_country_religion_pagan = no` - the same government/faith test `abm_location_settled_and_loyal` applies per location, here once on the country. `is_country_religion_pagan` is a vanilla trigger (used in `government_reforms/common.txt`, `laws/00_theocracies.txt`, `on_action/_hardcoded.txt`) although it is absent from `trigger_localization/`, so scans report a false negative. The advance's `capital ?= { region = region:steppes_region }` gate was REMOVED - the restriction belongs on the province, which the action itself enforces, and a capital gate would deny it to a monarchy that wins steppe land after moving its seat. Modelled on vanilla `swe_slash_and_burn_new_farms`: province `select_trigger`, `location_modifier = { local_population_growth = 0.02  local_monthly_development = 0.01  local_migration_attraction = 0.25 }`, `is_finished` once the province fills up, `map_marker = { show_for_owner = yes }`. **Completion criteria (revised 2026-10-27 at the user's request):** the action ends when EVERY land location in the province has `development >= 20` AND `location_population_percentage >= 0.5` - i.e. 20 development and at least half of its population capacity filled. Both conditions live in one shared scripted trigger, `abm_steppe_province_needs_settling` (province scope), which is true while at least one land location still fails either test; the picker's `visible` requires it true and `is_finished` requires it false, so the two can never disagree. `province_average_population` was dropped entirely - it was an average, which would let one huge location mask an empty neighbour. **`location_population_percentage` is the pop-capacity trigger** (`LOCATION_POP_PERC_TRIGGER`: 'Has $CMP$ $NUM%$ of [population_capacity|e]'), a location-scope trigger taking a 0-1 fraction; vanilla uses it as `< 0.05` / `> 0.1` in `building_types/rural_buildings.txt` and `< 0.75` in `generic_mission_events.txt`. The progress readout now reports `GetAverageDevelopment` with `@development!` instead of average population (both `GetAverageDevelopment` and `GetAverageDevelopmentInfo` are real province accessors). **Gate is `abm_steppe_province_is_our_culture`** - a new scripted trigger in `abm_pontic_steppe.txt` (province scope) that fails if ANY owned land location has a dominant culture its owner does not treat as primary or accepted. Uses `owner ?= { has_primary_or_accepted_culture = prev.dominant_culture }` from inside `any_location_in_province`, which is the idiom `institution_triggers.txt` uses (`dominant_culture = owner.culture`). Two vanilla-proven province-picker tricks are used: `any_location_in_province = { region = region:steppes_region }` for the region test (`oma_zanzibar_integration` gates on `region:swahili_coast_region` the same way) and a `NOT = { any_location_in_province = { has_owner = yes is_land = yes NOT = { owner ?= scope:actor } } }` to require full ownership. **Cabinet action localisation needs 9 keys**, not the obvious 2: `<id>`, `<id>_desc`, `_action`, `_active`, `_action_progress`, `_action_progress_wordier`, `_action_progress_tooltip`, `_select_province`, `_no_provinces` (the last two feed the `select_trigger`). The `_action_progress_tooltip` convention is `#T <name>#!\nAverage [population|e] per [location|e] in [SCOPE.sProvince('target').GetName]: ...GetAveragePopulationWithTooltip\n\n#T Completed When#!\n[SCOPE.sCabinet('cabinet').GetActionEndConditions]`. `cabinet_already_performing_same_task_on_target` is engine-hardcoded (absent from `trigger_localization/` but used twice in the vanilla reference), so a 'is this trigger real' scan reports a false negative for it. **Pre-existing unrelated issue noticed:** the mod ships `in_game\common\cabinet_actions\reform_taxation_system.txt` as a plain (non-`REPLACE:`) duplicate of a vanilla file - same for `country_GLH.txt`'s `glh_legacy_of_jochi` - which is the same filename-collision class flagged back in the events audit.

- **Gazarian claim on Lusta (2026-10-27):** `main_menu\setup\1337\10_countries_steppes_region.txt`, GAZ block now carries `our_cores_conquered_by_others = { lusta }` alongside its `own_control_core` and `own_control_integrated`. **`our_cores_conquered_by_others` is the vanilla key for a core on land someone ELSE owns** (used 244 times in vanilla's `10_countries.txt`); plain `own_core`/`own_*` variants all imply ownership. FEO still owns Lusta and still keeps it in `own_control_core` - this only adds the Genoese claim, which gives GAZ a reconquest casus belli rather than the province. **Cores are NOT in `02_core.txt`** despite the name - that file is `institution_manager` + `religion_manager`. All core/ownership data lives in the regional `10_countries_*.txt` files. 
- **Gazaria keeps the Ligurian culture advances (2026-10-28):** the six vanilla Ligurian culture advances in `advances/culture_ligurian.txt` - `ligurian_coastal_seafarers`, `ligurian_merchant_diaspora`, `ligurian_spirit_of_navigation`, `ligurian_artisan_craft`, `ligurian_communal_councils`, `ligurian_enlightenment` - are all vanilla-gated `OR = { culture = culture:ligurian  culture = { merged_culture_group_contains_culture = culture:ligurian } }`, so adopting Gothic or Pontic culture would hide them permanently. **The mod already `REPLACE:`d all six in `in_game\common\advances\abm_italy.txt`, so the fix was a third branch in each `potential` OR:** `AND = { has_or_had_tag = GAZ  OR = { culture = culture:gothic_culture  culture = culture:pontic_greek_culture } }`. **Deliberately NOT a bare `has_or_had_tag = GAZ`** - the user was explicit that a Gazaria adopting ANY other culture must lose the Ligurian advances, so the exception is scoped to exactly the two identities `abm_gazaria_culture_choice` offers. The `has_or_had_tag` half is still needed so a Gothic-cultured Theodoro or a Pontic-cultured Trebizond cannot claim them. **`potential` on an advance is NOT retroactive** (advance readme: 'The advance will only appear if the specified triggers are fulfilled. NOTE: advances do not become retroactively visible..') - it decides whether the advance can still be RESEARCHED, while already-researched advances keep their effects. So the loss from a culture switch is the future of the tree, not the past. `grant_culture_bonus_advances = culture:X` would be the alternative - it grants that culture's bonus advances outright - but that hands the whole set over for free, which is why it was deliberately left out of `abm_gazaria_culture_choice`. Also confirmed: the user REMOVED the six `REPLACE:feo_*` advances (the ones shared between the FEO tag and `gothic_culture`) from `abm_steppes_ural.txt`, so no Gothic culture-locked advance set remains in the mod.

### 8. Open plains privilege for steppe nations (2026-10-31)
- **Files:** `abm_cossack_open_plains_privilege` in `in_game\common\estate_privileges\abm_cossacks_estate.txt`, `abm_tribal_open_plains_privilege` in `abm_tribes_estate.txt` (the tribes file was empty until now). Loc in `abm_estate_privileges_l_english.yml`. Replaced an earlier reform version of the same idea (`abm_gov_reforms_steppe_proximity.txt`, deleted).
- **Gate:** `original_capital ?= { region = region:steppes_region }`, `country_rank_level >= 3` (rank level 3 = kingdom, 4 = empire), and a settled religion (`religion ?= { group = ... }` listing christian, muslim, dharmic, buddhist, israelite_group, zoroastrian_group, manichaean_group, mandean_group, druze_group, yazidi_group). No government-type gate - the estate itself handles availability.
- **Effects:** `road_cost_on_distance_from_capital = -2` (country; base is 20 in `country_base_values`, so ~10%) + `location_modifier` with `potential_trigger = { custom_tooltip = { text = abm_tt_steppe_flatland_location topography = flatland } }` + `local_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation = 0.10`; on_activate gives a one-time `change_societal_value = { type = centralization_vs_decentralization value = 10 }`.
- **Pagan check:** use the vanilla trigger `is_country_religion_pagan = no` (country scope) - it is what `laws\00_theocracies.txt`, `government_reforms\common.txt:428` and `treaty_of_tordesillas.txt` use, and the mod already used it in `scripted_triggers\abm_pontic_steppe.txt:14` and `cabinet_actions\abm_settle_the_steppe.txt:37`. Do NOT hand-list the `folk_*_group` families. (There is no loc entry for the trigger, so it shows no tooltip text.)
- **Country-scope trap:** `road_cost_on_distance_from_capital` is `category = country` (00_modifier_types.txt:5295) and `land_cost_on_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation` likewise, so neither can live in a `location_modifier` - the `steppes_region` limit is carried by the privilege gate instead. There is no location-scope road proximity modifier at all (only `local_road_building_time`, `road_building_blocked`).
- **Mutually exclusive:** each privilege's `allow` has `NOT = { has_estate_privilege = estate_privilege:<the other one> }` (vanilla pattern: `peasants_free_peasantry`), so cossacks and tribes cannot both be taken.
- **Anti-farm:** the +10 centralisation is guarded by `has_variable = abm_open_plains_centralisation` (shared by both privileges), so revoke/re-grant - including revoking one privilege to take the other - cannot repeat it.
- **Loc gotcha:** there is no `[flatland]` game concept (vanilla writes `[ShowTopographyName('flatland')]`); mod lines say "flatland" in plain text.

### 9. Tauric "Black Soil Rights" town right (2026-11-03)
- **Files:** `in_game\common\town_rights\abm_tauric_town_rights.txt` (new folder for the mod), loc `main_menu\localization\english\abm_town_rights_l_english.yml`, revoke hook `in_game\common\on_action\abm_town_rights_on_actions.txt`.
- **Gate (final, 2026-11-03):** not tag- or advance-gated - open to **any** nation whose `original_capital ?= { region = region:steppes_region }` and whose government is monarchy/republic/theocracy (`government_type = government_type:X`, 489/363/269 vanilla uses). Same shape as `abm_formables_steppes.txt:64` and the open-plains privilege. The effect is +80% `local_max_rgo_size_modifier` / +25% `local_food_production_mult` on towns producing wheat, wine, millet ("sturdy grain", see the pastoral entry) or legumes. `abm_abtau_steppe_black_soil` is still ABTAU-only and no longer gates it.
- **The advance's hook:** `global_raw_material_output = 0.10` (country, percent, `00_modifier_types.txt:5047`) - EU5 has no literal "rgo efficiency" key; this is the RGO-output knob, and 0.1 is the standard vanilla magnitude (10 advance uses, e.g. `country_azt.txt:64` which pairs it with `requires = rgo_logistics_discovery`). Complements the town right: the right widens RGO size locally in four goods, the advance raises output nationally. Also carries `global_monthly_food_modifier = monthly_food_productivity_mild_bonus`. Rejected: `global_food_capacity_modifier` (0 vanilla advance uses - storage, not efficiency) and `expand_rgo_farming_cost_modifier` (farming-only, `color=bad`, a cost discount rather than efficiency).
- **`town_rights` schema** (own `readme.txt`, 7 keys): `color`, `allow` (**root = country, target = location**, so location checks go inside `scope:target`), `potential` (root = country), `location_modifier`, `country_modifier`, `is_specialization` (UI-only: the "Specialization" filter), `upgrades_to`. Vanilla country-specific rights also use the undocumented `kept_at_conquest = no`.
- **Ranks can only hold rights at town or above:** `location_ranks\00_default.txt` gives `megalopolis` 3 `local_possible_town_rights`, `city` 2, `town` 1, `rural_settlement` none. There is **no "≤ rank" comparison** in EU5 - `location_rank = location_rank:<id>` is equality-only, so "towns only" must be written as an explicit `OR` of the ranks to exclude (or a equality check on town).
- **Rank gate pattern:** wrap the rank check in `custom_tooltip = { text = <right>_rank_tt ... }` inside `scope:target`, as vanilla `studium_privileges` (00_traditions.txt:212) and `imperial_breadbasket_rights` (byzantine_greek_roman.txt) do. Loc uses `[ShowLocationRankName('town')]`, not a game concept.
- **Right can be revoked on rank change:** `revoke_town_rights_of_type = town_rights_type:<id>` is a location effect (`effect_localization\location_effects.txt:429`; used in `events\government\town_rights_events.txt:946`) and `on_location_changed_rank` (in `_hardcoded.txt:4841`, **root = location, old_rank, actor = country**, post-change) fires on both upgrade and downgrade. `allow` is only evaluated when granting, so without this hook a town that becomes a city keeps the right.
- **on_action keys merge across files** (vanilla defines `on_truce_broken` in both `diplomatic_reactions.txt` and the empty `_hardcoded.txt`), so a mod file can add to a hook without copying vanilla's file.
- **Food modifiers:** `local_food_production_mult` (+25% here) is a valid location-category modifier (`00_modifier_types.txt:5626`) but **no vanilla town right uses it** - vanilla rights use `local_food_capacity_modifier` / `local_food_preservation_efficiency_modifier` / `local_food_decay`. `local_max_rgo_size_modifier` is the standard RGO-size key (vanilla rights: 0.35-1.0).

### 6. Governor offices: unused governor posts -> reform slots / cabinet seats (2026-10-31)
- **Concept:** an unused local governor post is converted into government capacity by building a nobles building. Two building types, one reward each; each building **instance** = one converted post.
- **Revised 2026-11-02: no levels.** `max_levels = 1` on all three offices, `increase_per_level_cost` dropped, and `country_has_no_other_copy_of_building` removed from `allow` - the engine allows only one instance of a building type per location, so `max_levels = 1` *is* the per-location uniqueness, while copies in different locations are now allowed (one per free governor post). The reward had to move from levels to a per-count ladder: `abm_provincial_chancellery_1..4` / `abm_court_secretariat_1..4` / `abm_admiralty_board_1..4`, each keyed on `any_owned_location = { has_building = building_type:<id>  count >= N }` (N=1 needs no `count`). `has_building` = completed only, so construction does not hand out the slot early; `count >= N` inside an `any_` iterator is vanilla (`any_owned_location = { controller = scope:winner  count >= 25 }`, `peace_treaties\rtr_rein_in_rebellion.txt:103`). Ladder stops at 4 = the maximum number of posts (local cap 4, naval cap 4) - extend it if a new cap source appears.
- **Files:**
  - `in_game\common\building_types\abm_governor_offices.txt` - `abm_provincial_chancellery` (reform slot) and `abm_court_secretariat` (cabinet seat), `max_levels = 1`, nobles, `category = government_category`, `forbidden_for_estates = yes`.
  - `in_game\common\production_methods\abm_governor_offices.txt` - `abm_governor_offices_maintenance` (`no_upkeep = yes`, like vanilla `cardinal_maintenance`).
  - `in_game\common\auto_modifiers\abm_country.txt` - `abm_provincial_chancellery_1..4` / `abm_court_secretariat_1..4` / `abm_admiralty_board_1..4`, each `-1` governor post plus `government_reform_slots = 1` / `government_size = 1`. **This is where the reward lives** (see below).
  - `in_game\common\scripted_triggers\abm_governor_triggers.txt` - `abm_has_unused_local_governor` (mirrors `local_governor.allow`, incl. the lieutenancy / itinerant court seat / king's manor deductions and the `minimum_num_local_governors` floor) and `abm_has_unused_naval_governor` (just `built < modifier:num_naval_governors`, no deductions).
  - `in_game\common\building_types\abm_naval_governors.txt` - `REPLACE:naval_governor` with the exclave rule removed (`NOT = { is_connected_to_through_realm = owner.capital }` dropped from `location_potential`, the `remove_if` that deleted the building once connected dropped entirely). Ports next to the capital are now valid; the cap check and `local_proximity_source = 80` are untouched.
  - `abm_admiralty_board` in `abm_governor_offices.txt` + `abm_admiralty_board_1..4` in `abm_country.txt` - the naval route: one unused naval governor post -> one cabinet seat per board, placed in a port city (not the capital, not overseas).
  - Loc: `abm_buildings_l_english.yml` (names, descs, `<id>_abolished_tt`) and `abm_modifiers_l_english.yml` (`AUTO_MODIFIER_NAME_/DESC_` per block).
