- **Placement:** capital, or a cored town/city in the capital's **area**: `OR = { is_capital = yes AND = { is_city = yes is_core_of = owner area = owner.capital.area } }` in both `location_potential` and `allow`. Verified triggers: `is_city` (town_or_city), `is_core_of = owner` (culture_buildings.txt:24), `area = owner.capital.area` (flavor_NOR.txt:630). One copy per country via `country_has_no_other_copy_of_building = { type = X }` (royal_court); upgrades still allowed.
- **Why an auto-modifier and not `capital_country_modifier`:** the latter only applies "if built in the capital" (building_types/readme.txt), but the building may stand anywhere in the capital area - and it is scaled by goods access, which would make integer modifiers fractional. The auto modifier is country-wide and reads the building's level.
- **Level counting uses `any_owned_location = { location_building_level = { building_type = ... value >= N } }`, NOT `total_building_levels_including_construction`.** The including-construction version would hand out the slot as soon as construction STARTS; cancelling construction would then leave the enacted reform while the governor post returned.
- **Demolition:** allowed, but `on_destroyed` charges `add_gold = { value = monthly_income_total multiply = -3 }`, `add_stability = -10`, `add_political_influence = political_influence_severe_penalty` (-10, default_values.txt:73). Rationale: the reward is derived from the building level, so deleting it returns the governor post; enacted reforms are permanent, so the penalty is the deterrent. `remove_if` only fires when the location stops being a city/town or loses its core (never on capital moves), and warns that it may also trigger the penalty.
- **No `commoners_estate`:** the estates are nobles/clergy/burghers/peasants/tribes/cossacks/dhimmi + crown.
- **In-game test 2026-11-02 (01:59 run):** the building vanished right after completion because the test gate was `average_control >= 0.10` while `remove_if` kills at `< 0.5` - a country averaging 10-50% control builds it and loses it on the next evaluation. Not a code bug. Confirmed live: `average_control` is a working country trigger, `on_built` runs (the area effect appeared), `add_location_modifier` on the pushed static modifier works. Found + fixed by that run: `owner = { this = scope:actor }` inside `any_location_in_area` spammed **14104** `Event target link 'owner' returned an invalid object` errors at `abm_provincial_administration.txt:61`, one per unowned location per evaluation -> **`exists = owner` does NOT guard a null owner** - on unowned/colonisable locations the link resolves to a null object, `exists` passes, and the comparison then errors (`Invalid left side during comparison 'owner'`, 638 per run). The working guard is the safe-compare operator: `owner ?= scope:actor`, exactly as vanilla does in `assume_fort_command.txt:61` (`any_location_in_area = { owner ?= scope:actor }`) - `?=` treats a null/unset side as **no match, no error**, and the AND block then short-circuits so later `owner = { ... }` children never run. `remove_if`'s capture clause is now `location = { exists = owner  exists = controller  NOT = { controller = owner } }` - without `exists = controller`, an unset controller link makes `controller = owner` fail and `NOT` of that destroys the building in a province nobody took (no vanilla building uses `controller` in remove_if, so this is untested engine behaviour either way). `on_destroyed`'s old if/else (`does another administration survive in this area`) is unreliable because the dying building may still register - replaced with an unconditional strip, plus `in_game\common\on_action\abm_provincial_administration_on_actions.txt` hooked into `yearly_country_pulse` that strips where our administration is gone and re-applies around every one we hold. That also fixes locations gained/lost inside an area. `yearly_country_pulse` is safe to define again: on_actions merge across files (mod already redefines `monthly_country_pulse` in `abm_mp_limits_on_actions.txt`).
- **Unverified in game:** `location_building_level` availability inside a country auto-modifier's `any_owned_location`, whether `on_destroyed` also fires on `remove_if`, and tooltip rendering.

### 8c. Building icons + per-building upkeep methods (2026-11-02)
- **Icon lookup is by building key, no sprite entry:** `loading_screen\common\defines\graphic\00_graphics.txt` has `BUILDINGS_ICON_PATH = "gfx/interface/icons/buildings"`, so a building type `foo` needs `main_menu/gfx/interface/icons/buildings/foo.dds`. No entry in any `.gfx`/sprite file. Missing file falls back to `_default.dds`.
- **DDS spec (measured from vanilla, 2026-11-02):** 311 of 502 building icons are **128x128, DXT5, 8 mipmaps, 22000 bytes**; 191 older ones are 125x125 / 7 mips. Match a 128 one. ImageMagick recipe: `magick src.png -define dds:compression=dxt5 -define dds:mipmaps=8 out.dds` - note **`dds:mipmaps=8`, NOT `=all`** (`all` silently writes 1 mip and 16512 bytes).
- **Pure hue rotation (`-modulate b,s,h`) barely changes these icons** - the art is mostly white/cream, so hues don't shift. What works is a colour wash: `-modulate 100,90,100 -fill <hex> -colorize <30-42>` (sepia / deep green / amber / navy / burgundy), clearly distinguishable at 64px.
- **Family marker:** a 1px gold rim traced from the alpha silhouette - `-alpha extract -morphology EdgeOut Diamond:1`, turned into an overlay via `gold.png mask.png -alpha off -compose CopyOpacity -composite`, then composited over. ~643 px of rim on a 128 icon, subtle by design.
- **Icons shipped:** `abm_provincial_administration`, `abm_provincial_chancellery`, `abm_court_secretariat`, `abm_admiralty_board`, `abm_proconsulate` - recoloured from `colonial_administration`, `chancery`, `royal_court`, `admiralty`, `local_governor`.
- **Production methods split one per building** (`production_methods\abm_governor_offices.txt`): `abm_provincial_chancellery_maintenance`, `abm_court_secretariat_maintenance`, `abm_admiralty_board_maintenance`, `abm_proconsulate_maintenance`, each `icon_type = building_type` + `icon = <its own key>`. The old shared `abm_governor_offices_maintenance` is kept (unused) only so pre-split saves resolve; its icon now points at the chancellery. Provincial Administration uses an inline PM - its `icon` moved from `naval_governor` to its own key.
- **PM localisation: the key IS the loc key** (vanilla: `local_governor_maintenance: "$local_governor$ Maintenance"`). Ours had no entries at all, so the upkeep line rendered as a raw key; all four added to `abm_buildings_l_english.yml` using `$abm_<building>$`.
- **Remaining known issue:** `abm_capital_buildings.txt` / `abm_starting_manpower_buildings.txt` PMs still have no loc keys (pre-existing, untouched here).

### 8b. Proconsulate: +25% proximity source on a governor seat (2026-11-02)
- **Concept:** annex to a `local_governor` that consumes a **naval** governor post and amplifies the proximity source the location already has.
- **Building:** `abm_governor_offices.txt` (appended, shares `abm_governor_offices_maintenance`). `max_levels = 1`, nobles, `large_noble_employment`, `town = no` / `city = yes` (matches `local_governor`'s own ranks), `build_time = large_capital_build_time`.
- **Gates:** `location_potential` + `allow` = `has_building = building_type:local_governor` (verified location-scope trigger), `owner ?= scope:actor`, `scope:actor = { abm_has_unused_naval_governor = yes }`. `remove_if = { location = { NOT = { has_building = building_type:local_governor } } }` - it dies with the governor it is an annex to.
- **Effect:** `raw_modifier = { local_proximity_source_modifier = 0.25 }` - percent, `category = location` (`00_modifier_types.txt:5496`). `raw_modifier` and not `modifier` so goods access cannot shrink the quarter; with the governor's `local_proximity_source = 80` that location goes to 100.
- **Post accounting (final shape, 2026-11-02): vanilla's own idiom, centralised.** A building that parks on a governor post WITHOUT touching the modifier is *subtracted* from the cap in an inline `{ value = ... subtract = ... }` block - exactly how vanilla treats `lieutenancy` / `itinerant_court_seat` / `kings_manor` in `local_governor.allow` and `lieutenancy.allow`. Ars Belli keeps that arithmetic in **one** place per post type - `abm_has_unused_local_governor` and `abm_has_unused_naval_governor` in `scripted_triggers\abm_governor_triggers.txt` - and every enforcer calls the trigger: vanilla's two local enforcers via `REPLACE:local_governor` (`abm_local_governors.txt`) and `REPLACE:lieutenancy` (`abm_lieutenancy.txt`), the naval one via `REPLACE:naval_governor` (`abm_naval_governors.txt`), plus all six Ars Belli consumer buildings. Local list: `itinerant_court_seat`, `kings_manor`, `lieutenancies_in_governor_pool`, `abm_provincial_chancellery`, `abm_court_secretariat`. (Game 1.4.8, re-synced 2026-11-12: the DLC branch is gone; lieutenancies take a governor post only above `modifier:num_lieutenancy`, and `lieutenancy.allow` is `OR = { lieutenancies < num_lieutenancy  abm_has_unused_local_governor = yes }`.) Naval list: `abm_admiralty_board`, `abm_provincial_administration`, `abm_proconsulate`. A future consumer = one `subtract` line, nowhere else. `total_building_levels_including_construction` reserves the post the moment construction starts. Auto modifiers no longer carry `num_local_governors` / `num_naval_governors` - they are pure reward ladders (`government_reform_slots`, `government_size`); reducing the modifier too would count the post twice. Costs: the three REPLACE files are full vanilla copies (re-diff after every patch; expected deltas are exactly the allow lines), and the governor tooltips show the raw cap because the modifier is untouched.
- Loc: `abm_proconsulate` / `abm_proconsulate_desc` in `abm_buildings_l_english.yml`.

### 9. Provincial Administration: area-wide effects from a building (2026-11-02)
- **Files:** `in_game\common\building_types\abm_provincial_administration.txt` (building), `main_menu\common\static_modifiers\abm_provincial_administration.txt` (`abm_provincial_administration_area`), (no auto-modifier - the naval post is accounted for arithmetically, see below), `abm_area_has_no_other_copy_of_building` in `scripted_triggers\abm_governor_triggers.txt`, loc in `abm_buildings_l_english.yml` + `abm_modifiers_l_english.yml`. Staffed by **clergy**: `pop_type = clergy` + `employment_size = large_religious_building_employment` (0.1), the same pairing vanilla `sacred_council` uses; `large_religious_building_employment` is the value literally named "large" for clergy (bigger options: `cathedral_building_employment` 0.3, `order_religious_building_employment` 0.4, `huge_religious_building_employment` 0.5).
- **Gate:** one per **area per country** (`abm_area_has_no_other_copy_of_building`, which filters `owner ?= root.owner` inside `any_location_in_area` - without it a neighbour's administration anywhere in the area blocked ours; `allow` also has `owner ?= scope:actor`, vanilla naval_governor's "own land only" rule, so `root.owner` is the building country), `scope:actor = { average_control >= 0.7  abm_has_unused_naval_governor = yes }`, plus `area = { NOT = { any_location_in_area = { owner = { this = scope:actor }  NOT = { integration_level = core  dominant_religion ?= owner.religion  owner = { has_primary_or_accepted_culture = prev.dominant_culture } }` (`?=` and not `=`, because a location can have no dominant religion and comparing an unset scope is an invalid-left-side script error) } } }` - every one of our locations in the area must be cored, of our religion, and with a primary/accepted dominant culture. Single level (`max_levels = 1`). `location_potential` deliberately omits `has_proximity_source_building = no` (it was copied from naval_governor in earlier drafts): the building neither creates nor amplifies a proximity source, so a location that already holds a `local_governor` is a valid seat, and either build order works - `local_governor` only forbids *other* proximity-source buildings, which this is not.
- **Removal:** `remove_if = { OR = { owner ?= { average_control < 0.5 }  location = { exists = owner  NOT = { controller = owner } } } }` - the administration is abandoned when the realm's grip collapses, and destroyed outright when its seat is captured (`controller` is a location-scope comparison trigger; vanilla: `disasters\horde_civil_war.txt:33`, `country_interactions\transfer_occupation.txt:63`). (The port/location restrictions in `allow` are commented out and remain only in `location_potential`.)
- **Why effects and not a modifier block:** buildings can only apply location modifiers to their OWN location (`modifier`, `raw_modifier`, `capital_modifier`) or country modifiers (`capital_country_modifier`, `foreign_country_modifier`). There is **no area or province modifier block in building_types**, and the game contains **zero area-category modifiers** and exactly one province-category modifier (`province_integration_speed`) - the four wanted effects exist only as `category = location`. So the area is covered in `on_built` by `area = { every_location_in_area = { ... add_location_modifier ... } }` (vanilla shape: `international_organization_effects.txt:1802`).
- **No stacking:** the static modifier is always applied with `mode = replace` (not `mode = add`), so the same id can only exist once per location; `on_destroyed` re-applies instead of removing when another governorate survives in the area. `remove_location_modifier` takes the **bare modifier name** (`remove_location_modifier = isolating_cities`, vanilla `generic_actions\black_death.txt:227`), not a `modifier = { }` block - the block form parses but removes nothing.
- **Naval post accounting (checked 2026-11-02):** an auto modifier is applied **once** while its `potential_trigger` is true, so a single `num_naval_governors = -1` block charged only 1 post no matter how many administrations existed. The post is therefore subtracted arithmetically, the way vanilla `local_governor.allow` subtracts `lieutenancy` / `kings_manor`: `abm_has_unused_naval_governor` and the `naval_governor` REPLACE both use `total_building_levels_including_construction:naval_governor < { value = modifier:num_naval_governors  subtract = total_building_levels_including_construction:abm_provincial_administration }`. `total_building_levels_including_construction:<type>` is a country-scope **value** usable inside a `{ value = ... }` block (vanilla `capital_buildings.txt:959`), so it counts queued buildings too. Known gap: `abm_area_has_no_other_copy_of_building` only sees `has_building`, i.e. completed buildings, so two administrations can be queued in the same area (bounded by the post cap; `mode = replace` prevents stacking).
- **No `modifier` block on the building at all** (proximity source deliberately scrapped 2026-11-02): the seat is inside the area, so the static modifier pushed by `on_built` already covers it, and a second copy would double its own effect on the seat only. Because the building provides no `local_proximity_source`, `location_potential` needs no `has_proximity_source_building = no` (7 of 9 vanilla source buildings carry it, incl. both governors), and a `local_governor` and the administration can sit in the same location in either build order. `local_proximity_source` is what a vanilla governor post is actually worth (80), so this building consumes a naval post for no source - intentional, its value is the area effect. `local_proximity_source_modifier` (percent, `category = location`) only amplifies an existing source and is therefore also unused here.
- **Upkeep:** `unique_production_methods` with `naval_governor_maintenance` copied verbatim from vanilla `naval_governor` - it is **building-local** (defined inline in `capital_buildings.txt`), there is no global production method of that name. `construction_demand = local_governor_construction`, same as vanilla.
- **Unverified in game:** `average_control` is declared in `trigger_localization\country_triggers.txt:2207` but used nowhere in vanilla script (fallback: `"area_average_control(scope:actor)" >= 0.7`, proven in `assimilate_area.txt:91`); whether `on_built` fires after the level registers; whether `on_destroyed` fires before or after the building is removed from the location.

### 7. Three-level capital manpower buildings (2026-10-31)
- **What changed:** the capital / fixed-level manpower buildings go from `max_levels = 1` to `max_levels = 3` with `increase_per_level_cost = 0.5` (vanilla `construction_center` pattern). Per-level `local_manpower` stays vanilla, so a full building gives 0.045 (`sergeantry`, `city_guard`, `temple_militia`), 0.03 (`warrior_temple`) or 0.0075 (`jurchen_barracks`). Paid for in gold, build time, soldier pops and maintenance, so nothing changes at the 1337 start.
- **Files:** `in_game\common\building_types\abm_starting_manpower_buildings.txt` - five full `REPLACE:` copies of the vanilla entries in `manpower_buildings.txt`; re-sync if vanilla edits them. `order_headquarters` is 3-level as well, but its `REPLACE:` lives in `abm_capital_buildings.txt` (the mod already overrides it there with manpower 0.05) - **never define the same id twice**, so the level change was applied in that file and its `local_defensive = 0.1` moved to `raw_modifier` so it does not triple either.
- **Which building for which government:** `sergeantry` = monarchy, `city_guard` = republic, `temple_militia` = theocracy (and NOT `military_order_reform`), `warrior_temple` = nahuatl religion, `jurchen_barracks` = Jurchen dominant culture, `thema_headquarters` = `modifier:allow_thema_headquarters` (Byzantium), `order_headquarters` = military orders at `country_rank_level >= 2`.
- **Vanilla asymmetry worth knowing:** `temple_militia` is placed for **nobody** at the 1337 start (0 occurrences, vs 151 sergeantries), so theocracies start without a capital manpower building. Of the 23 theocracy tags in the mod's start setup, 6 have `order_headquarters`, 3 have a `sergeantry`/`city_guard` they cannot upgrade (CSI, CTW, LNG - those are monarchy/republic-gated), and the rest have nothing.
- **Why REPLACE and not INJECT:** INJECT merges and *appends* blocks, and appended modifier values **add** (`country_ranks\abm_country_ranks.txt` relies on it). Building modifiers are "multiplied by building level and goods access", so `local_crown_estate_power = 0.25` (sergeantry) / `local_clergy_estate_power` (warrior_temple 0.1, temple_militia 0.25) would have tripled, and INJECT cannot remove a key. A `REPLACE:` copy moves them to `raw_modifier` ("applied to the location, not scaled"), keeping vanilla totals.
- **INJECT status:** real (`INJECT:` / `INJECT_OR_CREATE:`), used by vanilla on laws/reforms/genes/portrait_modifiers and by the mod on `country_ranks`, `location_ranks`, `auto_modifiers`. No vanilla `INJECT:` precedent in `building_types/` - unverified for that database.
- **Watch in game:** `remove_if = { is_no_longer_capital = yes }` now destroys a 3-level building on a capital move; level 3 needs 3x `manpower_employment` soldier pops in the capital, so a small capital under-staffs it.

### 5. Rough-terrain homeland reform (2026-10-31)
- **What changed:** every advance that granted a country-wide `mountains_/plateau_/hills_/wetlands_proximity_impact` bonus now sets `unlock_government_reform = abm_homeland_proximity_reform` instead. ONE standard reform for all of them (only `abm_che_wetland_adaptation`'s `hills_proximity_impact = 0.25` penalty is left in place as its drawback).
- **Why a reform / why not the terrain modifiers:** all 22 `*_proximity_impact` are `game_data = { category = country }` in `main_menu/common/modifier_type_definitions/00_modifier_types.txt`, so they cannot be limited to a region. **They are also NOT valid inside a `location_modifier`** (2026-10-31: scanned every depth-1 entry of every top-level `location_modifier` block in vanilla - only `category=location` and `category=all` keys, never a country one; an unknown key there is silently dropped, so the reform would do nothing). The location-scope equivalent is `local_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation` ("Local Proximity Efficiency"): +100% cancels the mountains penalty (-0.5), and clamps for hills (-0.25) / plateau (-0.125); flatland has no penalty so it gains nothing - i.e. it behaves like `*_proximity_impact = -1.0` for rough terrain only. A `country_modifier = { pure_tooltip_entry = abm_homeland_proximity_reform_tt }` spells the effect out in the reform tooltip (vanilla `itinerant_court_policy`).
- **Files:** `in_game\common\government_reforms\abm_gov_reforms_rough_terrain.txt` (single reform; `potential = { OR = { has_advance = <the 15 advances> } }` like vanilla `voivode_reform`); `in_game\common\advances\abm_rough_terrain_advances.txt` holds `REPLACE:` blocks for the 10 vanilla advances (verbatim vanilla minus the proximity keys - re-check if vanilla edits those entries); the 5 mod-owned advances were edited in place (`abm_caucasus`, `abm_anatolia`, `country_ira`, `abm_china_east`, `abm_china_west`).
- **Region is resolved at runtime, not per country (2026-10-31, second pass):** the reform's `location_modifier.potential_trigger` saves `this` (the location) as `scope:abm_homeland_location`, then `trigger_if` = "owner has a location with `is_historical_capital = yes` AND in `scope:abm_homeland_location.region`", `trigger_else` = "owner's `original_capital` is in that region". So the bonus follows the historical capital's region and falls back to the original capital's region. **`historical_capital` is NOT a data link** (only the location trigger `is_historical_capital`, plus `counts_as_capital`) - that is why the historical capital is found by iterating `any_owned_location`.
- **Verified shapes:** `save_temporary_scope_as` inside a trigger (`scripted_triggers\00_event_illustration_triggers.txt`), `region = scope:x.region` (`country_interactions\invite_prince.txt`), `is_historical_capital` at location scope (`building_types\capital_buildings.txt` `location_potential`), `location_modifier` + `potential_trigger` + `custom_tooltip` (`laws\10_italian_wars_laws.txt`).
- **Loc:** `abm_homeland_proximity_reform` + `_desc` and `abm_tt_homeland_historical_capital_region` / `abm_tt_homeland_original_capital_region` in `main_menu\localization\english\abm_gov_reforms_l_english.yml`.
- **Cost / unverified:** the per-location `potential_trigger` scans the country's owned locations (O(n) per location) to find the historical capital - fine for normal countries, worth watching for giant ones. The reform is *unlocked*, not auto-enacted (player spends a reform slot + 2 years), and none of this is confirmed in game yet.
- **Alpine split (2026-11-16):** Savoy, the Swiss and Provence get a SECOND reform instead, `abm_alpine_proximity_reform` (same file, same effect value `local_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation = 1.0`), whose `potential_trigger` is a flat `OR = { area = area:... }` over the Alpine arc - no owner/capital scope at all, so it works for every location in the range wherever the capital sits. Areas (vanilla names from `main_menu\localization\english\area_l_english.yml`): `provence_area`, `savoy_area` ("Savoy and Dauphine"), `western_switzerland_area` (Romandy), `central_switzerland_area` (Switzerland), `eastern_switzerland_area` (Raetia), `tirol_area` ("Tyrol and Trentino"), `lombardy_area`, `veneto_area` ("Veneto and Friuli"), `carinthia_area`, `styria_area`. **Unlockers:** `swiss_alpine_passes` + `sav_alpine_identity` (REPLACE blocks in `abm_rough_terrain_advances.txt`) now unlock the alpine reform and were **removed from the homeland reform's `potential`** so the two cannot both be taken; Provence has no vanilla mountain advance, so `abm_tarascon_castle` (`abm_france.txt`, age 3, PRO) carries the unlock. **Austria (HAB/TIR/STY) deliberately stays on the homeland reform** - Tyrol/Carinthia/Styria are listed as *effect* areas for the alpine holders, not as extra unlockers. Loc: `abm_alpine_proximity_reform`/`_desc`/`_tt` + `abm_tt_alpine_areas` in `abm_government_l_english.yml`.

## Project Structure
The repository mirrors the EU5 file structure:
- `in_game/`: Contains gameplay logic (advances, effects, triggers, setup).
- `main_menu/`: Contains game rules, localization, and static modifiers.
- `loading_screen/`: Contains defines.
- `deploy.ps1`, `release.ps1`, `watch.ps1`: Automation scripts for mod development.
- `release.ps1` excludes `.exe` files from the release archive.
- `merge_country_setup.ps1`: deploy.ps1 + release.ps1 run it on the COPY (deploy dest / release staging), never repo (script refuses repo unless `-CheckOnly`). Merges `main_menu/setup/<folder>/10_countries_*.txt` into one `10_countries.txt`, deletes regional files. Why: game spends ~2 s per setup file on new-game generation, size no matter → 62 files ≈ 2 min. Repo keeps split files (split via `.tools/scripts/split_10_countries.py`) so no 100k-line merge conflicts. Order = ordinal filename = old load order (first claim on a location wins). Throws on dupe tag, header mismatch, odd trailer, unbalanced braces. Release runs `-CheckOnly` before version bump. Works for `setup/start` (1.3) and `setup/1337` (1.4). No BOM on output.

## Implementation Details for Agents
- **Scripted Effects:** Check `in_game\common\scripted_effects\ars_belli_scripted_effects.txt` for the core logic of the ranking system.
- **Monthly Pulse:** The `mp_limits_monthly_pulse` on-action triggers the recalculation of scores and ranks.
- **Localization:** Multiplayer-specific rules and settings are localized in `main_menu\localization\english\ars_belli_rules_l_english.yml`.
- **REPLACE: prefix:** EU5 supports `REPLACE:key = { ... }` in mod files to override a single vanilla definition without copying the whole vanilla file. Use a separately-named mod file (convention: `ars_belli_<topic>.txt`); avoid same-name file replacement so future vanilla additions in that file still load. Works for resolutions, generic_actions, scripted_triggers, static_modifiers, auto_modifiers, prices, diplomatic_costs, advances. Only fall back to whole-file replacement (and `replaced_files.txt`) for non-keyed files like GUI.
- **`in_game/setup/countries/*.txt` are full vanilla copies and the mod deletes ~527 countries on purpose by commenting their blocks out (`#TAG = {` ... `#}`). Keep them removed; never re-add vanilla's definitions.** A deleted tag is fatal only when a file the game loads still references it as a country block: vanilla's `main_menu/setup/1337/10_countries.txt` (the 1.4 bookmark) does, and the reader aborts the whole file at the first undefined tag (1.4: `SMI`, dropped from `_scandinavia.txt` line 100) -> no country start-state at all (every country "has invalid government type", every market is ownerless, pops get invalid culture/religion), engine asserts `pPop->GetCulture().Lookup().IsValid()` and the frontend dies in `GenerateFromBookmarkInterfaceIdler`. The mod's own `main_menu/setup/start/10_countries*.txt` never reference a removed tag, so the cure is to keep the mod's start-state as the loaded one - not to restore tags.
- **1.4 renamed the bookmark start folder: `main_menu/setup/start/` -> `main_menu/setup/1337/`.** The mod's folder has been ported to `1337/` (renamed, same 73 files). If the mod's start-state is not the loaded one, vanilla's `10_countries.txt` aborts on the removed countries -> crash above, and every bookmark override (pops, markets, diplomacy, IOs, wars) silently stops applying. Proof of the 1.3 mismatch in `debug.log`: errors reported against vanilla's file contents, e.g. `Undefined country tag: OBD`, which exists uncommented only in vanilla's `12_diplomacy.txt`. Re-check this path on every game update.
- **1.4 content still missing from the ported start-state (backlog - NOT yet ported, all provably 1.4 additions with no commented trace in the mod's blocks):** (a) DLC `d009_across_the_pillars` reforms/privileges/laws/parliament for `ANR CAT GRA LON MLL MOR NAV`; (b) 1.4 generated-ruler name pools inside `government` for `BLL BYZ CAR CLI CRH CRO MTR PAT SMO TES` (BYZ 41 lines, CRO 21, CRH 14, CAR 10, PAT 7, BLL 4, MTR/TES 2, CLI/SMO 1); (c) `ai_advance_preference_tags` for `FRA` (`exploration = 5`) and `TUR` (`warfare = 1 land = 1`) - the only balance-relevant item; (d) `BYZ` `past_court_dialects`/`past_liturgical_dialects = { roman_dialect }`, `SBL` `rebellion_demand = the_goals_of_balliol`, `ALB` `culture = neapolitan`. Deliberate mod removals left as-is: 7 vanilla markets (`mecca kyoto chengdu edinburgh kashgar urgench al_hajr`), the Ilkhanate/Lord-of-Ireland IOs, `kola_area`/`connaught_area` development (commented "to reset to 0"), `type = pop`/`add_pops_from_locations` on 39 tribes. Already synced to 1.4: 5 Campania roads, towns `avila`/`sale`, `pamplona` development, `SECOND` -> `second` war key (all marked `#1.4`).
- **1.4 added 19 country tags (checked 2026-10-13); none are in the mod's setup.** Method: the mod always marks a deleted country by commenting it out, so a tag with no live *and* no commented trace in the mod's 1.3-era copy did not exist in 1.3. (a) 6 new tags = 1.4's split of the mod-deleted `TRG`: `AHG AJJ IWL KAH KAY KGS` (Tuareg, `type = pop`, holding *exactly* TRG's 31 locations). Mod's world loses nothing: those lands are owned by its own tags (`AIR` 15, `ABBBN` 7, `TMK` 4, `FZA` 2, `GHA`/`DJE`/`SON` 1) - note `AIR` is a mod-only tag, 1.4 has neither `TRG` nor `AIR`. (b) 13 tags in files the mod never copied (`iberia.txt`, `maghreb.txt`, `_default.txt`): Iberian latent crowns `ALV ATU CRD JAE MUC SEV TOL` (core-only, no land), `CNR` Guanche (owns the 6 Canary locations - unowned in the mod's world), `BIJ` Bijaya (27 locations, owned by others in the mod's world), `ALM` Almohads (latent), tool placeholders `DUMMY PIR MER`. (c) 12 pre-existing latent tags that 1.4 *promoted* into the start-state: `BJA` (16 Bjarmian locations), `MAD` Maden, `MSL` Misool, `WGE` Waigeo, `MLK` Moloka'i (those 20 locations are unowned in the mod's world), `ORK` (4 locations, owned), `HAW KAU LHN MUI OAH` (no land), `JAP` (`type = boolean`; the mod has Japan content for it). Net: 1.4 leaves 26 locations unowned in the mod's world (BJA 16, CNR 6 Canaries, MAD/MSL/WGE/MLK 1 each). Resolution: the 1.4 owners the mod actually supports were restored - `BJA` (all 16 locations, own `#1.4` block in `10_countries_russian_region.txt`: `own_control_core` + `starting_technology_level = 3` + vanilla's `discovered_*`, i.e. the same conversion the mod applies to its other 39 tribes instead of vanilla's `type = pop`/`add_pops_from_locations`) and `MLK` (`moloka_i`, vanilla's block in `10_countries_polynesia_region.txt`); `MAD` -> `KAM` and `MSL`/`WGE` -> `JLO` (same province/area, lines marked `#1.4: was unowned`). Left unowned on purpose (user confirmed): the 6 Canary locations (nothing owns their island group; the mod keeps Hawaii and the Atlantic islands empty too). Same 1.4 pass: `NOR`'s commented `#shetland orkney` line was uncommented, so Norway holds Shetland and Orkney again. Still 54 DB-live tags have no start-state block (13 of them 1.4-new) - the leftover `government_type` error class after the port (was 1294 countries).
- **`03_markets.txt` / `06_pops.txt` vs 1.4 (checked 2026-10-14).** Method: both files are derived from vanilla 1.2.1 (git `50acc71 "1.2.1 files"` is the pristine snapshot), so parsing that snapshot + HEAD + vanilla 1.4 splits every difference into *mod-edited* / *vanilla revised after 1.2.1* / *both*. Gotcha found while doing it: 11 location names contain uppercase (`beja_TUN jama_TUN massa_MOR tata_MOR asir_ALG al_khadra_ALG constantine_ALG matanda_aChiwawa ratnapura_LKA targoviste_BUL trgoviste_SER`), so a lowercase-only name regex silently attributes their pops to the preceding location - always match `[A-Za-z]`. **Markets are complete:** vanilla added only `tarnovo` + `sarai_al_jadid` after 1.2.1 and the mod has both; the 7 markets the mod lacks (`edinburgh mecca al_hajr urgench kyoto chengdu kashgar`) are 1.2.1 vanilla markets the mod deliberately moved in `81ad7b7` (glasgow/jeddah/unaizah/khiva/osaka/baxian/yarkand, its own comments say so); the file structure is unchanged in 1.4. **Pops:** the mod has a block for every one of 1.4's 28,570 locations plus 3 empty 1.3-era leftovers (`lake_hovz_soltan lake_namak zagros_mountains8`, valid impassable `qom_province` locations, harmless); schema unchanged (`define_pop = { type size culture religion }` only); every culture (2,083) and religion (285) used is defined in the effective DB, so pops are not an invalid-culture/religion source. Of all locations: 21,557 already match 1.4, 6,256 are the mod's own rebalance, **760 were locations the mod never touched (byte-identical to 1.2.1 vanilla) that vanilla had since revised, and 24 where the mod edited and vanilla revised**. All 760 were synced to 1.4's composition in one pass (safe: no mod intent there, totals unchanged 14,506 -> 14,438): Iberia 419 (`sephardi` nobles+clergy added to Sevilla/Toledo/Cordoba/Granada/Valencia/Lisbon...), Maghreb+Sahara 242 (`mastaarabi` burghers/peasants + `andalusi` peasants in Marrakesh/Fez/Tlemcen/Sijilmasa/Oujda, Zenati split into Mozabite/Ibadi + Messufa, Western Sahara `hassaniya` -> `godala_culture`/`lamtuna_culture`, `ajim` eastern_amazigh -> zenati), Wales 24 (`english` burghers in the Welsh towns), German/Czech/Alpine 9 (danube_bavarian -> southern_bavarian; new danube_bavarian/saxon/german_silesian burghers in Olomouc/Plzen/Kutna Hora/Brno/Opole), Khorasan 3 (`mongolian_culture` soldiers garrisons in Balkh/Akhsisek/Andkhoy), Trieste/Gorizia (venetian -> friulian), Benghazi/Derna, Seguedine, Lubeck (`hanseatic` burghers), Montpellier, Mesudiye (clergy -> turkoman), Deh Kundi, Romagna/Marche 18 (`emilian`/`median` -> `romagnol`). For the 24 conflicts 1.4's composition was adopted and the mod's own added `peasants` rows were kept on top, which silently drops the mod's *size* tweaks where 1.4 has the same row. Three such locations (the only ones) were restored by hand on the user's call: Theodoro keeps its Gothic majority (peasants gothic 11.588 instead of vanilla's 2.897, and the mod's gothic nobles 0.076 re-added alongside vanilla's pontic nobles 0.019) and Maimana keeps its bigger Oirat tribesmen (2.560/0.639 vs vanilla 1.896/0.473); Kengtung needed nothing - its tweak was an *added* `tai_lu` peasants row and 1.4 has the same row with a larger value (3.000), so 1.4's number stands. Dating: the Iberia/Maghreb/Jewish-Muslim rework is **1.4** (it is the `d009_across_the_pillars` DLC pass - 1.3's `region_iberia.txt` has 0 d009 references, 1.4's has them), while `romagnol` is **>= 1.3** (the 1.3-era `.tools/eu5_tags_original.csv` already lists `CES FAE IMO PES RAV RIM URB` with `culture = romagnol`), i.e. that pop fix was a 1.2.1 -> 1.3 revision the mod had missed. Totals after the sync: 1.4 393,828 vs mod 347,184; the -12% is entirely inside the mod's own 6,256 edited locations. Per-location record: session `files/pops_1.4_sync_applied.csv`.
- **`07_cities_and_buildings.txt` vs 1.4 (checked + synced 2026-10-14).** Two sections: `locations = { <loc> = { rank town_setup } }` and `building_manager = { <building> = { tag level location } }` (one line per building instance); the mod removes things by commenting lines (903 in this file). Locations: the mod has all 1.4 locations (1,282 live vs 1.4's 1,024 - 258 are its own "NEW urban settlements", 45 are town->city upgrades, 19 commented). 4 stale values were synced: `hangzhou` rank city -> `megalopolis` (1.4's 4th megalopolis, after constantinople/cairo/dadu), `granada`/`malaga`/`almeria` -> `granada_city`/`malaga_city`/`almeria_town`. **These three setups live in `in_game/common/town_setups/00_default.txt`, which the mod overrides with a 1.3-era copy - 1.4's only additions to that file are exactly those three, and they were added to the mod's copy in the same commit. Any future town_setup reference must be added to BOTH files or the reference is undefined.** building_manager: 1.4 added 66 entries the mod never had; 3 were already present under the mod's own tags (`cathedral BYZ plovdiv` -> BUL, `city_walls CHI dadu` -> YUA, `jingzhao` -> CSI) and the other 63 were appended as one `#1.4` block, with 6 retagged to the mod's owner of the location (hangzhou -> WUU, kaifeng -> CSO, xiangyang -> ABCHU, coria -> LON, coruna -> GLC, bejaia -> TUN; all verified against the start-state). The 833 other 1.4 entries the mod lacks are its own removals (799 explicitly commented) - never re-add those. 1.4 also changed the owning tag on 11 entries; 10 stay as the mod has them (kozelj BUL, plovdiv BUL, dadu YUA, jingzhao CSI, 5x kurultai TIM/ATR/ABKAZ/NOG, and kyoto's `royal_court` which the mod gives to its own ABASK - note that line uses an uppercase `TAG =`, so attribute parsing must be case-insensitive), and 1 was stale and fixed: `castle SAV lausanne` -> LSA (the mod's own Lausanne prince-bishopric owns lausanne). Also: a real bug fixed - `castle = { tag = UPE level = 1 location = minya }` (mod commit `81ad7b7`) pointed at a tag that exists nowhere (Minya is MAM); it was the only undefined tag among the 934 the file uses. 4 entries 1.2.1 had and 1.4 dropped were commented out (cathedrals logrono/vitoria, stockades cork/limerick - 1.4 replaced them with city_walls and moved Castile's cathedrals). 6 castles the mod deliberately uncommented (BRB antwerp, FLA ghent, HOL the_hague, NAV pamplona/tudela, UTR utrecht) were left alone. Side observation, outside the 1.4 diff: 535 entries hand a building to a country that does not own the location - mostly legitimate (HSA kontors 41, KNI commanderies 31, GEN/VEN fondaci, TEU, GRA trade offices abroad) but `CHI` 119 and `CAS` 52 deserve a separate look, and 8 entries are duplicated in the mod's own file (e.g. `castle BUL kozelj`, `orthodox_monastery NOV novgorod`).
- **`08_institutions.txt` vs 1.4 (checked 2026-10-14, deliberately NOT synced).** Format is flat: `locations = { <loc> = { feudalism/legalism/meritocracy = yes } }` and both versions use exactly those three institution types - **1.4 added no new institution type**. This file is not in the `50acc71 "1.2.1 files"` upload; it arrived in `c87c114` (2026-06-20) as a vanilla copy, so that revision is the baseline to compare against, and the mod's only change is the **683 added lines in `d0bb9cd`** (63 existing blocks got `legalism`, 155 new `feudalism + legalism` blocks - e.g. kano, antananarivo, West-African and Hokkaido towns). Against that baseline 1.4 only *removes*: `legalism` from 30 locations (Ural/Perm 21 - cherdyn, solikamsk, usolye, nyrob, koygorodok, lalsk, kirs, kizel, gubakha, dobryanka, afanasyevo, chernaya, gayny, kelich, kosa_perm, lupya, obyachevo, podosinovets, ust_kulom, vizinga, yusva - plus Zimbabwe 9 - great_zimbabwe, matendere, mabveni, majiri, muchuchu, naletale, charumani, chibvumani, zvipadze) and whole blocks from 26 (Sahel 18 - agadez, assode, bodou_sailay, cherfe, gambaru, garoumale, iferouane, in_gall, makary, manan, marte, mondo, moussoro, ngousa, nguigmi, njimi, takedda, yo - plus Guinea 8 - igboho, ikoyi, irawo, kisi, ogbomosho, oyo_ile, saki, tede). Everything else (12,997 locations) is identical. **User decision: keep the mod's institutions everywhere** - the mod spreads them on purpose (218 blocks/entries of its own), so vanilla's 56 removals are intentionally not adopted; if a sync is ever wanted, those 56 are the exact list. All 181 extra location names in the mod's file are real map locations (no typos), and the file has no comments in either version.
- **`09_roads.txt` vs 1.4 (checked 2026-10-14): the file is unchanged in 1.4 - nothing to sync.** Format is `road_network = { <locA> = <locB> ... }` (undirected edges, one per line); vanilla 1.2.1 and 1.4 are **md5-identical** (1,646 edges, `744da8f1...`). The mod keeps all 1,646 vanilla edges and adds 640 of its own (228 marked `#NEW`, the rest under its `###NEW ADDITIONS###` sections: Scotland, Persia/Khorasan, China, India, SE Asia, West Africa, Lowlands, Kyushu/Shikoku/Honshu), plus one deliberately commented-out edge `#nei = yoshiki`. Three malformed lines added by the mod were fixed: `tsuga = ashikaga` -> `ashikaga_loc`, `lanark dumfries` -> `lanark = dumfries` (it had no `=` at all), and the broken `hinai = hiraka` line was dropped (`hiraka` is not a location; the next line already has `hinai = hiraka_aomori`). Note the 5 Campania roads marked `#1.4` are actually in vanilla 1.2.1 too - they were simply missing from the mod's file and were restored, so that marker means "restored from vanilla", not "1.4 addition".
- **`12_diplomacy.txt` vs 1.4 (checked + synced 2026-10-14).** `diplomacy_manager` with `dependency` / `scripted_mutual` / `scripted_oneway` statements (statements are indented with 4 spaces, comment headers with tabs). The mod removes entries by commenting them (28 statements) and dropped 384 of the baseline ones; it has 184 statements of its own. 1.4's delta against the 1.2.1 upload is tiny: it **added** `CAS -> GRA tributary` (its own comment: Treaty of Jaen 1246 / parias tribute) and `TUN -> BIJ familial_governor`, **changed** `TUN -> GFS` from `vassal` to `familial_governor`, and **dropped** `SER -> SMC vassal start_date 1330.1.1` (the mod never had it). All three applicable changes were applied and marked `#1.4`. `familial_governor` is a 1.4-new subject type: it is defined in vanilla's `in_game/common/subject_types/familial_governor.txt` - the mod only overrides `appanage.txt` in that folder - and localized in vanilla's `main_menu/localization/*/subject_*` files, which the mod does not override, so it is safe to reference. **This file is crash-critical to keep overridden:** every tag in the mod's live statements exists in the mod's DB, while vanilla 1.4's own copy references 18 deleted tags - that is exactly where `Undefined country tag: OBD` came from when the vanilla bookmark got loaded instead. The mod's 11 undefined-country references (`OBD KOD KZY LYA SVA BGJ MTS ACT CTV STO OPX`) are all inside commented-out lines; keep them commented. No self-dependencies, no duplicates.
- **`14_development.txt` vs 1.4 (checked 2026-10-14): nothing to sync - the file is the mod's own tuning, not a vanilla copy.** It holds the development formula (`base`, `coastal`, `river`, `road`, `city`, `town`, climate/vegetation/topography keys) plus per-location/area/province/region overrides. The mod's copy has 473 live entries - 180 marked `#NEW` and ~55 regional/climate keys annotated `#increased from X to Y` / `#reduced from X to Y`, which are its deliberate deviations from vanilla's numbers (e.g. `arctic = -3` vs vanilla -5, `tibet_region = 15` vs 10, `madagascar_region = 11` vs 5). Because every difference from 1.4 is annotated, none of it is stale data to import. The only keys 1.4 has that the mod does not are `connaught_area = -2` and `kola_area = -5`, both **commented out on purpose** ("commented out to reset to 0", i.e. the mod deliberately removes those two penalties); `pamplona = 2` (1.4's only added entry) was already ported. This file is one of the ones that is **not** in the `50acc71` upload (it arrived in `c87c114`, 2026-06-20, already containing the mod's `#NEW` entries; `d0bb9cd` then fixed the `doab_area = = -2` double-`=` typo and raised `ezo_area` -25 -> -16, `ryuukyuu_area` -15 -> 0, `kaffa` 3 -> 4, and added `crimea_area = 3`). All location/area/province/region keys resolve to real map names; only the formula keywords are not location names (that is expected), and there are no unparsed lines.
- **`15_international_organizations.txt` vs 1.4 (checked 2026-10-14; one fix applied).** Blocks are `add_international_organization = { type = ... members/leader/regions/areas/provinces/locations }`. 1.4 changed only **4 blocks** against the 1.2.1 upload and added no IO type: `high_kingship` +`INI` (Inishowen), `PAD` moved from `guelphs_io` to `ghibellines_io`, and `SMC` removed from an `autocephalous_patriarchate`. The mod already matched the PAD and SMC cases; **`INI` was added to `high_kingship`** so that block is now identical to 1.4. The mod's own IO work: it comments out 5 blocks (ilkhanate - commented in `d0bb9cd` - lordship_of_ireland, middle_kingdom, tribal_confederation x2; 1.4 keeps all five live), reworks the HRE (Burgundy/Flanders/lyonnais areas, `venice_province`, the trieste/rovinj/pola Gorizia-Istria locations, +ABSEL VEN TTE, -GNV -VUD, PST->SEL), rebuilds the Japanese shogunate + shinto from 146 Japanese tags down to its own `ABASK ABHJO ABTKG` set (leader ABASK), and adds a `defensive_league` IO instance of its own (valid type; vanilla's start-state creates none). **Silently pruned members, worth a second look if it was accidental:** present in both 1.2.1 and 1.4 but absent from the mod's file since the June upload and with no comment - one autocephalous patriarchate is missing `BIA BLD HSC HTN IAS SRC SSI`, `sect` misses `PAH` / `CHI` / `DDI`, `hindu_branch` misses `HSL JNG` / `BES LGK MNJ SNG` / `BHD GHL JLW PLT RNP`. All of those are live countries with land in the mod's world (HSL alone owns 47 locations); left unchanged by user decision. Every tag in the mod's live IO lines exists in its DB, and all 14 IO types it uses are vanilla-defined.
- **`16_wars.txt` vs 1.4 (checked + partially synced 2026-10-14).** `war_manager` with `truce` / `war` / `civil_war` blocks. 1.4's delta against the 1.2.1 upload: **added** the `SCALIGER_WAR_NAME` war (1336.6.22, attackers VEN + FLO/MLO/FER/MAN, defenders VER + its vassals PAD/PAR/LUC, war goal `superiority_coalition_scaliger_war` / `cb_scaligeri_war`), **added two Granada truces** (`CAS -> GRA` and `MOR -> GRA`, start 1337.1.1, months 13 - the same Across the Pillars batch as the Granada tributary in `12_diplomacy.txt`), and **renamed** the Tlemcen war from `NORMAL_WAR_NAME` to `FATE_OF_TLEMCEN`. **Applied: the two Granada truces and the rename** (marked `#1.4`); the Scaliger war was deliberately *not* adopted (user decision, 2026-10-14). The mod's file also **never contained vanilla's `CRUSADE_WAR_NAME` war** (the Northern Crusade from 1283.1.1: TEU/LIV plus their subjects vs LIT plus its dukes) - it exists in both 1.2.1 and 1.4 but in no mod commit since May; also left out by decision (the mod's `12_diplomacy.txt` does match its subject assumptions exactly: TEU->LIV, LIV->RIG/ARR/KUR/BIO/BID, TEU->ERM/SMD/PMS/CHL, LIT->DRU/NRK/PNK/POK/RZH/SSK/TPS/TUV/VBK, so it would drop in cleanly if ever wanted - every key it needs is vanilla-defined in folders the mod does not override: `wargoals/00_default.txt` for `superiority_coalition_scaliger_war` and `crusade_conquest`, `casus_belli/01_event_triggered.txt` for `cb_scaligeri_war`, `casus_belli/crusade_cb.txt` for `cb_crusade`; the mod only overrides 6 unrelated casus_belli files). The mod's own war work is left alone: the Ilkhanate war was rewritten in `d0bb9cd` (renamed to `NORMAL_WAR_NAME`, war goal switched from `unify_ilkhanate_wargoal` to `take_province`/`conquer_province` at baghdad, participants retagged to its `ABQUD ABALP ABLEB ...` tags - 1.4's version has 9 more attackers and 15 more defenders), and its truce list (21 after this change, vs vanilla's 10) includes its own additions (China/Japan, Levant, Hungary, NAP->ALB, CHE/CDN, its Bohemia rework) plus 100-month versions of the ENG->SBL/SCO and FRA->SBL truces where 1.2.1/1.4 use 48. Note: the `SECOND = {` -> `second = { #1.4` edit in this file was a style fix from the port, not a 1.4 sync - 1.4 still writes `SECOND` in that block, it only dropped `ordinal=1` there. All tags in the file resolve to countries in the mod's DB.
- **`19_diseases.txt` vs 1.4 (checked + synced 2026-10-14).** Not in `50acc71`; it arrived in `c87c114` (2026-06-20) already modded, so that upload is the reference. `disease_outbreak_manager` holding `add_disease_outbreaks` / `add_disease_resistance` blocks (`type`, `resistance`, `regions` / `areas` / `locations`); 10 active + 2 commented example blocks in every version. 1.4's delta only touched entries the mod had never edited: `smallpox` resistance **0.3 -> 0.5** (applied, `resistance = 0.5 #1.4`) and the two `malaria` blocks (`add_disease_outbreaks` + the `resistance = 1` one) gained `locations = { ferrara argenta ficarolo rovigo mirandola guastalla lodi mantova ostiglia voghera salo_ita pancevo petrovaradin sremska_mitrovica sabac }` (applied verbatim, each preceded by a `#1.4` marker line) - Po-valley and Sava/Danube marshes. The two **`bubonic_plague`** blocks are the author's own rework (`d0bb9cd`, 2026-06/07): vanilla's 0.1/0.4 tiers use whole `regions` only (20/41), while the mod lists `areas` for Europe/China/India (e.g. the England+Wales areas instead of `great_britain_region`, so Scotland is outside that tier) - deliberate, left alone. The third malaria block (`resistance = 0.3`, Mediterranean areas) matches 1.4 exactly. Verification: entries token-compared against 1.4 (no mod-only / vanilla-only tokens left), braces 35/35, 568 CRLF and 0 bare LF, no BOM.
- **1.4 error-log triage (2026-10-14).** EU5 rotates `logs/error.log` at ~1 MB and the fresh chunk takes the LOWEST free number, so one session is `error.3.log -> error.2.log -> error.1.log -> error.log` (chunk mtime = chunk end; a rotation resumes at the exact boundary timestamp). Parsing `error.log` alone therefore hides ~78 % of a session (3,625 of 16,758 occurrences). `.tools/tmp/error-audit-2026-10-14/tools/parse_session.py` merges the chunks of one session (session detection by boundary timestamp) and calls `parse_abm_errors.py`; `tools/audit_report.py` derives the tables in that folder (see its README.md for the cluster breakdown of the 21:51 session). Root cause of nearly all counts: **"ghost" countries** - country objects that own nothing, so vanilla systems (`auto_modifiers/catholic_church.txt` 6.5k, `byzantium.txt` 1k, `unique_buildings.txt`, `00_coa_triggers.txt:351`, `05_characters.txt`, `character.cpp:3040`) fail on their unset `government_type`/`culture`/`religion` links. Ghosts come from (a) the mod's ~150 `AB*` tags that exist only as `in_game/setup/countries/*.txt` definitions, (b) the 124 vanilla tags the mod removed from the 1337 start-state that vanilla's `05_characters.txt` (871 tags) and `26_ai_personalities.txt` still reference - mostly vanilla Japanese clans, since the mod re-tags Japan with `AB*` (ABASK = Ashikaga) - and (c) countries in the mod's own 1337 files with an empty `own_control_core` (VDK `# karikkode`, STR, PDU). **Fixed in this pass (cluster B, first batch):** `modifier:allow_military_order_units` (never a 1.4 modifier; vanilla gates the same units with `can_recruit_crusader_regiments`) in `unit_types/0_abm_knights.txt` x2; restored the 10 vanilla levy definitions the mod's `common/levies/0{2,3,4,5}_*.txt` overrides had dropped (`levy_{heavy,light}_camelry_1..4`, `levy_order_knights`, `levy_order_knights_2` - they broke `2_army_unlocks.txt`'s `unlock_levy`); `mai_culture` -> `mai_culture_culture` (1.4 culture key) in `10_countries_china_south_region.txt`; dropped the `TUN -> BIJ` dependency added in `edc5833` (BIJ is 1.4-new, never in the mod); `LONG_IMPERIAL_REGENCY_YEARS` -> `define:NInternationalOrganization|LONG_IMPERIAL_REGENCY_YEARS` in `international_organizations/hre.txt` (1.4 define syntax). Also migrated (B2) `global_trade_through_owned_territory_efficiency` -> `trade_land_efficiency` in all 16 `in_game/common/advances/abm_*.txt` uses (`#1.4: was ...` marker). Despite the name it is NOT a 1.3 modifier: it only ever existed in the mod's own EU4-import tables, and `.tools/eu4_vs_eu5_advances.csv:500` describes it as "the reach of the trade_range of a country when traveling over a location they own" - word for word 1.4's `trade_land_efficiency` description - so the import invented the false name while the mod's own advances already used `trade_land_efficiency = {tiny,small,medium,large}_trade_land_efficiency_bonus` (21x; those are code-provided script values). The mapping sources were corrected too, so a re-import cannot reintroduce it: the MOD_MAP key in `.tools/scripts/update_advance_names_icons_requires2.py`, `.tools/eu4_vs_eu5_advances.csv:500`, `.tools/eu5/caucasus.csv:56` and `india_indochina_indonesia_advances.csv:100,166`. The 16 import values were then rewritten to the code-provided script values in `main_menu/common/script_values/default_values.txt` (tiny 0.05 / small 0.10 / medium 0.20 / large 0.40 / huge 0.66), value-equivalently: 0.10 -> `small_trade_land_efficiency_bonus` (7), 0.15 -> `medium_...` (1), 0.20 -> `medium_...` (5), 0.30 -> `large_...` (1), 0.40 -> `large_...` (2); the mod's advances now hold 22 medium / 10 large / 7 small. The matching CSV cells were switched from percents to bucket names too (`.tools/eu5/caucasus.csv:56`, `india_indochina_indonesia_advances.csv:100,166`) - every other row in those tables already used bucket names. (The sibling `global_trade_through_owned_territory_cost_modifier` is unused and has no 1.4 counterpart - `.tools/eu4_vs_eu5_advances4_clean.csv` suggests `global_trade_power`, which 1.4 does not define - so its MOD_MAP key was left alone.) Fixed (B3) all 25 dead advance prerequisites: an advance key reference only resolves when the target's file was already parsed, and the 23 refs to `slave_raid_advance` (defined in `region_maghreb.txt`) fail 23/23 while every ref to a `[0-3]_*`/`0_age_*`-defined advance resolves - so a prerequisite must come from an early-loaded file. `.tools/scripts/update_advance_names_icons_requires2.py` picked those names from the stale `.tools/eu5/advances/` (1.3-era) corpus, so re-runs can reintroduce them; both were replaced with the same-age, early-file equivalents: `slave_raid_advance` -> `trade_advance_age_of_trad` (23x, `0_age_of_traditions.txt`) and `viceroyalty_advance` -> `trade_companies_advance` (2x, `0_age_of_reformation.txt`), each marked `#1.4: was <old>`. **Superseded (2026-10-15, release build):** the earlier "needs no fix / build drift" verdict for `kingdom_of_god_slot_modifier` was WRONG - the 1.4-open-beta file set did define it at `main_menu/common/static_modifiers/country.txt:6178`, but the released build removed it (nothing matching `kingdom_of_god` under `static_modifiers` any more; vanilla's `kingdom_of_god_reform` now grants `government_reform_slots = 1` itself and vanilla's `flavor_pap.20` just calls `unlock_government_reform_effect` + `add_reform`), so the 6 rows (2 at `government_reforms/abm_gov_reforms_europe.txt:25,27`, 2 at `events/abm_pap_kingdom_of_god.txt:49,67`, + 2 PostValidate) were **live errors**, now fixed by mirroring vanilla (dropped the `on_deactivate` cleanup and both event `if` blocks): the logged session ran the `1.4-open-beta` branch (`logs/code_revisions.log` `game_branch: ud009/update_byz_dlc`) while Steam switched the game folder to `public` ~1 h later (`appmanifest_3450310.acf`: `MountedConfig.BetaKey = public` vs `UserConfig.BetaKey = 1.4-open-beta`, `LastUpdated 22:56:57`, buildid 24187685, 2.1 GB still pending; vanilla file mtimes 22:55-22:56). **Generalize: the parsed `error.log` and the installed files can be different builds.** `events/DHE/flavor_mll.txt` and `common/achievements/d009_achievements.txt` exist nowhere on disk, and `crypto_jewish_community` / `crypto_muslim_community` / `mll_leges_palatinae` / `centralized_makhzen_policy` / `negotiated_authority_policy` have zero definitions in the current install; the `kurultai`/`order_commandery` "cannot be built here" warnings are likewise NOT beta-only: they come from the mod's own `REPLACE:` blocks in `common/building_types/abm_unique_buildings.txt` (`kurultai` is `rural_settlement = yes`, `town/city = no`; `order_commandery` the mirror image), both keeping `allow_wrong_startup = yes` so the inherited vanilla placements still work, and they still fire on the release build (37 rows, +3 from the mod's `#NEW` ranks in `07_cities_and_buildings.txt`: `masqat`, `jaunpur`, `enice`). So **re-validate every DB-object row against the current build before fixing it** - B1 `can_recruit_crusader_regiments` (vanilla `unit_types/0_knights.txt:197`, defined in `main_menu/common/modifier_type_definitions/00_modifier_types.txt`), B10a `mai_culture_culture` and B16 `define:NInternationalOrganization|LONG_IMPERIAL_REGENCY_YEARS` (vanilla `international_organizations/hre.txt:397,852,858`) all re-checked good, but the audit's "vanilla-file" bucket was only attributed against the ABM repo (never against the 13 workshop mods), so treat it as a lower bound. **Fixed in that pass:** (B6a) `common/production_methods/unsorted_building_inputs.txt` is a 1.3-era full-file override that had dropped 17 vanilla `*_maintenance` production methods (`sergeantry`, `city_guard`, `temple_militia`, `thema_headquarters`, `warrior_temple`, `warrior_lodge`, `sofa_barracks`, `kalari`, `kurmina_headquarter`, `hre_imperial_armory_{own,foreign}`, `korean_barracks`, `provincial_garrison`, `telpochcalli`, `segovia_artillery_academy`, `order_stronghold`, `trade_company_governance`) which `building_types/*` reference; the file was otherwise a strict same-order subset of vanilla, so each block was restored byte-identical at its vanilla-relative position (`#1.4:` marker, 83 -> 100 blocks). (B6b) the mod's `REPLACE:order_commandery` (`building_types/abm_unique_buildings.txt`) had `is_foreign = yes` but no `want_foreign_pop_created`, which 1.4 defaults to **yes** and then requires `pop_size_created` for (all 43 vanilla `is_foreign` buildings set one or the other; vanilla's own `order_commandery` sets `no`) - added `want_foreign_pop_created = no`. **Still open (need decisions):** the 18 "above max level" and remaining "cannot be built here" rows are **location-dependent** caps (the same building reports `Max is 1` in one location and `Max is 2` in another) evaluated against `town_setups/00_default.txt`, which the mod deliberately overrides in full (126 setups vs vanilla's 120, nothing missing; its 45 shared-setup differences are the mod's own level/mix choices) - they need a fresh log from the branch actually played; `abm_buddhist_monastery_stronghold`'s "too high efficiency for manpower ... its 0.02 when 0.0125 is allowed" is an engine-derived check whose reported 0.02 matches no literal in the block (`local_manpower = 0.002`), so also re-check; `crisis_of_the_chinese_dynasty.7` triggered by `cabinet_actions/reform_taxation_system.txt` although the mod removed the disaster (and emptied that event file); `flavor_jap.2.*` loc keys missing; CRI and ZAZ both own the same 39 Dnieper locations; the 15 `setup/countries/colonial_nations.txt` entries lack `culture_definition`/`religion_definition`; and the mod's `gui/*.gui` copies still use 1.2-era widget types (`ContextMenuActionEntry`) and data functions (`PossibleTrade`, `QuickTradeOrders`, `AlertHasUneducatedChild`). Fixed (B7) the duplicate start-state ownership behind 39 `Country 'X' is assigned as owner of location 'L' but its already owned by 'Y'` warnings: the mod's own `main_menu/setup/1337/10_countries_*.txt` blobs claimed the same location twice, and the engine keeps the FIRST claim (file order, then line order) and silently drops the later one - so the rejected tag ends up landless, i.e. a **ghost** (this is how the warnings and the cluster-A error cascade connect). Which side to strip is a design call, not a mechanical one: for the ZAZ case the *later* claimant is the intended owner (the mod's `07_cities_and_buildings.txt:5173-5175` gives ZAZ `republican_assembly`/`sergeantry`/`castle` in `kichkas` with `#NEW - ZAZ` markers, ZAZ's `capital = kichkas`, and ZAZ's list is a curated harvest of CRI's grouping whose rejected candidates are left commented out), so CRI ceded 23 locations (21 in `own_control_core` plus `oril`/`adalaga` in `own_control_integrated`, which then became empty) and KIE ceded `karlivka`; everywhere else the user's primary tags win, so GLH ceded the 8 Astrakhan locations to its own `#NEW` vassal ATR, TDJ ceded `akkus`/`terme` and HCI its only location `mesudiye` to TRE, KLT ceded `kannur`/`thalassery` and ALU `udupi` to VND (HCI/KLT/ALU are now landless as `own_control_core = { }`, vanilla's own form for such tags), and ETH's `gambo` appeared in both `own_control_core` and `own_control_integrated` so the integrated copy was dropped. Verified by rescanning all 63 `10_countries*.txt`: **zero** locations are now owned by more than one tag. (Finding the conflicts needs a parser that treats `own_control_core = { }` as already closed - naive line scanning mis-reads the lines that follow an empty section as locations.) Fixed (B9) the 16 + 15 `initialize_from_bookmark.cpp:498/501` `Country 'X' has no culture/religion set` warnings: `in_game/setup/countries/colonial_nations.txt` is a generated stub (15 EU4-derived `AB*` colonial nations; its header points at `.tools/scripts/import_eu4_tags_flags_colors_definitions.py`, which has no culture/religion handling at all, and `.tools/eu5_tags_mod.csv` has `culture,religion` columns but no `AB*` rows) whose entries carried only `color`/`color2`/`description_category`/`difficulty`. Every other `AB*` entry in the mod's `setup/countries/*.txt` declares `culture_definition` + `religion_definition` right after `color2`, so the two fields were added in that order, valued as the historical mother country: ABALA `muscovite`/`orthodox` (Russian America), ABBRZ `portuguese`/`catholic`, ABQUE `french`/`catholic`, the six Spanish ones (ABSNA ABTEX ABBOL ABARG ABCOL ABPER) `castilian`/`catholic`, the six British ones (ABCSC ABVRM ABWSI ABAUS ABEIC ABNZL) `english`/`catholic` (the mother country's own 1337 values, which is what the rest of that DB uses - switch to `protestant` if the colonies should reflect the post-Reformation colonizer). The 16th culture warning was a different tag, ABEJP (Eastern Japan) in `east_asia.txt:81`, which had `religion_definition = shinto` but no culture; it got `tougoku_culture`, the id the mod already uses for its other eastern-Japan tags (ABHJO, ABTKG - western Japan uses `saigoku_culture`). Verified with a full scan of the mod's 39 `setup/countries/*.txt`: no entry missing either field, and every referenced id exists in the current build. Two grep traps found while validating ids: `english = {` is the FIRST line of `cultures/british.txt` behind the BOM (so `^english = {` finds nothing), and `satsana_phi  = {` in `religions/folk_se_asian.txt` has two spaces before the `=`, so id extraction must tolerate both. **Fixed (B15)** the deleted-systems event errors: the mod removes whole vanilla systems by shipping 50 files whose only content is `#This is an empty file for now.` - but an emptied *event* file also loses its `namespace =` declaration, and the namespace is what makes references resolve, so a stub silently invalidated it and every trigger of those events errored twice (`jomini_eventmanager.cpp:118 'X' does not have a valid namespace` plus `Event [X.n] not found` / `Invalid event id`). 9 stubs hide a namespace - `events/disaster/{chinese_crisis_events,death_of_hayan_wuruk,decline_of_majapahit,decline_of_mali,dissolution_of_delhi}.txt`, `events/situations/{fall_of_delhi,red_turban_rebellions,rise_of_timur}.txt`, `events/imperial_examination_events.txt` (find them with a mod-vs-vanilla namespace diff, not by eye) - and each now declares its namespace at the top while still defining ZERO events, so the content stays removed. Also dropped the mod's own dangling trigger: `common/cabinet_actions/reform_taxation_system.txt` is already disabled (`potential = { always = no }`) but still carried vanilla's `on_fully_activated = { scope:actor = { trigger_event_non_silently = crisis_of_the_chinese_dynasty.7 } }`, which is validated at load even when the potential can never pass. That accounts for 21 of the 53 rows in this class; the other 32 are references inside vanilla-only files (`on_action/_hardcoded.txt` x5, `on_action/country_yearly.txt`, `scripted_effects/{on_action_effects,situation_effects}.txt`, `generic_actions/{crisis_of_the_chinese_dynasty_actions,middle_kingdom}.txt`) - fixing those needs either stubbing those files or declaring no-op events for the referenced ids (unverified: whether an event without options is legal). Two stub-set gaps found while checking: the mod stubs the crisis's AI list but not `generic_actions/crisis_of_the_chinese_dynasty_actions.txt`, and it stubs the middle_kingdom IO/laws but not `generic_actions/middle_kingdom.txt` (+ `generic_action_ai_lists/middle_kingdom_list.txt`; those 8 action keys are also listed in the mod's `main_menu/gui/messagetypes.txt`), while the only live `middle_kingdom` references left in the mod are in `events/DHE/flavor_KOR.txt`.** Follow-up (same pass): the five half-stubbed files were completed - `common/generic_actions/{crisis_of_the_chinese_dynasty_actions,middle_kingdom,decline_of_majapahit_actions,lordship_of_ireland}.txt` and `common/generic_action_ai_lists/middle_kingdom_list.txt` are now empty placeholders as well. **Rule learned: an emptied AI list WITHOUT its actions file is worse than neither** - the engine then logs `Action X is not explicitly listed in an ai list!` for every action of the still-present file (14 such rows: 5 `cmd_*`, 6 majapahit, 3 lordship_of_ireland), so the pair must be stubbed together; all 12 actions/AI-list files for the removed systems are now stubs. Two things checked and deliberately left alone because the mod's older stubs prove they are inert (no rows): the vanilla price entries keyed by removed actions (`reshape_bureaucracy`, `strengthen_ministry`, `proclaim_decree`, `exempt_from_tribute`, `rtr_*` in `prices/00_hardcoded.txt` - a mod cannot delete a price key, only replace it) and the auto-generated `WE_PERFORM_<action>_ACTION` blocks still in the mod's `main_menu/gui/messagetypes.txt`. What is left of this family (~350 rows) is the `middle_kingdom` IO that the mod stubbed: `key 'middle_kingdom' does not lead to a unique international organization` and `Unknown international organization type middle_kingdom` are emitted by VANILLA files that reference it - `scripted_effects/international_organization_effects.txt:1675` (10), `scripted_effects/country_effects.txt:628/630/634` (6), `laws/02_country_specific.txt:985-987,1037`, `wargoals/00_default.txt:912,920`, `achievements/standard_achievements.txt:385,692`, `societal_values/00_default.txt:437,438`, `estate_privileges/{burghers,nobles}_estate.txt`, `decisions/flavor_decisions_various.txt:1932`, `cabinet_actions/gaituguiliu_policy.txt:7,8`, `events/DHE/flavor_chi.txt:8529`, `on_action/country_yearly.txt:44` - and it is a DESIGN call, not a mechanical fix: (A) restore the IO (plus its payments/special-status/land-ownership rules, and probably `laws/20_middle_kingdom.txt` + the tribute files) so the key resolves again, at the cost of reintroducing the institution, or (B) keep it removed and accept the noise, because the two big referencers cannot be stubbed without breaking unrelated systems; **decision (user, 2026-10-15): keep the IO removed and accept those ~350 rows** - do not restore the Middle Kingdom stack. **Fixed (B17)** missing event localization - the reported case was only `flavor_jap.2.*` (6 log rows) because the engine warns about the keys it happens to request, but a load-independent scan (collect every key from the mod's + vanilla's english loc, then check every `title`/`desc`/`name`/`custom_tooltip`/`historical_info` value used in the mod's event files) found THREE namespaces with no loc at all: `flavor_jap.2` (the mod's own Rangaku event, inserted where vanilla jumps .1 -> .3), `abm_enforce_peace.1/2/3` (the Enforce Peace chain fired from `country_interactions/abm_enforce_peace.txt`) and `abm_pap_kog.1` (Kingdom of God). The 21 keys were authored in `main_menu/localization/english/abm_events_l_english.yml` (the mod ships english only, files named `abm_*_l_english.yml`, BOM + CRLF, keys indented by one space, `#italic ...#!`/`#bold ...#!`, `[scope:x.GetName]`, `$key$`); note `<ns>.<id>.entry` keys are conventional, not required - vanilla omits them for some of its own DH events and the engine only complains when an event has NO loc at all, and `abm_mp_limits.1/2` are not DH events so their missing `entry` was a false positive of the auto-entry rule. `jap_rangaku`, set by `flavor_jap.2.a`, is read by nothing yet. **Fixed (B10b/B10c)** the log's single B10 row: **(a)** `10_countries_japan_region.txt:137 dynasty = tokugawa_dynasty` - dynasties are DATABASE objects in `main_menu/setup/1337/04_dynasties.txt` (the `dynasty_names_l_english.yml` loc only *names* them), the mod does not override that file and no `tokugawa_dynasty` exists anywhere (vanilla's Japan set: ashikaga/imagawa/oda/nitta/genji - no Tokugawa/Matsudaira), so the correctly-placed country-level line was commented out in the mod's own `#dynasty = ...` idiom (`ruler = random` now picks a dynasty from the culture pool); a rescan of every `dynasty =` in the mod's `setup/1337/*.txt` against vanilla's 1289 dynasty keys gives 0 broken refs (beware: `canbolad_dynasty` is space-indented in vanilla's file, a tab-only regex reports it as missing). **(b)** `10_countries_deccan_region.txt:188 JWR tolerated_cultures = { konkani }` - the mod re-cultured 5 of the 104 shared Indian tags in its `in_game/setup/countries/india.txt` (BGL/DOG/JWR/KUT/MAB); JWR's primary became `konkani` (vanilla `bhil`) while vanilla's bookmark line kept tolerating `konkani` -> duplicate; fixed to `{ }`, mirroring the mod's own BRA block that keeps its other minorities but comments out the primary (`#brandenburgish`). Scan of all 63 files (comments stripped): 0 duplicates. **B4 closed without code changes**: both `requires = viceroyalty_advance` sites (`abm_africa_sahel.txt:329`, `abm_arabia.txt:1241`) were already rewritten to `trade_companies_advance` in the earlier dead-prerequisite pass and the substitute is sound (vanilla `0_age_of_reformation.txt:207`, no `potential`, same age as both dependents); re-auditing the class found 0 refs to non-existent advances (of 5680 keys) and 0 missing `REPLACE:` targets. **Load-order rule refined**: a parse-time key reference only fails inside a NEW (non-`REPLACE:`) definition - references inside `REPLACE:` blocks resolve in the deferred pass, which is why `abm_ruthenia.txt` can require `flexible_loyalty`/`mother_of_russian_cities`/`ruthenia_commoner_education` from vanilla's later-loading `culture_ruthenian.txt` with no log row at all. **Fixed (B20)** the 28 `Regnal number refers to a non-localized name X` rows (22 distinct names, `ruler_term_container.cpp:134`), all mod-side: vanilla 1.4 has none of those keys and its `main_menu/setup/1337/10_countries.txt` is not even loaded (the mod ships a header-only stub at that path), so the 63 regional files are the only country-data source. The engine resolves a regnal key by its FIRST `.`-separated component, so `name_john.name_asen` passes while bare `Ali`/`Miszko`/`Barisone`/`Moritz`/`Iwan` and misspelled `name_henri`/`name_solomon`/`name_thobald`/`name_friedrich` fail; bare keys that do resolve (`Kazimir`, `Orzocco`, `Torchitorio`, `Gonario`, `Egilmar`, `Saltaro`, `Ubaldo`, `Chaka`, `Abd_al-Haqq`, `Abd_al-Wahid`) are hyphens-allowed plain entries in vanilla's `character_names_l_english.yml`. All 22 were remapped to the exact key vanilla uses for the same tag (e.g. CYP `name_henri`->`name_henry`, TRN `Aymo`/`Girold`/`Willhelm`->`name_haimo`/`name_gerald`/`name_william`, TOR `Enzo`->`name_henry`, OLD `Moritz`->`name_maurice`, BNT `name_dirk`->`name_theodoric`, SAN `name_eudes`->`name_otto`, BUL `Ivan_Asen`->`name_john.name_asen`, PNK/TUV `name_sviatoslav`->`name_svyatoslav`) - 27 line edits across 11 regional files; recheck: 1655 regnal entries, 0 unresolved. **Release-build session (2026-10-15 00:52):** drift is over - Steam finished the pending update at 23:18:11 (`appmanifest`: buildid 25905365, `TargetBuildID 0`, 49308464/49308464 bytes) and the game then ran the release branch (`code_revisions.log` 23:46:41 `caesar/ud009/release/next`, 2026.41.0); that session (`error.3.log` -> `error.log`, 23:46:43-00:15:44) parsed to 5666 rows, 193 pointing at a mod file, kept as `session_2318_release_build_all.csv`. It confirms B1, B2, B3, B5, B6a and B16 are silent now; the work done after 00:03 (B9/B10/B14/B15 stubs/B17 loc/B20 regnal) needs one more run to clear, though B14 was live (now fixed) and `ruler_term_container` still lists exactly the 22 names B20 fixed. Also visible: `advance_definition.cpp:857 Unknown levy-setup 'levy_sometent'` and `jomini_scriptvalue.h:906 Cannot read [] as a script value at common/advances/abm_persia.txt:80`. CSV rows whose message carries no path are mis-attributed (the `castilian_*` DNA-template rows are vanilla `gfx/portraits/portrait_modifiers/zz_beard_pool_prototype.txt`), so grep the merged log before blaming a file. **B6c/B6d (fixed 2026-10-15, user decision: keep the mod's `kurultai`/`order_commandery` restrictions and accept their rows).** In `main_menu/setup/1337/07_cities_and_buildings.txt` ten lines are commented out with `#1.4:` notes: the 3 mod-side conflicts - `enice`'s duplicate `town` rank line (462, enice stays the `steppe_rural_settlement` that keeps CRI's kurultai legal) plus its `trade_office` placement (2552), `market_village` at `masqat` (2860) and `elephant_hunting_grounds` at `jaunpur` (4087), both because the mod's `#NEW` town ranks win - and the 6 invalid mod-added placements (5591 `leh`/hospital, 6076/6077 `changhua`+`wenchang`/sergeantry, 6357 `seta`/bog_iron_smelter, 6585/6586 `ishigaki`+`iriomote`/farming_village), which are impossible under 1.4: hospital and sergeantry are `town = yes, city = yes` only, farming_village needs an agricultural raw material (miyakojima works, the other two Ryukyu islands do not), seta has no bog iron. **B6e (fixed 2026-10-15).** The "above max level" rows come from the town_setup templates, and the caps from script values (`guild_max_level = 1 + 0.1*development + 0.05*population` +5 city / +10 megalopolis; `rural_building_cap` adds `0.5*max_rgo_workers`). The mod reuses vanilla's `east_african_coastal_town` - written for `kilwa_kisiwani`, a *city* here, so its +5 city bonus is what makes `mason = 3` legal - for 8 `#NEW` towns, and 5 of them overflow. Fix: a new `in_game/common/town_setups/abm_town_setups.txt` (the folder is a flat named-template DB, 117 vanilla entries, no wrapper) defining `abm_swahili_port_town` (mason 2, jewelry/tools/furniture 2) and `abm_swahili_port_town_small` (all guilds 1), both keeping the coastal flavour; `lamu`/`malindi`/`tintingue` use the first, `mozambique`/`memba` the second, and `zanzibar`/`mombasa`/`kisiju`/`kilwa_kisiwani` keep vanilla's template because they fit. `kaisou` was not a template case at all - its `fiber_crops_farm` is an explicit entry (6468), so it went 7 -> 6. `suceava`/`kursk`/`pasai` are byte-identical to vanilla in rank + template, so their 6 rows are vanilla-data parity and stay accepted. **B21 (fixed 2026-10-15):** `common/advances/abm_persia.txt:80` was `monthly_diplomats 0.05 #new` - a missing `=` that the parser read as an empty script value (`Cannot read [] as a script value` + `Badly read script value`); a repo-wide scan for `<key> <number>` lines without `=` found this as the only instance, and `monthly_diplomats` is a real field (vanilla `advances/0_age_of_discovery.txt:277` uses `= 0.1`). The other new row, `Unknown levy-setup 'levy_sometent' to unlock`, is **vanilla-side**: only vanilla `in_game/common/advances/region_iberia.txt:192` mentions it and no levy with that id exists anywhere in the install, so it goes in the cluster-C ignore list (the mod does not ship that file).  **B22 fixed (2026-10-15):** `main_menu/gui/messagetypes.txt` was a stale full override - **1371 top-level entries vs vanilla 1.4's 1551**, so 204 vanilla message types were unresolvable (the 57 `message_handler.cpp:434 Failed to find message type` rows). The file is otherwise vanilla's order, with the mod's 23 custom entries appended after a `# --- Ars Belli: custom message types (added below) ---` separator; all 23 belong to live mod content (`abm_enforce_peace`, `abm_pay_for_access`, `worsen_opinion`, `break_others_guarantee`, `abm_send_economic_support`, `abm_force_break_union`), so they were kept. Fix: inserted the 204 missing blocks at their vanilla-relative anchors (23 of them, being the vanilla tail, go just before the ABM separator), dropped one redundant duplicate (`INDEPENDANCE` was declared twice - `geopolitics` matching vanilla plus a stray `diplomacy` copy; the file's other 5 duplicate keys are **vanilla's own** - `WE_PERFORM_request/remove_cardinal_seat`, `OTHER_PERFORMS_tordesillas_claim_area`, `PERFORM_reinstate_the_legislative_assembly`, `PERFORM_challenge_bureaucratic_entrenchment` - and were left alone), and aligned the **7** drifted bodies to vanilla (5 `ESTATE_CULTURE/RELIGION_CHANGED_*` where vanilla disabled the sound with `#sound=yes - TODO in ud010`, plus the 2 `UNCONDITIONALSURRENDER_*` where vanilla **has** `sound=yes`). Result: 1574 entries / 1569 distinct = vanilla's 1546 + the mod's 23, brace balance 0, BOM + CRLF preserved, every vanilla 1.4 block present verbatim. **52 of the 57 rows clear; the other 5 are vanilla-side** - vanilla declares only the `WE_PERFORM_<x>_ACTION` half for `add_discriminated_culture`, `favor_heir`, `remove_discriminated_culture`, `return_lent_unit` and `set_colonial_charter_goal`, so the engine still asks for the missing `PERFORM_<x>_ACTION` counterpart (cluster C). **Method traps:** a line-based diff is useless here (all blocks look alike - myers reported ~500 hunks for what is 204 inserted blocks + 8 replaced ones; `git diff --patience` gives the true 2049/15), an in-place edit loop that replaces byte ranges must not mutate the list it derived its indices from, and `utf-8-sig` must be used to read the first key or the BOM hides it.  **B8 part 1 (2026-10-15, in progress).** The mod ships 12 `in_game/gui/*.gui` files (NOT `main_menu/gui/`). Diagnosis: they are 1.2/1.3-era full copies of vanilla files, so each one *replaces* vanilla 1.4 - and the mod's `context_menu.gui` copy was missing vanilla's whole market/goods type block, which made **vanilla's own** files fail to resolve `GoodsContextMenu`/`GoodsMarketContextMenu` (the rows reported at `market_goods.gui`, `cooltip.gui`, `trade_policies_lateralview.gui`, `goods_production_lateralview.gui`, `trade_order_lateral_view.gui`, `production_method_details.gui` all trace back to it). Method: take vanilla 1.4 as the base and re-apply only what the mod actually authored - the `# Ars Belli` comment markers are the reliable signal for that, and `git diff --patience` (or a block-level compare) is the only way to read the result.
