Done so far: **context_menu.gui fully ported** (vanilla 1.4 base + its 3 marked changes: the INTERVENE_IN_WAR pin filter and the two BECOME_MERCENARY exclusions; 17 types, brace balance 0, no `ContextMenuActionEntry` and no dead `PossibleTrade.*`/`QuickTradeOrders.*` refs remain - note vanilla 1.4 renamed `ContextMenuActionEntry` to `targetted_button_regular` + `using = contextmenu_entry_button_inner_template` + `scripted_action_tooltip`, and the mod's 7 extra Quick*/PossibleTrade context-menu types were *unmarked* 1.2 leftovers, not mod features); **`use_global_input_instance` removed at all 5 live sites** (`single_unit_window.gui` x3, `right_panel.gui`, `alertmanager.gui`) - vanilla 1.4 has zero live occurrences (its 9 are all commented out); **`war_lateralview.gui`** surrender button ported from the 1.2 `action_button = {` form to 1.4's `button = {` + `scripted_action_tooltip`/`button_tooltip_override`/`enabled` idiom. **B8 part 2 (2026-10-15):** `alertmanager.gui` **ported** - and the earlier "it is a real fork with its own alerts" reading was WRONG. Its 5 extra alert ids (`has_uneducated_child`, `can_use_religious_action`, `has_high_complacency`, `has_no_supplies_trade`, `has_unprofitable_trade`) are **removed 1.2/1.3 vanilla alerts**, not mod features: none appears among vanilla 1.4's 137 `in_game/common/alert_descriptions/00_default.txt` entries, and 1.4 replaced them (`heir_can_have_better_education`, `unprofitable_buildings`). The mod ships **no** `alert_descriptions` folder at all, so those GUI blocks could never have registered. The file's only real mod content is **one line** - the `can_intervene_in_rival_war` alert's action, which vanilla 1.4 still defines as `[OpenDiploAction('intervene_in_war')]` and the mod repoints at `[OpenDiploAction('abm_enforce_peace')]`. Ported by taking vanilla 1.4 verbatim and changing that one line: `git diff` vs vanilla is now exactly 3 lines (the note + the action), brace balance 0, CRLF-only, no BOM, all 377 alert widgets present. Clears all 16 rows (10 missing `_action` widgets, 4 failed callbacks from the `[AlertX.Next]` of the dead alerts, 2 malformed `AlertHasUneducatedChild.*` tokens, 2 unlocalized `ALERT_CHILD_UNEDUCATED_*`, 2 "Not enough widget entries", plus the `use_global_input_instance` one). **Generalize: in these GUI files an unmarked difference is 1.2/1.3 vanilla text that 1.4 rewrote or removed, NOT mod intent - only `# Ars Belli` markers and `abm_` references count as mod content.** Also: the mod's localization lives in **two** trees (`main_menu/localization/english/` *and* `in_game/localization/english/`), so a "missing loc key" must be checked in both before being called missing. **Still open in B8:** `panels/organization/union.gui` (6 rows - the file adds one marked `# Ars Belli force_break_union button` block using the 1.2 `action_button_diamond` type, which needs the 1.4 equivalent), `foreign_country_lateralview.gui` (1 row - 3 marked ABM blocks to re-apply: "Foreign Diplo Limits" and "row 2: AI personality + relative power"), and the six error-free stale copies (`multi_unit_window.gui`, `army_builder.gui`, `sell_location_action_view.gui`, `war_lateralview.gui` partially, `shared/abm_force_break_union.gui` is mod-only and fine, `select_war_to_intervene.gui` is a deliberate documented override - verified clean). The `hud_bot.gui` (`mapmode_slot_15..24` input actions) and `shared/textures.gui` (3 missing `.dds`) rows are **vanilla-side** and out of scope. Trap: `untyped is not a valid type` at `right_panel.gui:754` was a *consequence* of the malformed `use_global_input_instance` line, not a separate fault. **Release-build session #2 (2026-10-15 20:56).** Drift long over (same buildid 25905365); `error.1.log`+`error.log` = 4998 rows, only **91 mod-owned** (was 193), archived as `session_2015_release_build_all.csv`. Confirmed silent: B6d, B14, B17, B20 (all 0), B6e at 6 = exactly the `suceava`/`kursk`/`pasai` rows that are byte-identical to vanilla, B6c at 37 = the `kurultai`/`order_commandery` restrictions the user chose to keep. **B6e's fix confirmed**: the new town_setup templates removed 13 of 19. Three new items, all fixed: **B21b** 5 more tolerated-culture duplicates (`GOR` restored to vanilla's `slovene croatian`, `GRY`/`BLM`/`DIS`/`MSV` had mod-only blocks listing their own primary culture - my first scan missed them because it only read the mod's `in_game/setup/countries/*.txt`, while these tags' cultures come from vanilla files the mod does not ship; always merge vanilla then overlay the mod by filename); **B23** five broken character ids in the bookmark files, each a typo/short form of a real vanilla character (`epi_eudokia_doukania_kommene` -> `epi_eudokia_doukaina_komnene`, `ser_helena` -> `ser_helena_sratsimir`, `amg_mascarose_ii_lomagne` -> `amg_mascorose_ii_lomagne` (vanilla's own misspelling), `hhb_friedrich_iv_von_zollern` -> `hoh_friedrich_iv_von_zollern`, `for_cia_ubaldini` -> `fae_diletta_di_cunio`, the ruler's actual spouse); **B24** `16_wars.txt` made a truce participant of `PUN` (Punjab), which is only a vanilla *formable* with no country at start (vanilla loc keeps `PUN: "Punjab"`, and the mod's advances use it via `has_or_had_tag`, which is fine) - the line is commented out. Scan traps found on the way: characters can be declared at 2 tabs in `05_characters.txt` (a `^\t` regex reports `sav_humbert_i_savoy` as missing when it exists), and the `#was <old id>` comment idiom is the repo's non-1.4 fix marker. **Still open: B8/B18/B19** (12 mod GUI files) and the new **B22** - the mod's `main_menu/gui/messagetypes.txt` is a stale full override with **204 vanilla 1.4 entries missing** (1365 vs 1546), which is the sole cause of 57 `message_handler.cpp:434 Failed to find message type` rows; only 8 shared entries differ in body (6 `ESTATE_*` where vanilla commented out `sound=yes #TODO in ud010`, 2 `UNCONDITIONALSURRENDER_*` where vanilla has `sound=yes`), and the mod's own 23 `abm_*` entries plus its `INDEPENDANCE` category choice must be preserved.
- **1.4 error-log state (2026-10-15 22:24).** After the GUI ports (`union.gui`, `foreign_country_lateralview.gui`) and the `war_lateralview.gui` template restore, a whole session now logs **0 rows referencing any file the mod ships** (session #5, 22:18-22:24; session #4 had 8). Everything left is classified and deliberate: Cluster A `has invalid government type` (51 countries, 2,656 occurrences - see `data/ghost_countries.tsv`), the deliberately removed `middle_kingdom` IO family (~350), `Event target link 'international_organization' returned an unset ...` (684), `is_situation_active` (86), and vanilla's own missing assets (`gui/hud_bot.gui` `mapmode_slot_15..24` 10 rows, `gui/shared/textures.gui` 6 rows - the mod ships neither file). **Caveat:** `parse_abm_errors.py`'s "mod-owned" count is a *path* test, so it cannot see rows that name mod content without naming a file (Cluster A's `AB*` tags, the `middle_kingdom` keys).
- **1.4.6 (checked 2026-10-30).** Base `in_game/common` is unchanged from 1.4.4; only `flavor_MOR.txt`, `court_flavor_events.txt`, translations and `eu5.exe` changed. Across the Pillars (D009) patches 5 reforms and 4 laws by `INJECT:` and adds to on_actions, with no clash with the mod. The 1.4.4 base `10_countries.txt` already held 12 `has_dlc = "d009_across_the_pillars"` blocks (CAT, ANR, MLL, LON, GRA, NAV, MOR) that the regional files lacked; they are in now, marked `#1.4.6`. With the DLC, NAV starts with 3 reforms: base has 2, and the mod adds `sons_of_the_oak_reform`.
- **1.4.7 and 1.4.8 (2026-11-10).** Overrides brought up to game 1.4.8 (Steam testing branch `1.4.8-testing-branch`; the public branch is still 1.4.7). Text copies of both versions are in `E:/Modding/EU5/vanilla-game-1.4.7` and `-1.4.8`; no 1.4.6 copy exists (Steam lists no such branch). Tools in `.tools/scripts/game_update/`: `resync.js <old game> <new game> [--apply]` 3-way merges every same-path override; `resync_blocks.js` does `REPLACE:` blocks and the regional `10_countries_*` country blocks; `classify.js` estimates a missing old version from the 1.3.11 copy, git blame and markers. Lesson: "the mod still has 1.3.11 text" does NOT mean the game changed it (order-stronghold levies, `legalism = yes`, black `color2` are mod design), so without a base copy adopt only what a patch note confirms (`classify.js --adopt`). 1.4.7 was small: under the mod only `union.txt` (`join_visible_trigger = { always = no }`) and `battle_for_the_strait.txt` (`BFTS_ACTION_GRANADA_NOT_RECENTLY_VICTORIOUS`) changed. 1.4.8: 12 override files, 6 `REPLACE:` blocks, 3 country blocks (VEN, CRO, WAL) and Tabelbala pops. Kept the mod side: `guarantee.txt` expiry blocks (removed by the mod), the Maghreb levy rate (mod 2%; the game went 10% to 8% with its own `levy_maghrebi_tribal_cavalry_size`). Took both: Order Knights need `religion = religion:catholic` AND the mod condition. The mod Bedouin copies (`a_abm_early/late_bedouin_cavalry`) got the game mercenary fix (nobles x0.3 + tribesmen x0.01). All 296 prefixed entries still have targets in 1.4.8, and the one key 1.4.8 removed (`d009_gc_winning_war`) is not used. Players on 1.4.7 may log errors where the 1.4.8 copies use new script (`source_object`, new tooltip keys). 1.4.8 renamed every combat and movement define to a `_DAILY` and an `_HOURLY` pair (`ARMY_MOVEMENT_SPEED_DAILY/_HOURLY`, `LAND_STRENGTH_DAMAGE_MODIFIER_DAILY/_HOURLY`, `COMBAT_PHASE_DURATION_*`, `MINIMUM_COMBAT_DURATION_*` ...), so the mod's four (speed x0.75, land damage x2) are set under the old names for 1.4.7 and under both new names for 1.4.8, same multipliers. Not run in game. 1.4.8 also adds a Time Steps game rule (hourly ticks) and changes how reserves enter big battles (spread over sections, 125% of frontage a section): the battle simulator does not model that yet.
- **Message types moved to a folder in 1.4.4 (2026-10-28).** The 1.4.4 exe reads only `gui/messagetypes/` (sole path string left in `eu5.exe`; vanilla ships `gui/messagetypes/00_messagetypes.txt`, every file loads, later key wins). The old full-copy `main_menu/gui/messagetypes.txt` (B22) was silently ignored, so it is deleted; ABM types now live in `main_menu/gui/messagetypes/abm_messagetypes.txt` (additive, no vanilla copy), generated by `.tools/scripts/sync_messagetypes.py`. Do not recreate the old file.
- **Data files re-synced onto 1.4.4 (2026-10-28, branch `upgrade/1.4-patch-2`).** The first 1.4 pass (`upgrade/1.4-patch`) only patched what logged errors against an early 1.4.x; every same-name override and `REPLACE:` block was then rebuilt as *vanilla 1.4.4 + the mod's edits* (3-way merge against the 1.2.2 copies on the `vanilla-files` branch where the mod file was a full copy). Mod edits inside rebuilt blocks carry `# Ars Belli: vanilla X` comments. Deliberately NOT re-synced (design forks, keep as is): the formables `REPLACE:`s, `balance_the_budget`, `setup_colonial_nation`, `sco_civil_war`, `new_colonial_nation_founded`, the disabled `make_unit_available_for_hire` / `reform_taxation_system`, and the blanked Delhi / Timur / Middle Kingdom / Ireland / Ilkhanate / Red Turbans content. `setup/countries`: live tags' `color2` placeholder `rgb { 16 41 202 }` was replaced with 1.4.4 values; culture/religion of the 32 tags never touched since import was moved to 1.4.4 values too (the 1.4 start-state/pops/laws assume them: ATQ syriac Nestorian got stripped of its Islamic beylik laws at start); tags whose culture/religion a mod commit set keep the mod value. New 1.4 `type = pop` tags stay out: the mod removes ALL pop countries (0 live vs 453 in vanilla 1.4.4; commented blocks kept in every `10_countries_*_region.txt`), so e.g. the six Tuareg tags AHG AJJ IWL KAH KAY KGS that 1.4 split out of TRG were deliberately not added (user decision 2026-10-28). When the game updates again, redo it the same way: diff each file in `replaced_files.txt` against the new vanilla, not just the error log. Also compare file names case-insensitively: 1.4.4 lower-cased `advances/country_CHG/GLH/IRA/MGE.txt`, and the game treats a case-only difference as a separate file, so the mod copy stopped overriding (both loaded; the mod-removed `ira_islamized_persia` came back) until the mod files were renamed. **Unmarked does NOT mean stale (lesson of 2026-10-28):** many real mod edits carry no `# Ars Belli` comment, and the re-sync's "unmarked difference = stale vanilla" rule silently reverted several (ZMW advances widened to all Shona culture, the "no two hegemons" alliance check back, COMMON_RIVALS alliance/guarantee AI bonus back, offensive_vs_defensive AI biases + no-CB antagonism, coalition-wargoal release_cost, Calvinist art chance, map_OCC colour; restored on `fix/1.4-restore-mod-edits`). Next game update, classify each difference 3-way BEFORE re-basing: previous mod release vs PREVIOUS vanilla (text copy at `E:\Modding\EU5\vanilla-game-1.3.11`, make a new one before switching) vs new vanilla, with the file's original import (`vanilla-files` branch or first commit) as tie-breaker. A line in the mod but not in the previous vanilla is a mod edit even unmarked; keep it. A vanilla line the mod lacked only because its copy predates it is a balance change for live campaigns too, so list those for the user instead of adopting them silently.
- **CTD triage (2026-10-30, 1.3.11 + workshop v25).** `crashes/*/exception.txt` has no symbols (stack is 19x `eu5.exe (+0)`), so the address is the only fingerprint: **1.3.11 always dies at `0x144B0B0CC`** (2026-10-24 02:22 and 2026-10-30 22:22 - deterministic, same state/action), 1.4.3 at `0x147EA03DC` (2x), 1.4.5 at two different addresses. Crash stack (`meta.yml`) is always `InGameInterfaceIdler` (1.3) vs `GenerateFromBookmarkInterfaceIdler`-era load (1.4.5). **1.4.5 crashes (2026-10-28, DEV-ABM):** bookmark init at 1337.4.1, `Important assertion failed (Modifier update already in progress)` then CTD 5 s later, with **692x `Invalid culture from scope` / `culture` unset scope** in `common/laws/01_legal_system.txt:345` and 52 `real country <TAG> has invalid government type` (the `AB*` formable definitions + 1.4 latent tags `ALM ALV ATU BIJ CNR CRD HAW JAE JAP KAU LHN MAD MSL MUC MUI OAH ORK SEV TOL WGE`) - i.e. the ghost-country class from the 2026-10-14 entry, now provably reaching an engine assert. **1.3.11 gameplay crash:** last lines before the CTD are GUI-data spam (`BuildInLocationLateralView.GetBuildRankingTooltipTitle` "No context supplied" 38x, `Construction` datacontext `index 0 out of range [0,0]` 13x, `Could not push the provided stack context. ID: 0` 4x) - location/construction panel, no mod-file script error in sight. **Real 1.3 setup-data bugs (from the 2026-10-18 crash log, same v25 code):** `Country 'VND' is assigned as owner of location 'kannur'/'thalassery' but its already owned by 'KLT'`, `'udupi' by 'ALU'`, `TRE` vs `TDJ`/`HCI` (`akkus`, `terme`, `mesudiye`) - both fixed in 1.4 by `30f73e0`; and 18x `Location '<x>' has no culture/religion/raw material specified, but is given an owner` for the **wasteland/lake** locations `vembanad_lake` (lakes), `cardamom_hills`, `s_cardamom_hills`, `western_ghats_{karkala,kozhikode,siruvani}` (`hills_wasteland`) that `VND.own_control_core` lists - those are still owned in 1.4 and were removed on 2026-10-30 (empty `06_pops.txt` blocks prove they stay wasteland in 1.4 too; vanilla never gives them an owner). Same session also logs `Important assertion failed (Changing state before updating its owner. This will break modifier calculation logic.)` right after those duplicate-owner lines. **Noise that is NOT the crash:** 612 `Script system error` at load (357x `key 'middle_kingdom' does not lead to a unique international organization`, 130x unset event-target scope, `trigger_event ... not found` for the deleted `flavor_dlh/tim/chi`, `rise_of_timur`, `fall_of_delhi`, `crisis_of_the_chinese_dynasty`), the 162x `Failed to read key reference` (mod v25 still ships the namespace-less `events/imperial_examination_events.txt` - fixed in the repo), `Object of type 'country' is not valid for 'mp_rank'/'alliance_points_display'/...` (5x, `[GetPlayer.Custom()]` in `right_panel.gui`; `CL_tt` in vanilla uses the same idiom), missing Wwise `construction_building_*` events, and 0 script errors pointing at any file the mod ships.
- **Load-phase gotcha:** static modifiers are loaded ONLY from `main_menu/common/static_modifiers/` — a file under `in_game/common/static_modifiers/` is ignored (unknown-directory), so a `REPLACE:` there never applies. Likewise, scripted-trigger `custom_description` text keys (`text = key` inside a trigger's `custom_description`) must live in `main_menu/localization/english/`: scripted triggers are validated before `in_game` loc loads, else the log reports `No trigger loc`. Runtime-only loc keys can stay in `in_game/localization/english/`.

## GUI File Update Procedure (1.4 port; all files re-ported onto 1.4.4 on 2026-10-28)
The mod ships 17 `.gui` files under `in_game/gui/`. All but four are **full copies of the vanilla files of the version they were written against**, so shipping one REPLACES the whole vanilla file. Two consequences drive everything below:
* anything vanilla added to that file *after* the mod's copy was made is silently lost;
* any syntax the engine has since **removed** is still in the file and logs a parse error.

Mod-only files (no vanilla counterpart): `in_game/gui/shared/abm_force_break_union.gui`, `in_game/gui/shared/abm_focus_picks_panel.gui`, `in_game/gui/panels/organization/abm_player_coalition.gui`, `in_game/gui/select_war_to_intervene.gui`.

**Real mod content is always marked** — a comment starting with `# Ars Belli` / `# MP Rank`, or an `ABM_` loc key. An *unmarked* difference from the current vanilla file is almost always stale vanilla text, not mod content. `replaced_files.txt` lists every replaced file.

**Port recipe (proven on `context_menu.gui`, `alertmanager.gui`, `union.gui`):**
1. Copy the installed vanilla 1.4 file as the new base from `<steam>/steamapps/common/Europa Universalis V/game/in_game/gui/`.
2. Re-apply only the marked blocks, in the 1.4 idiom (list below).
3. Verify: brace balance 0, CRLF and the file's original BOM state, markers kept, and `diff` vs vanilla == exactly the re-applied blocks.
4. Update `replaced_files.txt` if the file stops being a full replacement.

**Per-file state and marked content:**

| file | state | marked content to re-apply |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `alertmanager.gui` | **ported 1.4.4** (== vanilla + 1 line) | Enforce Peace alert action |
| `context_menu.gui` | **ported 1.4.4** (== vanilla + 3 edits) | "Make available for hire" + Intervene-in-War exclusions |
| `panels/organization/union.gui` | **ported 1.4.4** (== vanilla + the button) | `# Ars Belli force_break_union button` |
| `war_lateralview.gui` | **ported 1.4.4** | Enforce Peace buttons (both sides) + `abm_enforce_peace_tooltip` template at file end, Intervene-in-War hidden |
| `single_unit_window.gui` | **ported 1.4.4** | "Make available for hire" exclusions (7 category filters) |
| `army_builder.gui` | **ported 1.4.4** | Become Mercenary button removed (`right_widget_visible` / `right_widget`) |
| `multi_unit_window.gui` | **ported 1.4.4** | "Make available for hire" + pin-check exclusions |
| `sell_location_action_view.gui` | vanilla unchanged since 1.3 (== vanilla 1.4.4 + ceiling) | flat 100-gold sale ceiling |
| `foreign_country_lateralview.gui` | **ported 1.4.4** | 3 blocks, below, + two-row layout (`size = { -1 70 }`, outer `vbox`) |
| `panels/right_panel/right_panel.gui` | **ported 1.4.4** | 6 blocks, below |
| `regiments_overview.gui` | **1.4.6** (== vanilla + 1 block) | `# Ars Belli: mercenary age badge` |
| `setup_mercenary_requirements.gui` | **1.4.6** (== vanilla + 1 block) | `# Ars Belli: mercenary age badge` |
| `shared/combat_tooltips.gui` | **1.4.6** (== vanilla + 6 blocks) | `# Ars Belli: mercenary age badge` |

**1.4 GUI idioms (what changed vs 1.2/1.3):**
* `action_button` / `action_button_diamond` are **gone** → `button` / `button_regular_diamond`, with the action moved into `scripted_action_tooltip = { click_type = left  click_mode = <single|confirm>  action_name = "..."  actor = "[...]" [parameter = {...}] }`.
* `left_click_and_hold_action` is **gone**; 1.4 has only `click_mode = single` (1341 uses) and `confirm` (259). Use `confirm` for destructive actions (`force_break_union`, surrender).
* the 1.2 `title =` label on an action button maps to 1.4 `text =`.
* `enabled = "[PdxGuiWidget.IsUberButtonEnabled]"` is the only such data function in 1.4 — `PdxGuiWidget.IsButtonEnabled` does **not** exist.
* `use_global_input_instance` was **removed** (vanilla 1.4 has none live).
* `ContextMenuActionEntry` → `targetted_button_regular` + `using = contextmenu_entry_button_inner_template` + `scripted_action_tooltip`.
* IO/union action lists are data-model driven: `datamodel = "[InternationalOrganizationsView.GetActions]"` auto-lists vanilla's `type = internationalorganization` actions. An ABM `type = owncountry` action (`force_break_union`) is **not** surfaced there and needs a hand-written button.
* When copying a `using = X` line from vanilla, confirm `X` is defined in the mod's copy of the file — a missing template logs an error (this bit `war_lateralview.gui`).
* `Not enough widget entries: Property widgets must specify exactly one widget` (`pdx_gui_validation.cpp`) reported at a `using = X` / `tooltipwidget = { using = X }` line means **`X` is undefined** — a missing template, not a malformed button. The log does not name the template.
* Before treating a log row as open, compare the mod file's **mtime** against the session span: EU5 logs the path and line number, never the content, so a row can already be fixed by an edit made after the game was run.

**right_panel.gui** mod additions (6 blocks):
0. **Campaign timeline button removed** — vanilla `button_timeline` (first child of the bar widget) commented out with an `# Ars Belli:` note; it overlapped the MP rank / Tier List block and the timeline is reachable from the decisions screen.
1. **Alliance/Defensive/Guarantee points display** — a `flowcontainer` with `# Ars Belli multiplayer limits display:` comment, inserted after the `non_clickable_color_gold_texture` corner icon, before the age `flowcontainer`.
2. **Remove `max_width = 200`** from the age name `text_single`.
3. **Country rank display (clickable)** — a `flowcontainer` with `# Ars Belli Current country Rank:` comment, inserted after the age tooltip block, before `### CORNER2`. Inner `button` with onclick → `mp_limits_toggle_tier_panel` scripted_gui.
4. **Tier list panel** — a `widget` with `# Ars Belli tier list panel` comment, inserted right after the rank flowcontainer, before `### CORNER2`. Toggled by global var `mp_tier_panel_open`. Contains 5 `dynamicgridbox` sections iterating `GetGlobalList('mp_<tier>_list')`.
5. **Focus picks panel** — `abm_focus_picks_panel = {}` with `# Ars Belli focus picks panel` comment, right before the tier list panel; plus a `# Ars Belli focus picks` "Focus Picks" button hbox inside the tier panel (above the alliance-alert toggle). The panel TYPE lives in generated `in_game/gui/shared/abm_focus_picks_panel.gui` (not a vanilla file, no re-merge). Client-side toggle `GetVariableSystem('abm_focus_panel_open')`. Advances listed via loc `[ShowAdvanceName('key')]` -> hover = real advance tooltip w/ effects. Picked marker: `GetPlayer.MakeScope.GetVariable('abm_focus_<branch>').IsSet`.

**foreign_country_lateralview.gui** mod additions (3 marked blocks):
1. **MP Rank and Power Score hbox** — `# MP Rank and Power Score` comment, after the country rank icon's `glow` block (around the `GetCountryRankIcon` section), inside the same parent container.
2. **Foreign Diplo Limits hbox** — `# Ars Belli Foreign Diplo Limits` (~line 1322). Shows the viewed country's AP/DP/GP usage and limits.
3. **Row 2: AI personality + relative power** — `# Ars Belli row 2: AI personality + relative power` (~line 1409); its wrapper ends with `} # end Ars Belli row 2 wrapper`.
   **1.4 fix already applied:** the stale `scroll_list_is_empty` / `scroll_list_is_empty_text` overrides on `diplomatic_relations_content` were dropped — vanilla 1.4 omits them here and `FOREIGN_COUNTRY_LIST_EMPTY` no longer resolves.

To identify mod blocks, search for comments starting with `# Ars Belli` or `# MP Rank`.

Last updated: 2026-10-31 (age-based mercenary rework documented in 3p: two unit-type files, hide/age rules, NOT-line map, badge hack).

## Important Files
- `README.md`: Basic mod title.
- `changes.txt`: High-level summary of mechanical changes (forts, combat).
- `.metadata/metadata.json`: Mod ID, version, and supported game version.

All files should have UTF-8 BOM encoding, especially localisation files

## Indentation Convention
- `.txt`/`.gui` script files use TABS (tab width 4). `.yml` = 1 space. `.md`/`.json`/`.ps1` = spaces.
- Enforced in `.vscode/settings.json`.
- Gotcha: the `paradox-highlight` extension's formatter reads TOP-LEVEL `editor.insertSpaces` (ignores language-scoped overrides). Top-level must be `false`, else formatOnSave silently rewrites tabs -> spaces.

## Language Association
- Repo uses EU5 syntax. `paradox-highlight` only auto-detects `eu5` under a game-named folder (Europa Universalis V/EU/EU5/3450310), which this repo is not, so it falls back to generic `paradox`.
- Fixed via `files.associations` in `.vscode/settings.json`: `in_game/**`, `loading_screen/**`, `main_menu/**` `*.txt`/`*.gui` -> `eu5`.

## Advances: INJECT instead of whole-file replacement (2026-11-11)
- **Two ways to override a vanilla advance.** `REPLACE:<id> = { ... }` swaps the whole entry (in a new file) and is what `abm_rough_terrain_advances.txt`, `abm_country_SCO.txt` and `abm_generic_horde_country.txt` do. Alternatively a mod file with the *vanilla filename* replaces the entire file, which is what `country_ILK/glh/ira/mge/zmw.txt` do - those must then be re-synced on every patch.
- **`INJECT:<id> = { ... }` is the lower-maintenance option** when only a few modifiers change: vanilla keeps owning the file, untouched advances keep flowing through patches, and the mod file holds only the deltas. Five `country_*` files were converted this way and their content folded into the matching **regional** advance file (no standalone per-tag override files - the regional file is the home for a region's vanilla-tag rebalances): `country_chg` -> `abm_central_asia.txt` ("Chagatai (CHG)"), `country_glh` -> `abm_steppes_ural.txt` ("Golden Horde (GLH)"), `country_ILK` -> `abm_persia.txt` ("Ilkhanate (ILK)", replacing the old "#Merged into the basegame file" placeholder), `country_ira` -> `abm_persia.txt` ("Persia (IRA)"), `country_mge` -> `abm_mongolia.txt` ("Mongol Empire (MGE)"). Those five files were deleted and removed from `replaced_files.txt`, as was `colonial_nations.txt` (a CRLF-only copy of vanilla - content-identical, so the whole file was redundant). Only `country_zmw` still replaces a whole vanilla advance file. `abm_persia.txt` is the Persia *region* idea-set import - the IRA tag override is `abm_iran.txt`.
- **INJECT appends, and repeated modifier keys ADD - so values must be written as DELTAS.** Vanilla `chg_border_wars` has `army_reinforce_efficiency = 0.2`; to land on 0.1 the inject carries `-0.10`, not `0.1`. Confirmed by vanilla/DLC precedent: `dlc/D009_across_the_pillars/.../d009_country_specific.txt` INJECTs `the_jewel_of_alandalus_reform` with two separate `country_modifier` blocks, i.e. blocks are appended, not replaced.
- **INJECT cannot remove a key**, so a *deletion* still needs `REPLACE:`. Likewise anything where you must re-specify `age`/`potential`/`icon` with different content: `INJECT` would append rather than swap. A *modifier-key swap* is the same case: `abm_golden_horde.txt` keeps `glh_legacy_of_jochi` as a `REPLACE:` because `army_light_cavalry_maintenance_cost_modifier -0.20` must disappear while `army_light_cavalry_reinforce_cost_modifier -0.15` takes its place; the file mixes one `REPLACE:` with two `INJECT:` deltas, which is the normal shape for these override files.
- **Retiring a vanilla advance in an add-on file.** There is no `DELETE:` keyword, so a removed advance becomes a `REPLACE:` whose `potential` is `always = no` (vanilla precedent: `building_types/event_only_buildings.txt` uses `country_potential = { always = no }`). The id stays defined - safe as long as nothing `requires` it - but never appears. Used for `ira_islamized_persia`.
- **Watch the sign on deltas.** A "nerf" is not always a negative delta: `town_upgrade_cost_modifier` goes `-0.25 -> -0.20`, so the inject carries `+0.05`.
- **A pre-existing duplicate to remember:** `ira_persian_plateau` is overridden by `abm_rough_terrain_advances.txt` (part of the 10-advance proximity-reform batch), NOT by `abm_iran.txt` - the old `country_ira.txt` carried a second, identical copy of the block, which is easy to reintroduce when splitting that file up. Check `abm_*` files for the id before adding an override.
- **`age` / `requires` cannot be INJECTed either** - they would append a second value rather than swap, so an advance that moves age or re-parents in the tree has to be a `REPLACE:` block (`abm_mongol_empire.txt` keeps 3 of its 5 overrides that way and INJECTs the other 2, which only touch modifiers).
- **A mod file that is content-identical to vanilla is pure liability**: it freezes the file against patches for zero gain. Always `diff` a same-named mod copy against the installed game before keeping it.
- **Corollary - don't stack INJECT onto a file the mod already REPLACEs.** A stray `INJECT:glh_tatar_traditions = { army_light_cavalry_power = -0.10 }` in `abm_steppes_ural.txt` survived alongside the `country_glh.txt` replacement and drove that advance to `0.0` (vanilla `0.2`); removed when Golden Horde moved to `abm_golden_horde.txt`. Grep every layer before adding an override.

## State religion / culture can no longer follow the ruler (2026-11-11)
- **What changed.** `change_primary_culture` no longer accepts the *ruler's* culture as a basis for switching (`ruler ?= { culture = root }` removed from its `enabled`; `dominant_culture` and `dominant_upper_class_culture` still work). `convert_religion` never offered the ruler's faith a special path - under the default `dynamic_religion` rule any enabled religion was selectable - so it now carries `not = { scope:actor.ruler_or_regent ?= { religion = root } }` in both `select_trigger.visible` and `enabled`.
- **Why whole-file copies.** `REPLACE:` is inert in `generic_actions` (see the `make_unit_available_for_hire.txt` note), so `in_game/common/generic_actions/culture.txt` and `general_religion.txt` are same-name full copies of vanilla with those lines edited. Both are registered in `replaced_files.txt` and must be re-synced on vanilla patches.
- **Scope semantics.** `root` inside `select_trigger.visible`/`enabled` is the **candidate** religion/culture, not the country - that is what makes `... ?= { religion = root }` read as "the candidate is the ruler's religion". Vanilla relies on the same convention (`not = { scope:actor.religion = root }`).
- The alternative lever, the `culture_conversions_rule` / `religion_conversions_rule` game rules (`dynamic_culture` / `dynamic_religion` defaults), is too blunt - it would disable the dominant-culture and pop-share paths as well.

## Start-state edits: units and named rulers (2026-11-11)
- **Where things live.** `in_game/setup/countries/<region>.txt` only carries colour/culture/religion. The 1337 bookmark is `main_menu/setup/1337/` (start date **1337.4.1**, see `main_menu/common/bookmarks/`), with one file per concern: `05_characters.txt` (`character_db`), `10_countries*.txt` (`countries`, incl. `government = { ruler = ... }`), `27_armies.txt` (`unit_manager`).
- **New wrapper blocks merge across files** - proven, not assumed: `countries` appears in `10_countries` + `25_area_preferences` + `26_ai_personalities`, `locations` in four files, `diplomacy_manager` in three, and `unit_manager` twice *inside* `27_armies.txt`. So a mod-only file (`05_characters_abm.txt`, `27_armies_abm.txt`) can add entries without copying the 105k-line vanilla character file. This is the same mechanism the mod's 62 `10_countries_<region>_region.txt` splits rely on.
- **Named ruler:** define the character in `character_db` (`first_name = { name = name_<x> }`, `culture`, `religion`, `estate`, `adm/dip/mil`, `birth_date`, `birth = <location>`, `dynasty`, `tag`) and point the country's `government = { ruler = <id> }` at it - works for republics too (vanilla `GEN`). Age comes only from `birth_date`; there is no effect to set a character's age afterwards (`add_adm`/`add_dip`/`add_mil` exist, set-birth-date does not).
- **Starting army:** add a `unit_manager = { army = { country = X location = <loc> sub_units = { <unit> = { strength = 1 } } } }` entry. `strength` is a 0-1 fraction of the unit's `max_strength`. Only 8 countries had armies in vanilla 1337 (the two starting wars) because armies are normally raised from pops - so a peacetime `unit_manager` army is how you hand a tag a standing force at start.
- **Gazaria (GAZ) start state:** `gaz_andrea_adorno` (ligurian/catholic, `adorno_dynasty`, born 1303.4.1 = 34 at start, adm 94 / dip 79 / mil 75, `birth = kaffa`) + two `a_horsemen` (the standard age-1 recruitable light cavalry; `a_age_1_traditions_light_cavalry` is a hidden template, `a_light_cavalry` is the empty base) at `kaffa`. Name is invented - no source specified one.
- **Galicia (GLC) start state (2026-11-11):** in `main_menu\setup\1337\10_countries_iberia_region.txt` GLC's `own_control_core` now also carries West Asturias (`oviedo aviles castropol tineo`), East Asturias (`villaviciosa cangas llanes mieres`) and Astorga (`astorga castrocalbon ponferrada villablino villafranca_bierzo`); the same tokens were cut from LON's `own_control_core`, which keeps `leon mansilla riano sahagun` plus Zamora/Salamanca/Caceres/Badajoz. Provinces come from `in_game/map_data/definitions.txt` (`west_asturias_province`, `east_asturias_province`, `astorga_province` inside `leon_area`); their `mountain_wasteland` locations (`west_cantabrian_mountains`, `ancares_range`, `leon_mountains`) stay unassigned, as in vanilla. The mod-added `oviedo` castle in `07_cities_and_buildings.txt` moved from `tag = LON` to `tag = GLC`.
- **Navarra (NAV) start state (2026-11-11):** `10_countries_iberia_region.txt` NAV `own_control_core` gained Cantabria (`santander laredo potes san_vicente_barquera`, i.e. `montana_province` - there is no `santander_province`), Burgos province (`burgos briviesca logrono miranda_de_ebro najera villarcayo`) and the four extra locations `agreda arnedo calahorra` (from `soria_province`) + `reinosa` (from `palencia_province`). Removed from CAS; `potes`/`san_vicente_barquera` removed from LON (its `#La Montana` line is gone). Wasteland `demanda_range` / `picos_de_europa` stay unassigned. Mod-added castles at `santander` and `burgos` retagged CAS -> NAV in `07_cities_and_buildings.txt`; vanilla church buildings there (cathedral/monastery/brethren_marsh) keep their vanilla `tag = CAS`, same as the Galicia cathedrals.
- **Scotland (SCO) integrated Ross + Moray (2026-11-11):** `10_countries_great_britain_region.txt` SCO gained `own_control_integrated = { dingwall gairloch ullapool #Ross  inverness urquhart elgin badenoch #Moray }` - the mod's first `own_control_integrated` block; the Ross tokens left SCO's `our_cores_conquered_by_others` (SBL's identical claim is unchanged). The 7 locations came out of ROS `own_control_core` (block removed; ROS keeps `skye` in `own_control_conquered`) and MOY `own_control_core` (Moray line removed; MOY keeps `lochaber ardtornish`). Capitals repointed to land the tags still own (MOY `inverness` -> `lochaber`, ROS `ullapool` -> `skye`) because a present country's capital must be its own. `loch_ness` (lakes, no culture/religion in `location_templates.txt`) and `fannichs`/`cairngorm` (mountain_wasteland) stay unassigned. Buildings in the transferred locations retagged MOY/ROS -> SCO: inverness castle + sergeantry/royal_court/royal_garden/library/chancery, elgin and dingwall `itinerant_court_seat`.

## Localisation file layout (2026-11-11)
- **One file per type, optionally per region:** `abm_<type>_<region>_l_english.yml`, e.g. `abm_advances_india_l_english.yml`. Region is omitted when the content is global (`abm_diplomacy`, `abm_buildings`). Never one file per feature - that had grown to 65 files with half of them under 20 keys.
- **Current set (37 files):** 15 `abm_advances_<region>`, `abm_units` / `abm_units_italy` / `abm_units_india`, `abm_buildings`, `abm_government`, `abm_religion`, `abm_diplomacy`, `abm_events_steppe` / `_japan` / `_italy`, `abm_decisions_steppe`, `abm_tooltips`, `abm_interface`, `abm_country_names`, `abm_rules`, `abm_messages`, `abm_modifiers`, `abm_focus_picks`, `abm_culture_group_china`, `00_mp_limits`, plus `replace/` (2).
- **Merged on 2026-11-11 (28 files absorbed):** units <- merc_unit_types + unit_types_misc + cultural_units; buildings <- + steppe_buildings + unique_buildings + town_rights; government <- gov_reforms + both estate_privileges + remove_head_of_cabinet; religion <- miaphysite + crusade; diplomacy <- 14 single-action files (economic_support, enforce_peace, both expel_*, trade_office_rights, lesser_infiltration, pay_for_access, vision_sharing, force_break_union, forgive_antagonism, player_coalition, tributaries, sell_location, sabotage_fort); events_steppe <- flavor_abtau + genoa_gazaria; events_japan <- events; events_italy <- pap_kingdom_of_god; decisions_steppe <- steppe_minerals_decisions + steppe_rgo_conversion + settle_the_steppe + pontic_steppe; tooltips <- scripted_tooltips + work_of_art; interface <- map_modes; advances_india <- wip_abm_f3_india_doab.
- Each merged block keeps a `# --- from <old file>_l_english.yml ---` line, and only the file's FIRST block has the `l_english:` header. Verified after the merge: 7,617 unique keys, **0 duplicates**, 0 unlocalised script tooltip keys, all files BOM + CRLF.
- **Full content audit (2026-11-11).** Every key was traced to the script file(s) that reference it and to the vanilla key namespace it belongs to. Result: the 33 `abm_*` files carry **36 files / 7,617 keys** with no type or region misfilings left. Two were fixed: `mandala_system` + `_desc` (a *vanilla government reform* re-text) moved out of `abm_advances_indochina` into `abm_government`, and `abm_culture_group_china` (88 Chinese culture-group advance keys) merged into `abm_advances_china`.
- **How to audit this again:** (1) map each key to the script files mentioning it (`in_game/**/*.txt`) and take the dominant directory -> expected *type*; (2) map vanilla key namespaces (`advances_l_english.yml`, `government_reforms_l_english.yml`, ...) -> expected type for re-texted vanilla ids; (3) for advances, take the region token from `abm_advances_<region>*.txt`. Compare both against the `<type>`/`<region>` in the loc filename. Beware the trap that bit the first pass: the advance *script* files subdivide regions differently from the loc files (`abm_advances_india_hindustan.txt` -> loc `abm_advances_india`), so deriving the expected loc name from the script filename directly produces ~2,000 false positives.

## Localisation health audit (2026-11-11)
- **The IDE's loc diagnostics are mostly noise.** The Paradox language server flags `MODIFIER_TYPE_NAME_*` as "doesn't exist in English" even though vanilla `modifier_types_l_english.yml` defines 2,758 of them, and flags scope commands (`[ROOT...]`, `[SCOPE...]`) as "does not exist in data type None" because a loc file cannot declare its scope - the scope comes from the `custom_tooltip`/`text` site. Do not chase those ~800 markers. `abm_focus_picks_l_english.yml` alone produces 564 of them.
- **The authoritative check is the engine log**, not the IDE: `[pdx_localize.cpp:257]: Key 'X' defined in multiple files: 'A', 'B'` in `logs/debug.log`. Last measured session had 2,131 such messages, 1,748 involving a mod file.
- **CONFIRMED IN PLAY (2026-11-11): `in_game/localization/` is NOT read as localisation.** The user reports exactly the missing tooltips predicted below. All 28 mod files were `git mv`-ed from `in_game/localization/english/` to `main_menu/localization/english/` (525 keys, no filename or key collisions) and the empty `in_game/localization/` tree removed. **The engine's own report is authoritative for what is still missing:** `Unknown loc key <key>` in `logs/error*.log`. That listed four keys; three were genuinely unlocalised and now live in `main_menu/localization/english/abm_scripted_tooltips_l_english.yml` (`gra_shadhili_order_unlocked_via_event_tt`, `variable_nav_appanage_rights_granted_tt`, `variable_gag_left_hre_guelph_victory_tt`); the fourth (`abm_area_has_no_other_copy_of_building_tt`) was already localised and only appears in older logs.
- **Indexing gotcha when checking for missing text:** vanilla event/decision loc lives in SUBFOLDERS (`main_menu/localization/english/events/DHE/*.yml`) and the BFTS/Andalusia keys live in `dlc/D009_.../main_menu/localization/english/`. A flat `english/*.yml` glob reports ~86 false "missing" keys; the real total after indexing `**/*english*.yml` under both the game root and `dlc/` is **0**.
- **Only one vanilla localisation directory is real:** vanilla keeps all 164,993 keys in `main_menu/localization/english/`; `in_game/localization/` holds nothing but `jomini/script_system/` trigger docs and **zero** game strings. So the mod's `in_game/localization/english/*.yml` files (525 keys exist ONLY there) are the highest-risk location in the project - if that folder is not read as loc, those strings are blank at runtime. Not yet proven either way; prefer `main_menu/localization/english/` for all new text.
- **Duplicates between two MOD files are the genuine breakage** - both are mod files, so load order between them is arbitrary. Fixed on 2026-11-11: `in_game/.../abm_buildings_l_english.yml` (all 10 keys superseded by the newer `main_menu` copy - file deleted), `abm_the_genoese_arsenal` in `abm_genoa_gazaria_l_english.yml` (stale "The Gazarian Arsenal" vs the advance's "Build the Gazarian Arsenal"), `abm_eastern_christian_militant_church*` and `abm_buddhist_monastery_stronghold_method*` (in_game copies dropped; the `_method` key is a `building_maintenance` production method, so "Monastery Stronghold Maintenance" is the right label). Cross-file duplicate loc keys across both loc dirs are now **0**.
- **Duplicates against VANILLA keys are intentional re-texts** (~1,700, largest `abm_advances_japan_l_english.yml` with 684 keys re-labelling vanilla advances, 184 with different text). Mod files load after the base game so they should win; the log will always list them.
- **`main_menu/common/named_colors/02_map.txt` had 14 out-of-range RGB values** (channel >255, e.g. `map_tsawi = rgb { 578 213 219 }`, `map_kuku_yalanji = rgb { 41 3 6268 }`) plus `map_PRM` and `map_tunica` defined twice. Clamped to 255 with the original kept as a `#was a b c` comment; duplicate `map_PRM` removed (identical value) and the misplaced `hsv` `map_tunica` removed (kept the `#Cultures` `rgb` one). No duplicate colour names remain.

## Advance file naming (2026-11-11)
- **No age/folder prefixes.** Advance files are named `abm_<region>.txt` (`abm_anatolia.txt`, `abm_india_bengal.txt`, `abm_africa_sahel.txt`). The older `abm_f<N>[-_]t<N>_<region>.txt` scheme was retired and all 33 files `git mv`-ed (history preserved as R100). Any new advance file must follow `abm_<region>.txt`.
- The prefixes encoded nothing the game reads - file names are irrelevant to EU5, which keys off advance ids - but they leaked into docs, scripts and generator `--output` examples. Everything that referenced them was updated: `.tools/scripts/*.py` (`remap_advances.py`, `update_china_localization.py`, `generate_colonial_nations_advances.py`, `generate_qoyunlu_advances.py`, `generate_advances_from_ideas.py`, `split_africa.py`, `split_middle_east.py`, `export_advances_json_csv.py`), `.tools/llm_instructions.md`, `.tools/eu5/ADVANCES_CSV.md`, `in_game/setup/countries/colonial_nations.txt` and this file.

## Advances: EU4 national ideas imported as EU5 advances (2026-11-08)
- **Qoyunlu sets (QAR / AQQ).** `.tools/scripts/generate_qoyunlu_advances.py` reads `QAR_ideas` / `AKK_ideas` from `.tools/eu4/ideas/00_country_ideas.txt` and writes `in_game/common/advances/abm_caucasus_qoyunlu.txt` (20 advances: 2 traditions + 7 ideas + ambition per set; BOM + CRLF). `--check` validates without writing; it also asserts every `requires` exists and is no later than the advance's age, and every `icon` exists in vanilla.
- **EU4 tag AKK is AQQ in EU5.** Advance ids keep the EU4 idea-set keys (`abm_akk_*`) so `generate_advance_loc.py` resolves EU4 loc 1:1, but `potential` uses `has_or_had_tag = AQQ`. QAR is QAR in both; both are live in `main_menu/setup/1337/10_countries_caucasus_region.txt`.
- **Modifier conversions** live in the script's `CONVERSIONS` table, each row copied from an earlier import (ME/CIR/SRV/CPA/Indonesia advances); raw EU4 lines stay in the file as `# eu4_modifier = value` comments. EU4 `yearly_government_power` has no EU5 modifier, so it expands to the five monthly government-power sources (legitimacy / republican tradition / devotion / horde unity / tribal cohesion) at 0.10 each.
- **QAR `in_honor_of_ali` is empty in EU4** (`#Same as Ajam`); the script's `IDEA_OVERRIDES` mirrors Ajam's `tolerance_heretic = 3` → EU5 `tolerance_heretic = 1`.
- **`generate_advance_loc.py --out` append mode double-CR bug fixed**: it opened the file with `newline="\r\n"` while writing explicit `\r\n`, producing `\r\r\n`. Now `newline=""`.
- Localisation for these advances is appended to `main_menu/localization/english/abm_advances_caucasus_l_english.yml` (append-only; existing keys skipped).
- **Universal importer `.tools/scripts/generate_advances_from_ideas.py`.** Generalizes the four idea-set importers: source is any EU4 idea file (`--ideas-file`, positional path, or directory), a spec list (`--set QAR_ideas=QAR`, or a file of such lines), or `--all`; output is the usual 10-advance ladder plus localisation (BOM + CRLF). It validates everything before writing (7 idea slots, 2 tradition effects, missing `CONVERSIONS` rows summarized per modifier, duplicate ids vs other mod files, `requires` existence + age, icons in vanilla) and keeps the target file's preamble / existing loc keys. Tradition advances are named singular (`<Country> Tradition I` / `Tradition II`) from the EU4 `<SET>_ideas_start` string, which is plural ("Traditions"); `generate_colonial_nations_advances.py` applies the same rule. Reproduces the Qoyunlu import byte-for-byte with `--preserve-values --idea-key-prefix always`; the default matches the Africa/ME/colonial fixed-value mapping with bare idea keys. `--force` writes ids that also exist in another mod file (moving an import), `--prune-loc` drops stale loc keys.
- **Gotcha: an advance's `requires` must be an advance the tag can actually research.** Imported sets repeatedly pointed `requires` at advances defined in `culture_*` / `country_*` / `religion_*` / `2_ship_unlocks` files that carry a `potential` gate (`fortified_towns` is `has_or_had_tag = BOH`; `a_modern_nation` is ETH; `cunning_diplomacy` is Wallachian; `unlock_baochuan` is Chinese culture group; `church_councils` is orthodox/miaphysite), which silently makes the dependent advance unresearchable. Audit any file with a brace-aware pass over vanilla `in_game/common/advances/`, mapping each `requires` to its defining file and flagging targets whose `potential` matches `has_or_had_tag|culture = culture:|culture = \{|religion = religion:|has_culture_group`. Replace with an ungated advance of the same or earlier age: `supply_depot_advance_age_3_discovery` for forts, `gunpowder_advance` for artillery, `organised_mercenary_payment` for military contracts, `religious_tradition` / `clergy_rights_laws_advance` for church, `national_sovereignty` / `nation_state_advance` for national identity, `coastal_trade_routes_advance` for trade. Ages must also never go forward (`requires` age index <= the advance's own).
- **`deep_match_eu4_eu5.py` is a modifier matcher only** — it produces `eu4_vs_eu5_advances*_merged.csv`, not advance script; the idea-set importers above are the ones that emit `.txt`.
- **`abm_german.txt` conversion pass (2026-11-13).** Audited all 210 blocks / 90 distinct EU4 modifiers against the hand-tuned `abm_<region>.txt` files + the 1.4.8 advance dump. Fixed: `papal_influence` -> `monthly_religious_influence = 0.10` (vanilla advances use it 26x and `monthly_papal_authority` 0x; the latter is the Pope's own IO resource) in MUN/MVA/TRI; duplicate key in `abm_tri_seamless_robe_of_jesus` (two `global_clergy_estate_power` lines 0.10 + 0.15 -> one 0.25); `prestige_decay` -0.005 -> -0.001 (vanilla only ever uses -0.001); `light_ship_cost -0.2` -0.10 -> -0.20 (7 region precedents keep the EU4 magnitude); `core_creation -0.2` 0.15 -> 0.20 (corpus norm); `administrative_efficiency 0.05` 0.05 -> 0.10; `fort_maintenance_modifier` flat 0.10 -> value-scaled 0.15/-0.15 and 0.20/-0.2; `tolerance_heathen 1` was 2; `fabricate_claims_cost` now also emits `spy_network_construction = 0.25` (FR/GB precedent). **Deliberately NOT changed** (German already matches the corpus or vanilla modal value): land_morale 0.10, global_manpower 0.10, monthly_army_tradition 0.05 (vanilla modal 35x), development_cost -> prosperity 0.001, discipline 0.05, hostile_attrition 1, monthly_prestige 0.10, tolerance_own 1, trade_range_modifier 0.10 (vanilla modal), merchants -> `global_merchant_capacity_modifier` (vanilla key, 43x), `naval_forcelimit_modifier` -> `expected_navy_size_modifier` (real country modifier, just unused by vanilla advances), `governing_capacity_modifier` -> `global_max_bureaucracy_slots`. These edits live only in the file: the importer still emits the old values, so regenerating `abm_german.txt` from the spec reverts them (same as every other hand-tuned region file).

The spec list is a session file, not in the repo; re-run with `--set` lines or recreate it. Every emitted modifier key was checked against `in_game/common/advances/*.txt` in the 1.4.8 install. IRE already carries vanilla `culture_irish.txt` advances; the new ids are all `abm_*` so nothing collides.
- **Western Europe + Iberia sets imported (2026-11-12).** Two batches with the universal importer, split per map region: `in_game/common/advances/abm_iberia.txt` (20 advances: `GAL_ideas=GLC`, `ASU_ideas=ATU` - **EU4 Asturias is tag `ASU`, EU5 Asturias is `ATU`**; EU4 `AST` is Astarac) + `abm_advances_iberia_l_english.yml` (40 keys). `western_europe` turned out to be too coarse a bucket (it is the EU5 **subcontinent** holding 9 regions: scandinavian, north_atlantic_islands, north_german, south_german, great_britain, ireland, france, iberia, italy), so the five sets were re-split by map region into `abm_france.txt` (30: BRI, BUR, PRO - all `france_region`; PRO's cuneo is the one `italy_region` location), `abm_great_britain.txt` (10: WLS) and `abm_german.txt` (10: LOT) with loc files of the same names; the first `abm_western_europe.txt` pair was deleted. **Lotharingia has no region of its own**: the `LOT_f` formable requires lorraine_area (`france_region`), wallonia/brabant/friesland/rhineland_area (`north_german_region`) and alsace_area (`south_german_region`), so it lives with the German group. **EU4 spells Burgundy's set `BUR_Ideas` with a capital I**, so the importer is now case-insensitive about set keys (`split_set_token` uses `.lower().endswith("_ideas")`, `derive_tag` strips `_ideas` with `re.IGNORECASE`, `resolve_specs` builds a `casefold()` map) - this also fixes the misleading "idea set(s) not found: BUR_Ideas_ideas" error, which now reports the original token. One more conversion row: `sunk_ship_morale_hit_recieved` (EU4's typo) -> `naval_morale_modifier = 0.10`. So the imported `abm_sil_*` blocks were hand-rewritten from `has_or_had_tag = SIL` to a culture gate copied from vanilla `culture_group_german.txt`: `OR = { culture = culture:silesian, culture = culture:german_silesian, + merged_culture_group_contains_culture variants }` — do the same rewrite if these blocks are ever regenerated (the block header comment says so). `EU4 MOR_ideas` is **Morocco, not Moravia**; `SLO_ideas` is neither. Verified: 3106 advance blocks repo-wide, 0 duplicate ids, braces balanced, every `abm_german` id has a loc key **LOC MOVED 2026-11-14** -> `abm_advances_german_l_english.yml`..
- **`abm_german.txt` region CSV (2026-11-13).** `.tools/scripts/export_advances_json_csv.py --region-csv` emits `tag/culture,tier,age,advance_name,modifier,eu4_ideas` (old 6-col schema unchanged by default; regression-diffed byte-identical). `tier` = formable `level` from `formable_countries`, 0 when the tag has no formable; the `tag =` inside a formable's `potential` must be ignored (depth-0 only) or `LOT_f` reads as `LOR`. `--formables` is repeatable, vanilla dir first then mod dir (same id -> later dir wins). Culture-gated blocks (SIL) label `silesian/german_silesian` and take the level of the formable whose `potential` names that culture (SIL_f, level 2). Output `.tools/parsed/abm_german.csv`: 210 rows, tiers WES/SWA/HAN/POM/SWI/BOH/SIL 2, LOT 3, GER/HRE 4, rest 0 (HES TRI LUX BRA LOR HSA MUN MAG MVA). No-BOM **CRLF** CSV, unquoted (writer line terminator CRLF, not LF as first written here). **`region` column + sort added by hand 2026-11-14 (user ask):** header now `tag/culture,region,tier,age,advance_name,modifier,eu4_ideas`, rows sorted region asc -> tier desc -> tag -> age. `region` = EU5 map region of homeland: 1337 tags from `main_menu/setup/1337/10_countries_<region>_region.txt` (TRI MUN MAG LUX HSA HES BRA north; SWI MVA BOH WUR south; LOR france), formable-only + SIL from formable `areas`/`regions` resolved via `in_game/map_data/definitions.txt` (WES POM HAN north; SWA FKN south; silesia_area baltic; GER/HRE north+south; LOT france+north+south, joined with `+`). Not script-emitted: `--region-csv` regeneration drops column + sort. Docs: `.tools/eu5/ADVANCES_CSV.md` region schema.

Hand fix: WBG tradition_2 `papal_influence` -> `monthly_religious_influence = 0.10` (German corpus convention; importer emits `monthly_papal_authority = 0.05`). Loc appended to the regional `abm_advances_german_l_english.yml` (BOM+CRLF; convention 2026-11-14: **loc goes in the regional advances loc file, no per-nation loc files**). Verified: 0 duplicate advance ids repo-wide (3203 blocks), braces balanced, every new id has a loc key, all modifier keys exist in `modifier_type_definitions`. **Franche-Comté (FCB) NOT imported: this EU4 copy has no Franche-Comté tag and no idea set for it** (grep of `.tools/eu4` for FCB/FCO/FRC/comtois/franche = 0 hits) - nothing to convert without inventing data. CSV regenerated + region/sort re-applied (240 rows; SHL north tier 2, FRI north tier 0, WBG south tier 0).

The four per-nation files `abm_advances_switzerland/bohemia/moravia/silesia_l_english.yml` were merged in (key-for-key, order: 17 original sets -> SWI -> BOH -> MVA -> SIL -> SHL -> FRI -> WBG) and deleted, and their stale `# Localisation:` block headers inside `abm_german.txt` now point at the regional file. Same rule for future imports: pass `--loc-output main_menu/localization/english/abm_advances_<region>_l_english.yml` (the importer appends keys, keeps existing ones).
## Big-realm restoration decisions + unique reforms (2026-11-09)
- **Pattern.** "Own/integrate >80% of a historic homeland, get a unique government reform (and often empire rank)" is implemented as an `in_game/common/generic_actions/` action (`type = owncountry`, `show_message = no`) plus a `unique = yes` reform in `in_game/common/government_reforms/abm_gov_reforms_europe.txt`. `generic_actions/` is EU5's decision system; the action appears in the country's action list as soon as its `potential` matches, and its loc keys are the bare action id + `_desc` (+ a custom `<id>_tt` for the requirement line). Actions need no `price` (vanilla `set_colonial_charter_goal` has none).
- **Unlocking a hidden reform.** The action runs `unlock_government_reform_effect = { type = <reform> }` (bare type, no `government_reform:` prefix) and then `add_reform = government_reform:<reform>`; the reform's `potential` is `has_unlocked_government_reform_trigger = { type = <reform> }`, so it only becomes selectable after the decision. `locked = { is_locked_mechanic = { mechanic = government_reform type = <reform> } }`, `years = 2`.
- **Rank bump.** `if = { limit = { country_rank_level < 4 } set_country_rank = country_rank:rank_empire }` — the guard stops an existing empire from being re-set. Vanilla precedent: `rise_of_timur.txt`, `flavor_BUL.txt`.
- **Counting ">80% cored of X".**
  - One region/area: `region:caucasus_region = { any_location_in_region = { owner ?= scope:actor  integration_level = core  count >= N } }` (vanilla: `flavor_decisions_eng.txt:712`).
  - A union of several areas: `any_owned_location = { integration_level = core  OR = { area = area:a  ... }  count >= N }` — `area = area:<x>` as a location-scope trigger with `any_owned_location` is vanilla (`advances/region_eastern_europe.txt:6`), and `count` combines with filters (`decisions/flavor_decisions_rus.txt:511`).
  - A whole region *and* nothing else: `region:X = { any_location_in_region = { ... } }`. Location-scope `region = region:X` also works (`original_capital ?= { region = region:caucasus_region }`, cf. `region_indonesia.txt`).
  - Always compute N from `in_game/map_data/definitions.txt` (continent > subcontinent > region > area > province > location nesting): `count >= int(locations*0.8)+1`. Current figures: `caucasus_region` 241 locations / 31 provinces / 5 areas; `levant_area` 81 / 12; `cilicia_area+cappadocia_area+armenian_highlands_area+armenia_area` 47+58+63+33 = 201.
- **Implemented so far.** `abm_koj_proclaim_kingdom_of_heaven` → `abm_koj_kingdom_of_heaven_reform` (KOJ, 65 cored Levant, also converts to theocracy + `military_order_reform`, `blocked_from_conversion = yes`); `abm_geo_greater_georgia` → `abm_geo_greater_georgia_reform` (GEO, 193 cored Caucasus); `abm_arm_greater_armenia` → `abm_arm_greater_armenia_reform` (ARM, 161 cored across the four areas + must own `location:yerevan` / `sis` / `sivas`); `abm_greater_caucasus` → `abm_greater_caucasus_reform` (any state bar GEO/ARM whose *original* capital is in the Caucasus and whose culture group is caucasian/iranian/turkic, 193 cored Caucasus, monarchy/theocracy/republic only overlap `allow` + reform `potential`). Loc for all three lives in `main_menu/localization/english/abm_gov_reforms_l_english.yml` (CRLF).
- **Government-power trio for multi-government reforms.** The five government types are `monarchy` / `republic` / `theocracy` / `steppe_horde` / `tribe`, each mapping `government_power` to one monthly modifier. A reform open to several types lists `monthly_legitimacy`, `monthly_devotion` and `monthly_republican_tradition` together (`abm_greater_caucasus_reform`) — only the one matching the current type applies.
- **Reform modifiers.** `global_crown_estate_power` = Crown Power, "percent, good" — positive raises crown power. EU5 has no generic "monthly government power" modifier; use the resource for the government type (`monthly_legitimacy` for monarchies, `monthly_devotion` / `monthly_republican_tradition` / `monthly_horde_unity` otherwise). `government_reform_slots = 1` is on 78 vanilla reforms and is the usual filler for `unique = yes` capstones.
## Tooling: Paradox Syntax Checker & Agent (2026-10-31)
- **Validator:** `~/.local/share/eu5-pdx-tools/check_pdx_syntax.py` (user-level, works on any EU5 workspace). `python3 <path> <file|dir> [...]`, `--quiet`, `--no-encoding`, `--no-duplicates`, `--max-report N`, `--self-test`. Catches unbalanced braces, unterminated strings, `,`/`;`, keys with no value, duplicate top-level defs per `common/<db>` folder, BOM/CRLF/space-indent drift, loc header/dup-key/`$VAR$`. Calibrated to **0 errors** on vanilla 1.4.4 (10215 files) and on this repo. **Run `--self-test` after any edit to it** — the script once shipped a half-applied refactor whose call site no longer matched its signature. NOT a schema checker — unknown keys/scopes still pass.
- **Agent:** user-level custom agent `~/.copilot/agents/eu5-paradox-syntax.agent.md` ("EU5 Paradox Syntax"). PreToolUse hook `~/.local/share/eu5-pdx-tools/block-git-write.py` denies `git commit`/`git push` (project rule: agent edits, human commits).
- **Facts verified while building it** (do not re-litigate without checking vanilla): `INJECT:` is real (vanilla DLC `D008`/`D009` use it, `REPLACE:` also). `rgb {}` / `hsv360 {}` are inline blocks with NO `=`. Repeating `key = value` inside a block is legal (OR-lists, multi-`ruler_term`) — never "de-duplicate". `common/defines/*.txt` is C-like `KEY = 1.0f;`, not script. `.gui` tolerates a trailing `;`; the script parser does not. Loc keys may contain `'` (`Abul'l-Hassan`). `common/genes` and `common/on_action` legitimately repeat their top-level keys across files. EU5 dot-paths (`root.ruler.dynasty`) and `save_scope_as`/`save_temporary_scope_as`, `while`, `switch = { trigger = X ... }`, `select_trigger`, `order_by`/`position`/`max` all confirmed present in 1.4.4.
- **Checked + dismissed (2026-10-31):** `abm_player_coalition.gui` 124 `}` vs 123 `{` is **inherited from vanilla** `panels/organization/coalition.gui`, which has exactly the same 123/124 profile and shape (5 top-level blocks + a trailing `}`); the GUI parser tolerates it, so leave it. `action_button_diamond` at 307/331/350 exists only in the pre-port commit `bac11b1b` (replaced with `button_regular_diamond` + `text =` in port `24e3c35b`), so the `gui.log` rows from 2026-10-31 01:36 are a stale run artifact, not an open bug. Grep the current file before trusting a log line.
- **Wales annexes its 8 vassals; Cornwall becomes an independent duchy (start state, 2026-11-13).** `10_countries_great_britain_region.txt`: **WLS** rebuilt as a single `own_control_core` of **29** locations = its 11 + the 18 held by GWR GMG MAC AUD BRO POS EWY PMB; its old 12-entry `our_cores_conquered_by_others` claim list dropped (Wales now owns all of it). All 25 ownable locations of `wales_area` are WLS-owned (the 4 wastelands `snowdon berwyn_range brecon_beacons cambrian_mountains` are unownable); the 4 extra tokens are the Marcher towns outside the area (`wigmore ludlow oswestry ewyas`). The 8 ex-vassals became dormant claimants (`own_control_core` -> `our_cores_conquered_by_others`, capital left on their own core land, MAC also keeping its denbigh/montgomery claim), and their 8 `dependency = { first = WLS ... }` lines in `12_diplomacy.txt` are commented out - a landless vassal of a landless-less overlord would otherwise still appear as a subject. **CRN** flipped from vanilla's dormant block to a landed `catholic_monarchy` duchy owning **18** locations = every ownable land location of `cornwall_province` (3; `scilly_isles` is `flatland_wasteland`), `devon_province` (6; `dartmoor` is `hills_wasteland`), `somerset_province` (5) and `dorset_province` (4); capital launceston, `ruler = random` (vanilla's own `catholic_monarchy_welsh_releasable` template uses it for landed releasables). ENG lost exactly those 18 from `own_control_core` (145 -> 130) and its 3-location cornwall `own_control_integrated` block; the block's original section comments (`# Earldom of Chester`, `# Welsh March`, `# Northumberland`, `# france`) were restored after the token rewrite. 15 buildings retagged ENG -> CRN in `07_cities_and_buildings.txt` (stannary_court x9, wells monastery + cathedral, exeter cathedral, okehampton eng_royal_forest, launceston/truro stockades). Note the buildings key is `tag =`, not `owner =`. Foreign-owned buildings are legal in vanilla (hanseatic_kontor by BRD in london, KNI commandery in edinburgh), so the ENG monastery left in WLS-owned flint is not an error. Validator: 0 errors on the three files. Ownership scan caveat: a naive land filter misses `wigmore` because its `location_templates.txt` line carries `modifier = ...` before `topography =`; and commented-out tokens inside a block (NOL's `#york scarborough ...`) get counted as owned unless comments are stripped first.
- **Cornwall + Man advance imports (2026-11-13).** EU4 tag traps: **Cornwall = `CRN_ideas`** (already the EU5 tag) and **Man = `KOI_ideas`, not `MAN_ideas`** - EU4 `MAN` is *Mantua* (`MAN_free_city`, `MAN_studiolo`), and EU5 `MNN` is the Isle of Man while EU5 `MAN` is Mantua, i.e. the two games swap the tags. Imported into the existing `abm_great_britain.txt` / `abm_advances_great_britain_l_english.yml` (10 advances each: `abm_crn_*`, `abm_koi_*` + `abm_mann_*`). **Spliced, not regenerated**: the committed WLS block was produced with `--preserve-values` under an older CONVERSIONS table (`num_possible_artists`, `global_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation`, `global_production_efficiency = medium_production_efficiency_bonus`, `research_speed_modifier = 0.10`, `stability_cost_efficiency = 0.20`); re-running the importer over the file silently rewrites those 8 lines. All four legacy keys are still valid vanilla EU5 modifiers, so the diff is scale/mapping drift, not a bug - the new sets were generated separately (`--force`, temp paths) and appended, and only the `# Source:` preamble line was edited by hand. New CONVERSIONS rows in `.tools/scripts/generate_advances_from_ideas.py`: `global_regiment_recruit_speed -> global_levy_recruitment_speed_modifier = 0.10` (EU4 recruit *speed*: -0.10 is the same 10% faster, so the sign flips; vanilla `faster_levy_recruitment` advance uses 0.2) and `capture_ship_chance -> ship_capture_chance = 0.10` (EU5 keeps the key verbatim, cf. vanilla `country_kni.txt` 0.05). Verified: 30 unique ids in the file, no clashes with other mod files or vanilla, 60 loc keys covering every id + `_desc`, all 20 emitted modifier keys exist in vanilla, and the checker reports 0 errors / 0 warnings on both files. Importer gotcha reconfirmed: its duplicate-id check scans the mod's *other* advance files, so a `--check` run with the default `--output` reports every id of the target file as a duplicate - always pass `--output` explicitly.

Appended 10 advances `abm_nol_*` to `abm_great_britain.txt` + 20 loc keys, same splice-in-place method as CRN/KOI (only the `# Source:` header line edited by hand). Vanilla EU5 has no NOL-specific advances.
- **Multi-modifier idea slots are legal and intended.** EU4's `nol_secure_percy_rule` lists five government-power keys (`legitimacy`/`devotion`/`republican_tradition`/`meritocracy`/`horde_unity`) because only the one matching your government form is live. The importer keeps **all** of them (`monthly_legitimacy`, `monthly_devotion`, `monthly_republican_tradition`, `monthly_tribal_cohesion`, `monthly_horde_unity` at 0.10) and that is correct for EU5 as well - 87 vanilla EU5 blocks pair 2-5 of these keys (e.g. `region_africa.txt land_of_the_ancestors` uses all five). Never 'clean up' such a slot to a single line.

- **Provence pops +25% (2026-11-13).** PRO owns 21 locations (3 cornwall-style irrelevant: `aix_en_provence arles brignoles marseille toulon tarascon apt barcelonnette forcalquier sisteron nice barjols castellane digne draguignan frejus grasse puget_theniers cuneo nimes uzes`; `avignon` is only a core on papal land and was left alone). Total was **625.857** pop units, so the budget was **+156.464**. Method: water-fill ("least populated first") - the 14 smallest locations were each raised to a common level **33.702**, the 7 largest untouched; new total 782.318 = exactly **1.250x**. Within a location every `define_pop` row was scaled by the same factor (composition, culture and religion mix preserved), rounded to 3 decimals with the residual pushed onto the largest row so each location lands exactly on the level. 36 lines changed in `main_menu/setup/1337/06_pops.txt`, nothing else touched. Artifact to know about: strict water-filling leaves 14 locations at *identical* pop (33.702) and multiplies `barcelonnette` by 6.0 (5.566 -> 33.702) - if a natural spread is wanted instead, weight the budget by `(max_pop - pop)` or by `1/pop` rather than filling to a level. `06_pops.txt` parser notes: one `\t<location> = {` block per location with `\t\tdefine_pop = { type = X size = N culture = C religion = R }` rows, no blank lines inside a block, block closes with a bare `\t}` (so a naive `^\t}` end-of-block test is safe); the file is BOM-less CRLF and stays that way (the checker's 1 warning is that missing BOM, pre-existing). Pop types live in `in_game/common/pop_types/00_default.txt`; `has_cap = yes` only on nobles, and there is no hard per-location pop cap - overshoot is a balance question, not a syntax one.

Custom tags: definition goes in `in_game/setup/countries/<region>.txt` (`color`, `culture_definition`, `religion_definition`), loc in `main_menu/localization/english/abm_country_names_l_english.yml` (`TAG`, `TAG_ADJ`, `TAG_f: "$TAG$"`), 5-letter tags are fine (ABSEL, ABNUB...), and a missing `flag_definition`/CoA is harmless - only 266 vanilla tags have one; MVA/BIA/HSC have none and fall back to a generated flag. - **ZAZ cossack tribes along the rivers (2026-11-15).** Added **74.998** pop units of `type = tribesmen culture = cossack_culture religion = orthodox` (~75k people) to 13 of Zaporozhia's 35 locations. **How "has a river" was determined (there is no script-side river data in EU5):** `in_game/map_data/rivers.png` is a 16384x8192 palette PNG where index **255 = white = no river** and every other index is a water pixel (indices 2-11 are a cyan->dark-blue gradient = river sizes, 12-15 greens, 0/1 green/red). `in_game/map_data/locations.png` is the same geometry, and `in_game/map_data/named_locations/00_default.txt` maps `location = rrggbb` - that is the key to decode it (there is NO colour column in `definitions.txt`). Intersect the two images band by band (512 rows at a time to stay inside memory: full RGB is 402 MB) and each location is ~700-1800 px, so a river crossing it shows up as 4-90 river pixels (1-9% of the tile). Calibration sanity check: kyiv 7.5%, kremenchuk 4.8%, cherkasy 3.8% (all on the Dnieper) vs kryvyi_rih/poltava/zhytomyr 0.0% (small rivers not drawn at all - `rivers.png` only carries rivers big enough to render). Result: adalaga 89 px, sloviansk 58, kremenchuk 46, kichkas 43, chuhuiv 40, rozdory 25, kodak 23, zmiiv 23, myshuryn_rih 20, oril 17, samarchyk 17, usivka 5, mykytyn_rih 4. Budget split by `sqrt(pixels)` so the Dnieper towns get 7-11 and the marginal ones 2-5. **Where a location already had a `tribesmen`/`cossack_culture`/`orthodox` row the size was increased in place** (10 of the 13 - never add a second row with identical type/culture/religion); the other 3 (kremenchuk, chuhuiv, rozdory) got a new row. Values were deliberately kept non-round (10.983, 9.991, 10.607, 8.361, 7.508, 7.471, 7.447, 6.953, 6.843, 6.028, 5.473, 5.271, 4.635, 5.117, 2.439, 2.210) and any that landed on a trailing zero were nudged, so no line reads like `5.000` or `10.600`. `06_pops.txt` parser gotcha that bit me twice: location headers may carry a trailing comment (`\tkichkas = { #ZAZ`), so `^\t(\w+) = \{$` silently skips those blocks - anchor on `^\t(\w+) = \{` only. Validator: 0 errors, braces 81197/81197, BOM-less CRLF preserved (109806 CRLF, 0 bare LF).
- **ZAZ levy surge + cossack levy law (2026-11-15).** Two pieces: (1) a **timed static modifier** `abm_zaz_levy_surge` (`main_menu/common/static_modifiers/abm_country_changes.txt`) with `nobles_estate_levy_size = 1.0` and `tribes_estate_levy_size = 1.0` (+100% each), applied in the ZAZ block via `timed_modifier = { modifier = "abm_zaz_levy_surge" start_date = 1300.1.1 date = 9999.1.1 size = 1 }` so it is already live at the 1337 start and open-ended; (2) removal when the Black Death reaches ZAZ. New `in_game/common/on_action/abm_zaz_levy_on_actions.txt` adds `monthly_country_pulse = { on_actions = { abm_zaz_levy_surge_check } }`, whose trigger is `tag = ZAZ` + `has_country_modifier = abm_zaz_levy_surge` + `country_has_disease = disease:bubonic_plague` and whose effect is `remove_country_modifier = abm_zaz_levy_surge`. **Why that works:** `timed_modifier` in the setup only takes `modifier`/`start_date`/`date`/`size` - there is no end-trigger field - so the end has to be scripted. `country_has_disease = disease:X` localises as "X is present in [COUNTRY]" (verified in `trigger_localization/disease_outbreak_triggers.txt` + `triggers_l_english.yml:7468`), i.e. anywhere in the country's locations. There is **no** plague-related on_action hook in `_hardcoded.txt` (148 hooks, none disease-related) and disease files' `on_spread_to_country` is an effect block, not a hook, so a monthly pulse is the only way in. Duplicate `on_action` keys from several files **merge** (vanilla defines `on_ending_war` in both `_hardcoded.txt` and `religious_order_conquest.txt`), and the mod already runs `monthly_country_pulse` in `abm_mp_limits_on_actions.txt`, so no vanilla pulses are lost. `great_pestilence` is a different disease - only `bubonic_plague` (the Black Death situation's disease) removes the modifier. The check is monthly, so it lapses at most a month after the first infected ZAZ location, and it never returns. **Laws:** vanilla `medieval_levy_law` (the one holding `noble_levies` +50% nobles levy and `all_cultures` +20% wrong-culture levy) is `law_gov_group = monarchy` with `potential = { government_type = government_type:monarchy }`, and ZAZ is a republic - it can never see it. So a new law `in_game/common/laws/abm_cossack_levy_law.txt` (`law_category = military`, `law_gov_group = republic`, `potential = { has_reform = government_reform:abm_cossack_hetmanate_reform }`) carries both options verbatim with mod-unique ids **`abm_noble_levies`** and **`abm_all_cultures`** (option ids are DB keys, so they cannot reuse the vanilla ids), and ZAZ starts on `abm_noble_levies` via `laws = { abm_cossack_levy_law = abm_noble_levies }`. Only one option can be active at a time - if both effects are ever wanted simultaneously and permanently, put `nobles_estate_levy_size = 0.5` + `wrong_culture_levy_size = 0.2` in the `abm_cossack_hetmanate_reform` country_modifier instead of a law. Loc added to `abm_government_l_english.yml`. Checker: 0 errors across `in_game/common/laws`, `in_game/common/on_action`, `main_menu/common/static_modifiers` and the modified setup file.
