- **ZAZ's mystery light infantry and the start-units cleanup (2026-11-15, third round).** The user reported ZAZ still starting with one extra **light infantry** regiment next to the three light horse. Verified that no script can be its source: `main_menu/setup/1337/27_armies_abm.txt` is the only ZAZ unit block (three `a_horsemen` at kichkas), vanilla `27_armies.txt` has no ZAZ entry (its `army`/`levy` blocks cover only VEN/VER/PAD/MLO/MAN/FER/MOR/TLE because those are the 1337 wars), no setup template carries units, and the whole mod contains exactly three unit-creating effects - the CAS fleet start action, `generic_actions/battle_for_the_strait.txt` and `events/DHE/abm_flavor_chb.txt`. So the extra regiment is engine-side. The user's own ZAZ autosave (1338.4.1, `autosave_c6335f8f...`) shows the composition: ZAZ subunits are `a_tribal_cavalry` x3 + `a_feudal_levy` x4 (all LEVY - they carry a `levies={...}` list) plus **one regular `a_archers`** (`number=1`, no `levies` key), which is the age-1 light infantry (`2_unlocked_through_tech.txt:32`, `default = yes`). **Fix:** a start-state cleanup rather than a source fix - `abm_zaz_light_cavalry_only` (`in_game/common/scripted_effects/abm_start_units_effects.txt`) runs `every_country_sub_unit = { limit = { NOT = { sub_unit_type = unit_type:a_horsemen } } destroy_subunit = yes }`; vanilla idioms are `sub_unit_type = unit_type:X` and `every_country_sub_unit` (`dlc/D009_across_the_pillars/in_game/common/decisions/cas_decisions.txt:30`) and `destroy_subunit = yes` (`in_game/common/scripted_effects/unit_effects.txt:39`). It is called from both start hooks (inside `c:ZAZ = { ... }`) and from a `monthly_country_pulse` entry whose guard sits **inside the effect** (`if = { limit = { tag = ZAZ current_date < 1337.6.1 } ... }`) rather than in an on_action `trigger`: `monthly_country_pulse` runs for every country, so a trigger the engine ignored would have deleted every country's infantry. The date test uses `current_date` - the vanilla key (`diseases/bubonic_plague.txt:39`, `scripted_triggers/game_triggers.txt:59`); a bare `date < x` is NOT the trigger name. The window closes on 1337.6.1 so later recruitment is untouched. The fleet code moved from `abm_start_fleets_on_actions.txt` (deleted) into `abm_start_units_on_actions.txt` (`abm_start_units_game_start`), which now holds both start blocks. A `debug_log` breadcrumb sits inside the limited block, so `logs/debug.log` shows one line per removed subunit - if the next run shows the extra regiment still there with no breadcrumb, the unit is not a subunit at all. Remaining unknown: whether the engine hands *every* country one default light-infantry regiment (GAZ's two-horse setup army was not checked by the user). Validator: 0 errors on both new files.
- **Hansa becomes a landed country and absorbs Lubeck, Hamburg and Bremen (2026-11-15).** HSA was a **building country**: `10_countries_german_north_region.txt` had `type = building` (the enum is location / pop / building / army / navy, see the `country_type` trigger doc), `capital = lubeck` was owned by **LUB**, and LUB/HAM/BRM were its `hanseatic_member` subjects (vanilla `subject_types/hanseatic_member.txt`, `can_be_annexed = no`, `visible = { tag = HSA }`). Now: HSA has **`type = location`** and `own_control_core = { lubeck hamburg bremen }`; the three cities became dormant claimants (`own_control_core` -> `our_cores_conquered_by_others`, capitals untouched) and their three `dependency` lines are commented out in `12_diplomacy.txt`. LUB owned only two buildings (`dock`, `city_walls` at lubeck) - both retagged to HSA; the market at lubeck is location-based (`03_markets.txt: add_market = lubeck`) so it follows the owner automatically. **Two runtime rules had to be adjusted or the change would silently gut the Hansa:** (1) the reform `hanseatic_merchant_federation` gates on `country_type = building` in both `potential` and `locked` (vanilla `government_reforms/republic.txt:263`), and losing it would drop `allows_hanseatic_federation_buildings` and with it every `hanseatic_kontor` (their `country_potential` is that modifier) - so `REPLACE:hanseatic_merchant_federation` was added to `abm_gov_reforms_europe.txt` with `potential`/`locked` = `has_or_had_tag = HSA` and the modifier block copied verbatim; (2) the mod's `peace_treaties/dissolve_hanseatic_league.txt` fired `destroy_building_country = scope:loser` on `c:HSA`, so its `potential` now also requires `scope:loser = { country_type = building }`. **Known leftovers to watch on the next launch:** the template law `administrative_system = decentralized_trade_federations` (vanilla `laws/01_common.txt:940`) also requires `country_type = building`, so the game will strip it at start ("Removing invalid law") - left as is rather than copying the 400-line `administrative_system` law to change one option; `cultural_traditions_law = maritime_society` is safe (its potential is `OR = { has_or_had_tag = HSA ... }`); the kontors inside lubeck/hamburg/bremen are `is_foreign = yes` but that flag only *enables* foreign placement (building_types/readme.txt:7), so they are expected to survive; HSA's country culture is `hanseatic` (`in_game/setup/countries/north_germany.txt`) while those cities are holsatian/low german. The setup part needs only a new campaign, but the reform and peace-treaty overrides are `in_game` script, so **a full relaunch** is required. Validator: 0 errors on all five files.
- **Location ids = 1-based line numbers in `in_game/map_data/location_templates.txt` (verified 2026-11-15).** The user quotes locations as in-game ids (847, 857, ...). Resolution method: cross-checked against the 1338 autosave - navy `location=7184` is line 7184 `levantine_sea2` (`inland_sea`), its `last_port=6765` is line 6765 `mut` (land), an army `location=14162` is `shadodkot` (land), and ZAZ levy subunits with `home=4559` / `4560` are `kichkas` / `rozodry`. `sed -n "${id}p" location_templates.txt` gives the name. So ids 847-1056 are Holstein/Lower-Elbe locations (847 heiligenhafen, 848 ratzeburg, 849 eutin, 857 pinneberg, 1005 osterholz, 1007 delmenhorst, 1017 hoya, 1020 harburg, 1046 winsen, 1056 achim).
- **Hansa's second expansion: ten Holstein / Lower Elbe locations (2026-11-15).** HSA `own_control_core` (13 locations now) gained `heiligenhafen ratzeburg eutin pinneberg` + `osterholz achim delmenhorst hoya harburg winsen`, taken from **HPL** (keeps kiel plon), **RZB** (loses its only location), **LBB** (only location), **HSP** (only location), **BRE** (keeps stade neuhaus gestenthorpe bremervorde zeven), **OLD** (keeps oldenburg elsfleth), **HOY** (only location) and **LUN** (keeps 9). Every donor keeps its **cores** in `our_cores_conquered_by_others`; RZB/LBB/HSP/HOY therefore became dormant claimants with their `capital =` left on the ceded city (Irish/Italian precedent). No buildings, markets, wars, sieges or diplomacy had to change: none of the ten locations holds a building of any kind (`location = <loc>` appears nowhere in `07_cities_and_buildings.txt`), none of the donors is anyone's subject (BRE is only an overlord - DIT stays its vassal), and no war/truce mentions them. Note the ownership scanner gotcha found here: this file has lines like `\t\t}\t\tcountry_rank = rank_county`, so a line-based parser silently swallows the next country - parse by tokens (`key = {` / `}`) instead. Validator: 0 errors; ownership scan shows each of the ten owned by HSA only.
- **Hansa's 13 locations double their population (2026-11-15).** `main_menu/setup/1337/06_pops.txt`: every `define_pop` `size` inside HSA's 13 location blocks was **multiplied by 2** in place - 42 entries (lubeck 9, hamburg 5, bremen 5, heiligenhafen 2, ratzeburg 4, eutin 4, pinneberg 1, osterholz 2, achim 2, delmenhorst 1, hoya 3, harburg 2, winsen 2). Total went **209.151k -> 418.302k (~418,302 people)**. Sizes are always written with exactly 3 decimals and `x*2` never needs more, so a plain `f"{v*2:.3f}"` per line is lossless. The file is **ASCII, CRLF, no BOM** like the whole vanilla `main_menu/setup/**` tree - the checker's `missing UTF-8 BOM` warning is pre-existing there and must NOT be "fixed" by adding a BOM (that is the `in_game`/localisation convention, not the setup one). Recompute totals with a token walk that tracks `<location> = {` / `}` and sums `size`.
- **Flanders scaled up to 773k pops, Hansa scaled down to 379k (2026-11-15).** Same file (`main_menu/setup/1337/06_pops.txt`), proportional scaling: every `define_pop` `size` in the country's locations multiplied by a single factor. Flanders (FLA, 15 `own_control_core` locations - cassel dunkirk oudenaarde ypres veurne ostend bruges hulst ghent sint_niklaas aalst kortriik lille douai tournai) **636.283k -> 773.000k** = factor **1.214868**; Hansa (13 locations, at that moment 418.302k after the earlier doubling) **418.302k -> 379.000k** = factor **0.906044**, i.e. each HSA entry is now ~1.812x its pre-doubling vanilla value. 94 entries rewritten (52 FLA, 42 HSA).
  - Method for exact totals: scale each entry with `round(v*factor,3)`, then absorb the accumulated rounding residual (FLA -0.002k, HSA +0.002k) on the **single largest entry** of the country (bruges peasants 101.127, hamburg peasants 53.114). Only that one entry per country deviates by more than 1 person; all others are within 0.001k of the exact proportional value. Tiny entries (0.006k nobles at 3 decimals) cannot follow the factor exactly - expected, not a bug.
  - FLA's `own_control_core` list wraps its closing `}` with 8 **spaces** instead of a tab (`10_countries_german_north_region.txt:` the `lille douai tournai` line is followed by `        }`) - vanilla quirk in that file, left alone.

Kill `.1200` too only if the user asks for the whole storyline, not just the disaster.
  - Needs a **full process relaunch** (`in_game/**` is read at launch only). `error.log` should then have zero `common/disasters/turmoil_in_brandenburg.txt:14` lines - cheap in-game confirmation.
- **`.tools/scripts/pops.py` - the pop counting / rescaling tool (2026-11-15).** One stdlib-only CLI for everything we had been doing by hand: `count` (with `--group-by location|province|area|region|tag|culture|culture_group|religion|religion_group|pop_type`), `list` (per location), `scale` (multiplier or exact target, proportional) and `check` (audit). Every selector works on both subcommands: `--tag`, `--location` (name **or numeric in-game id**), `--province`, `--area`, `--region`, `--culture`, `--culture-group`, `--religion`, `--religion-group`, `--pop-type`; comma lists work (`--tag HSA,FLA`, `--location 847,1020`).
  - Data it reads: `main_menu/setup/1337/06_pops.txt` (`--pops`), `10_countries*.txt` for ownership (`--setup`), `<game>/in_game/common/cultures/*.txt`, `common/religions/*.txt`, `map_data/definitions.txt` (province/area/region), `map_data/location_templates.txt` (numeric ids). `--game`/`--mod` override the defaults; it fails loudly if the game folder is missing instead of guessing.
  - **Parser lesson (bug found and fixed while building it):** a bare-word list loses its *last* word if the closing `}` is not handled - `culture_groups = { german_group }` and `province = { a b c }` both silently dropped an entry (it showed up as 1518 of 2087 cultures having no culture group, and every province missing one location). Any future Paradox block parser must append the pending bare word on `}`. After the fix: 2064/2087 cultures have a group (23 really have none in vanilla), 27284 locations mapped to province/area/region.
  - `scale` is **byte-safe**: it rewrites only the `size` token of the `define_pop` lines it touches, keeps CRLF/tabs/no-BOM, leaves unchanged entries as their original text (so `--multiplier 1` and `x2` followed by `x0.5` are **byte-identical** round trips), refuses to run without a scope unless `--all` is passed, refuses negative sizes, and re-reads the file after writing to verify the new total. `--residual largest` (default) absorbs the 3-decimal rounding leftover on the biggest entry so targets land exactly (how HSA hit 379.000k and FLA 773.000k). `--dry-run`, `--backup`, `--verbose` (largest changes), `--format table|csv|tsv|json` (csv/tsv/json print *only* the data, no header lines).
  - Sanity values to compare against after future edits: **HSA 379.000k / 42 entries / 13 locations**, **FLA 773.000k / 52 / 15**, world 348915.619k / 52623 entries / 20827 locations with pops, 12269 owned locations over 1599 tags. `pops.py check` returns OK on a clean tree.
- **Brandenburg pops +25% (2026-11-15).** `pops.py scale --tag BRA --multiplier 1.25`: BRA's 27 owned locations (80 pop entries) went **424.092k -> 530.118k** (~530,118 people; exact +25% would be 530.115k, the +3 people are per-entry 3-decimal rounding). 73 of 80 `define_pop` lines changed, all inside the `size` token; the 7 that did not move are one-person entries (0.001k -> 0.001k), and every entry off the factor by more than 1% is under 0.05k, i.e. rounding of a handful of people. First real use of the new `pops.py` (see the tool entry above).

  - Hard city requirement goes in `allow` as `owns = location:X`, NOT in a `locations = { }` list: `required_locations_fraction` scales **every** required location, so with 0.7 a bare `locations` list would let you skip one of the three. Vanilla `KOA_f` (formable_countries:1018) is the exact precedent: level 3 + areas + `owns = location:tunis/tripoli` in allow.

Colour, tag, name, flags and `allow` untouched.

So as specced the Teutonic Order, the historical founder, is excluded, as is Stettin. Adding `pruthenian` (and `markish`) to the OR would restore both - left out on purpose until the user asks, since the culture list was explicit.

Under that: **BRA** owns nothing in `prussia_area` -> hidden; **TEU** owns plenty of prussia_area but its primary culture is **pruthenian**, which is `baltic_group`, not `german_group` -> hidden. That reproduces both symptoms exactly; the error log has **zero** `abm_formables_germany` lines, so nothing in the new file is rejected. Fix is a full relaunch (exit the exe and start it again), not an edit.

Added `culture = culture:pruthenian # Ars Belli: the Teutonic Order's Baltic Prussians`, so the culture OR is now prussian + pruthenian + eastern_pomeranian + western_pomeranian + brandenburgish. TEU's capital malbork is in prussia_area so it also passes the capital OR.

Restored the line; content now verified five cultures + three areas + level 3 + 0.7, md5 stable across repeat reads, checker clean. Keep an eye on this when editing the same file by hand.

Format is `# Prussia` on the line above `REPLACE:PRU_f = {`.

Both excluded. Everything else checked out by name (CLI=Cilli, MBZ=Bregenz, ROT=Rothenburg, GOS=Goslar, SOR=Lusatia, RVA=Ravensburg, TNT=Trent, HSA=Hansa, ...).
  - `OGK` is the legacy "old German Kingdom" tag (ruler history only, no land) whose set is `HLR_ideas`, but the advances sit on `HRE`; `SIL` (Silesia) has `SIL_ideas` imported yet `silesia_area` maps to `baltic_region`, so it is out of scope for a north/south-Germany query. 203 German-region tags have no EU4 idea set at all (minors, prince-bishoprics, Hanseatic towns).
- **Every German formable now requires an original capital in the German lands (2026-11-15).** `abm_formables_germany.txt`: 17 of the 19 blocks got an `original_capital.region` gate inside `potential` - **north**: SAX NSA MKL SHL WES HAN NED BEL (`region:north_german_region`); **south**: BAV HAB BOH SWA FKN SWI (`region:south_german_region`); **both, as an OR**: GER HRE LOT (Lotharingia is 4 north + 1 south + 1 French area). 43 added lines, 0 deletions.
  - **PRU_f and POM_f deliberately skipped**: both already carry a tighter area-based capital gate from earlier sessions (`brandenburg/pomerania/prussia_area` and `mecklenburg/pomerania_area`). For PRU a north-region gate would be actively wrong - `prussia_area` is in `baltic_region`, so it would exclude Königsberg/Malbork and stop the Teutonic Order from forming Prussia, which was the whole point of adding `pruthenian` earlier.
  - Only these 19 mod formables are in the German regions; nothing in the other `abm_formables_*.txt` files resolves to north/south_german_region. 144 start tags have their capital in north_german_region, 103 in south.
- **New `BRA_f` formable that nobody can form (2026-11-15).** Appended to `abm_formables_germany.txt`: vanilla has **no** `BRA_f` (Brandenburg exists at start), so this is a new id, not a REPLACE. Written with the repo's established unformable idiom (`potential = { always = no }` + empty `allow`, same as the disabled Irish/Indian formables in `abm_formables_british_isles.txt` / `abm_formables_india.txt`): level 3, `areas = { brandenburg_area }`, `tag = BRA`, `color = map_BRA` (named_colors/02_map.txt:245), `adjective = BRA_ADJ`. File now has 20 blocks.
  - Side effect to remember: anything keyed off a tag's formable `level` (e.g. the `.tools/scripts/export_advances_json_csv.py` tier column) now reads BRA as tier 3 instead of 0.
- **Great Moravia advances imported - EU4 `GMA_ideas` -> EU5 tag `GMA` (2026-11-15).** 10 advances appended to `abm_german.txt` (240 -> **250 blocks**; the file also ends with the commented-out HAB set, hence 260 `abm_` matches) + 20 loc keys in `abm_advances_german_l_english.yml`. Source: EU4 wind-of-change tag `GMA` = "Great Moravia" (`00_country_ideas.txt:18302`), ideas: reforge Moravia's hegemony, apostles to the Slavs, princes of Nitra, rebuild Veligrad, Rex Sclavorum, canonize Cyril & Methodius, Svatopluk's legacy.
  - **EU5 has NO `GMA` tag** - no formable, no country name, no flag definition (`main_menu/common/flag_definitions/00_flag_definitions.txt` has PRU but not GMA) and `braz_por` etc. never mention it; the only EU5 text about Great Moravia is SLO_f's description. The advances are therefore **inert** (`has_or_had_tag = GMA` never matches) until the tag exists. Options if wanted: re-tag to `MVA` (Moravia - but it already has MVA_ideas, so Moravia would have 20), or create a `GMA_f` formable (needs a COA/flag, which is why I did not invent one).
  - Five EU4 modifiers had no conversion row; used, with the precedents cited: `overextension_impact_modifier -> stability_cost_efficiency = 0.20` (same as the India Central import), `build_time -> global_construction_speed = 0.10` (FRI row), `monarch_admin_power -> monthly_legitimacy = 0.10` (no monarch power in EU5), `max_absolutism` and `max_absolutism_effect -> global_crown_estate_power = 0.10` (crown power is the EU5 absolutism analogue; 40 vanilla advances use it).
  - Same append-only method as before (generate to temp, append blocks + loc, never regenerate the file). diff vs the pre-write copy: 144 added lines, 1 removed line (only the `# Source:` preamble). Checker clean.
- **"The Crown of Great Moravia" decision + reform, and the GMA advances wired to it (2026-11-15).** New files: `in_game/common/decisions/abm_great_moravia_decision.txt`, `main_menu/localization/english/abm_great_moravia_l_english.yml`. Touched: `abm_gov_reforms_europe.txt` (new reform), `main_menu/common/named_colors/02_map.txt` (`map_GMA`), `abm_german.txt` (10 GMA potentials).
  - **Decision `abm_crown_of_great_moravia_decision`** (`decision_category = country_specific_decisions`, `only_once = yes`): `potential` = OR of `culture = culture:czech/moravian/silesian` AND OR of `original_capital.area = area:moravia_area/silesia_area/bohemia_area`, plus `NOT has_reform` of the reform. `allow` = `at_war = no` + the seven areas at 70%.
  - **70% is expressed with vanilla's iterator counter**, not a formable-style fraction (decisions have no `required_locations_fraction`): `bohemia_area = { any_location_in_area = { is_ownable = yes owner ?= root count > N } }`, the exact shape of vanilla `mallorca_decisions.txt:15-26`. Ownable counts (no water in these areas) are bohemia 40, moravia 16, slovakia 49, silesia 50, upper_saxony 49, lesser_poland 40, austria (= **Lower Austria**) 18, so the thresholds are 27/11/34/34/34/27/12 as `count > N`. That is 70% of **each** area; a union-based 70% (184 of 262) is not expressible with `count` and would need the hidden-formable trick.
  - **Effects**: `set_country_rank_effect = { rank = country_rank:rank_empire }`, `add_reform = government_reform:abm_crown_of_great_moravia_reform`, `change_country_color = map_GMA` (`map_GMA = rgb { 215 17 32 }`, read from the installed EU4 `common/countries/Greatmoravia.txt`).
  - **Reform `abm_crown_of_great_moravia_reform`**: granted-only pattern copied from vanilla `legatus_natus` - `locked = { is_locked_mechanic = { mechanic = government_reform type = ... } }` + `potential = { has_reform = <self> }` so it never shows in the reform list, `age = age_1_traditions`, `major = no`, `unique = yes`; modifier = `monthly_legitimacy 0.10`, `global_crown_estate_power 0.10`, `global_monthly_control 0.0005`.
  - **The 10 `abm_gma_*` advances** now read `potential = { OR = { has_or_had_tag = GMA has_reform = government_reform:abm_crown_of_great_moravia_reform } }`, so the decision actually unlocks them without needing a GMA tag (which EU5 does not have). Loc adds `GMA`/`GMA_ADJ` for the tooltip names.
  - NOTE: `owner ?= root` counts direct ownership only (same as the vanilla Mallorca decision); land held by subjects does not count, unlike formables' `owns_or_non_sovereign_subject_owns`.
- **Great Moravia decision: coring effect + `area:` scope fix (2026-11-15).** Two changes to `abm_great_moravia_decision.txt`: (1) the allow block's seven area scopes now carry the `area:` prefix (`area:bohemia_area = { any_location_in_area = ... }`) - vanilla always scopes areas that way (`mallorca_decisions.txt`), an unprefixed `${AREA}_area = {` key is not guaranteed to resolve; (2) the effect cores every location of the seven areas: `area:X = { every_location_in_area = { limit = { is_ownable = yes NOT = { is_core_of = root } } add_core = root } }`, the shape from `scripted_effects/international_organization_effects.txt:1802` plus vanilla's `add_core = ROOT` guard from `00_formable_countries.txt:1116`. 7 blocks, 262 ownable locations covered (incl. foreign-held ones, which become core claims).
  - If only owned land should be cored instead, add `owner ?= root` to the limit.
- **Great Moravia coring limited to owned land (2026-11-15, user clarification).** Added `owner ?= root` to the limit of all seven coring blocks, so only locations in the seven areas that the country actually owns get a core; foreign-held ones are ignored (`potential`/`allow` were already ownership-based, so nothing else changed). Condition order inside the limit: `is_ownable = yes` -> `owner ?= root` -> `NOT = { is_core_of = root }`.
- **Hansa gets the same treatment: `abm_hanseatic_federation_decision` grants the Hanseatic Merchant Federation (2026-11-15).** New files: `in_game/common/decisions/abm_hanseatic_federation_decision.txt`, `main_menu/localization/english/abm_hanseatic_federation_l_english.yml`. Touched: `REPLACE:hanseatic_merchant_federation` in `abm_gov_reforms_europe.txt`, 10 HSA advances in `abm_german.txt`.
  - **Requirements**: `owns = location:lubeck/bremen/hamburg` (all three, outright) plus 70% of the ownable locations per scope - `area:mecklenburg_area` 11/15, `area:holstein_area` 10/13, and the province definitions `luneburger_heide` 7/9 (Luneburg Heath), `aller` 4/5 (**Middle Aller**), `middle_weser` 3/4 (**Middle Weser**), `wesermarch` 5/7 (**Weser Marshes**), `elbmarch` 5/6 (**Elbe Marshes** = Hamburg's province, since there is no `hamburg_province`).
  - **Province scoping**: these names come from `map_data/definitions.txt`, so they are **`province_definition:X_province`** scopes iterated with `any_location_in_province_definition` / `every_location_in_province_definition` (vanilla `peace_treaties/scaligeri_peace_treaties.txt:127` and `building_types/unique_buildings.txt:2650`), not `province:` - that is a different object.
  - **Effect**: `set_country_rank_effect = { rank = country_rank:rank_kingdom }`, `add_reform = government_reform:hanseatic_merchant_federation`, then the same owned-only coring as Great Moravia (`is_ownable = yes` + `owner ?= root` + `NOT is_core_of`) over the 2 areas and 5 province definitions.
  - **Reform made decision-only**: `potential = { has_reform = <self> }` + `locked = { is_locked_mechanic = { mechanic = government_reform type = hanseatic_merchant_federation } }` (vanilla legatus_natus pattern) so it can no longer be picked from the reform list, and **`government = republic` was removed** so a monarchy that takes the decision keeps the reform (the game drops reforms whose government/potential fail). HSA still starts with it and keeps it.
  - The 10 `abm_hsa_*` advances now read `OR = { has_or_had_tag = HSA has_reform = government_reform:hanseatic_merchant_federation }`, so the reform unlocks them for any taker.
- **Hansa got a never-formable formable stub `HSA_f` (2026-11-15).** Appended to `in_game/common/formable_countries/abm_formables_germany.txt` (21st block, after `BRA_f`): `level = 2` + `required_locations_fraction = 1.0` + `rule = historical` + `potential = { always = no }` + `allow = { }`, `name/flag/adjective/tag = HSA/HSA/HSA_ADJ/HSA`, `color = map_hansa`, `areas = { mecklenburg_area holstein_area }`. Same precedent as `BRA_f` and vanilla's own `always = no` entries (`00_formable_countries.txt:1902,3745`).
  - **`level` is the rank level**: `rank_county = 1`, `rank_duchy = 2`, `rank_kingdom = 3`, `rank_empire = 4` (`in_game/common/country_ranks/00_default.txt:160,139,85,33`). So "rank 2" = `level = 2` = duchy, matching HSA's vanilla start `country_rank = rank_duchy`.
  - **Hansa's map colour is `map_hansa`, not `map_HSA`** (`main_menu/common/named_colors/02_map.txt:171`, lowercase). The mod's copy already carries it clamped to `rgb { 255 32 32 } #was 256 32 32`.
  - **`flag = HSA` needs no `flag_definitions` entry**: HSA has no block there, but its CoA is pre-scripted (`coat_of_arms/coat_of_arms/pre_scripted_countries.txt:17319`), and vanilla `SWI_f` does the same (`flag = SWI`, SWI also pre-scripted-only).
  - No `REPLACE:` prefix - vanilla ships no `HSA_f`, so this is a new id (same as `BRA_f`).
- **Dalmatian advances opened to Venice (2026-11-15).** New file `in_game/common/advances/abm_dalmatia.txt`: 12 `REPLACE:` blocks copied verbatim from vanilla `common/advances/culture_dalmatian.txt` - the 6 `dalmatian_*` culture-gated ones and the 6 `dal_*` DAL-tag ones. Only the `potential` blocks differ; a script diffed every block back against vanilla to prove head/tail are byte-identical.
  - New union: `OR = { has_or_had_tag = DAL | culture = culture:dalmatian | culture = { merged_culture_group_contains_culture = culture:dalmatian } | AND = { has_or_had_tag = VEN  has_reform = government_reform:signoria_of_venice  OR = { culture = / has_accepted_culture = / has_tolerated_culture = culture:dalmatian } } }`. Ragusa's `rag_*` advances are untouched.
  - **Venice has dalmatian as a TOLERATED culture, not accepted** (`10_countries_italy_region.txt:3026 tolerated_cultures = { dalmatian }`), and at 1337 it holds `council_of_forty`/`council_of_ten`/`merchant_republic` - `signoria_of_venice` (`government_reforms/country_specific.txt:2689`, `unique = yes`, `government = republic`) is unlocked later by the VEN DHE / `establish_the_signoria_of_venice` parliament issue. So the gate is: form or adopt the Signoria first, and the tolerated branch is what actually makes it fire.
  - Triggers used are vanilla-proven: `has_accepted_culture = culture:X` (`advances/culture_group_german.txt:474`), `has_reform = government_reform:X` (`advances/beyliks.txt:9`), `has_tolerated_culture` (`trigger_localization/country_triggers.txt:2783`, used in `estates/00_default.txt`). There is also a combined `has_primary_or_accepted_or_tolerated_culture` trigger, but its only script usage takes a scope (`gui/filters/16_culture.txt:47`), so the explicit OR was preferred.
  - `REPLACE:` is proven for the advances database: `abm_balkans.txt:1 REPLACE:defense_of_the_faith` targets vanilla `advances/country_kni.txt:1`.
  - Vanilla `advances/region_balkans.txt` also lists `culture = culture:dalmatian` in its Balkan-region sets, but those are regional (capital in balkan/carpathia region) and were deliberately left alone.
- **Venice's Dalmatian possession: decision + permanent modifier as an extra advance gate (2026-11-15).** New `in_game/common/decisions/abm_dalmatia_decision.txt` (`abm_secure_dalmatia_decision`): `potential = { has_or_had_tag = VEN  NOT = { has_country_modifier = abm_secured_dalmatia } }`, `allow = { at_war = no  province_definition:north_dalmatia_province / south_dalmatia_province = { NOT = { any_location_in_province_definition = { is_ownable = yes  NOT = { owner ?= root } } } } }`, effect `add_country_modifier = { modifier = abm_secured_dalmatia months = -1 mode = add }`. Loc: `main_menu/localization/english/abm_dalmatia_l_english.yml`.
  - **Dalmatia is not an area** - it is the two province definitions `north_dalmatia_province` (knin novigrad sibenik sinj zadar + `dinaric_alps1` wasteland) and `south_dalmatia_province` (dubrovnik brac makarska metkovic lastovo_island_wasteland slano split), both inside `croatia_area` (`map_data/definitions.txt:1806-1817`). `krk`/`pag` sit in `lika_province`, so they are NOT part of Dalmatia. `lastovo_island_wasteland` is an ownable location with an empty pops block (`06_pops.txt:25938`); `dinaric_alps1` is `topography = mountain_wasteland` - both filtered by `is_ownable = yes`.
  - **100% ownership idiom**: `NOT = { any_location_in_province_definition = { is_ownable = yes  NOT = { owner ?= root } } }`. Vanilla precedents: `NOT = { any_location_in_area = { owner ?= root } }` (`events/expeditions/western_ocean_voyage_events.txt:94`), `NOT = { regent ?= scope:actor.ruler }` (`country_interactions/lordship_of_ireland.txt:205`), `owner ?= root` (`decisions/canal_passage.txt:7`).
  - **Permanent country modifier**: `abm_secured_dalmatia` appended to `main_menu/common/static_modifiers/abm_country_changes.txt` (`game_data = { category = country }` + `global_monthly_control 0.005` / `navy_galley_power 0.10` / `trade_sea_efficiency 0.05` - all three verified as static-modifier keys). Granted with the vanilla permanent idiom `months = -1 mode = add`; a duration would silently switch the advances back off. Loc keys `STATIC_MODIFIER_NAME_`/`STATIC_MODIFIER_DESC_abm_secured_dalmatia` in `abm_modifiers_l_english.yml`, usable as `$STATIC_MODIFIER_NAME_abm_secured_dalmatia$`.
  - **The 12 Dalmatian `REPLACE:` blocks now live in `in_game/common/advances/abm_italy.txt` (from line ~505), not in a separate file** - the user folded `abm_dalmatia.txt` into it between turns. Do not recreate that file. Their Venetian branch is now `AND = { has_or_had_tag = VEN  has_reform = government_reform:signoria_of_venice  has_country_modifier = abm_secured_dalmatia  OR = { culture = / has_accepted_culture = / has_tolerated_culture = culture:dalmatian } }` - i.e. signoria AND the conquest modifier AND the culture.
- **CORRECTION (2026-11-15): `has_tolerated_culture` removed from the Dalmatian advances - it is redundant.** `has_accepted_culture` already counts a tolerated culture, so the 12 `AND = { ... OR = { culture = / has_accepted_culture = / has_tolerated_culture = culture:dalmatian } }` branches in `in_game/common/advances/abm_italy.txt` are back to `OR = { culture = culture:dalmatian  has_accepted_culture = culture:dalmatian }`. Supersedes the two earlier notes in this file that said the tolerated branch was needed for Venice's 1337 start (`tolerated_cultures = { dalmatian }`). Supporting evidence: only 2 vanilla files mention `has_tolerated_culture` at all (`estates/00_default.txt`, `trigger_localization/country_triggers.txt`) and vanilla never ORs it with `has_accepted_culture` in a condition.
- **Venice formable stub `VEN_f` (2026-11-15).** Appended to `in_game/common/formable_countries/abm_formables_italy.txt` (after the commented-out Kingdom of God block): `level = 2` + `required_locations_fraction = 1.0` + `rule = historical` + `potential = { always = no }` + `allow = { }`, `tag/name/flag/adjective = VEN/VEN/VEN/VEN_ADJ`, `color = map_venice`, `areas = { veneto_area }`. Same never-formable pattern as `BRA_f` / `HSA_f`.
  - `map_venice = rgb { 54 167 156 }` (`main_menu/common/named_colors/02_map.txt:168`; `map_venetian` is the same colour at :58). There is no `map_VEN`. VEN does have a real `flag_definitions` entry (`00_flag_definitions.txt:10550`) as well as a pre-scripted CoA, and `VEN_ADJ: "Venetian"` exists.
  - `venice` sits in `venice_province` inside `veneto_area` (`map_data/definitions.txt:1222`). Vanilla ships no `VEN_f`, so no `REPLACE:` prefix (new id).
  - `abm_formables_italy.txt` has no trailing newline after its last commented line; appends must start with CRLF.
- **EU4 `TUS_ideas` imported as advances (2026-11-16).** Generated with `.tools/scripts/generate_advances_from_ideas.py --set TUS_ideas=TUS` (validation passed, incl. icons/requires against the checkout), then spliced by hand into `in_game/common/advances/abm_italy.txt` **before `REPLACE:genoese_galley`** (~line 422) under a `# TUS_ideas — Tuscany (TUS)` header - writing the importer's `--output` straight at that file would have truncated everything after the first block (`read_preamble` stops at the first `name = {`). Loc appended in `abm_advances_italy_l_english.yml` under a matching header. 10 advances: 2 traditions (age 1), birthplace_of_the_renaissance + tuscan_condottieri (age 2), tuscan_banking + tuscan_arts (age 3), porto_franco + tuscan_administrative_reforms (age 4), tuscan_grand_duchy (age 5), ambition (age 6).
  - **Florence = `FLO` in EU5, not EU4's `LAN`.** EU4's `TUS_ideas` trigger is `tag = TUS OR tag = LAN #Florence`. EU5 TUS is not on the map in 1337 (it is only a formable, `TUS_f`), so every potential was hand-edited to `OR = { has_or_had_tag = TUS  has_or_had_tag = FLO }` or the set would have been unreachable until someone formed Tuscany. FLO had no advances before this.
  - `abm_tus_ideas_tradition_2` carries `global_clergy_estate_power` twice (0.15 from `church_power_modifier`, 0.10 from `monthly_fervor_increase`) - the importer maps both to that modifier. Leaving it: duplicate modifier keys are already the established pattern in the mod (`abm_ala_navy` has two `global_sailors_modifier`).
  - `abm_porto_franco` keeps the `abm_` prefix so it cannot collide with vanilla Ragusa's `rag_the_ragusan_porto_franco`.
  - `abm_italy.txt` has **no** `# Generated by` / `# Source:` header line (unlike abm_german.txt); its per-set convention is `# <SET> — <Name> (<TAG>)`, and blocks are separated by one blank line.

  - **`reform_progress_growth` has no EU5 conversion** - the only gap in both sets. Used `--conversions` JSON `{"reform_progress_growth": ["legislative_efficiency = 0.10"]}` (temporary file, not committed). Precedent for a different mapping exists in `abm_india_west.txt:1079` (`clergy_estate_target_satisfaction`).
  - **`SIC_ideas` → TWS, and OR'd with `SIC`**: EU4's trigger is `tag = TTS OR tag = SIC`; EU5 TWS is only a formable while **SIC exists at 1337** (`10_countries_italy_region.txt`, owns palermo/messina/...). NAP is deliberately not included - EU4 Naples had `NAP_ideas`, a separate set.

So three imported titles collided with a vanilla advance the same tag can see; they were renamed in loc only: `abm_porto_franco` → "Porto Franco of Livorno", `abm_sic_constitions_of_melfi` → "Melfi Reforms", `abm_sic_sicilian_silk` → "Sicilian Silk Trade". Check this on every future import - compare the new titles against advances gated on the same tag.
  - `abm_italy.txt` now holds 81 top-level blocks: SPI, latin, PAP, TUS, DAL, SIC, then the REPLACE:/INJECT: overrides, then the Dalmatian vanilla replacements.
- **Tuscany raised to a tier 2 formable (2026-11-16).** `REPLACE:TUS_f` added to `in_game/common/formable_countries/abm_formables_italy.txt` (~line 281, between `REPLACE:TWS_f` and the commented Kingdom of God): vanilla block copied verbatim with only `level = 1` -> `level = 2` (and the `REPLACE:` prefix). A diff against vanilla `00_formable_countries.txt:4519` shows exactly two differing lines.
  - **No `form_effect` rank line was added on purpose**: 106 of the vanilla formables at level >= 2 have an empty `form_effect` (GBR_f, SCA_f, NED_f, ARA_f ...) and the engine derives the rank from `level`, so `level = 2` alone makes the result a duchy (`country_ranks/00_default.txt:139 rank_duchy level = 2`). The 26 formables that do script `set_country_rank_effect` are the exception, not the rule.
  - Vanilla ships exactly one `TUS_f` (no DLC copy), so `REPLACE:` is the right prefix. `map_TUS` is a vanilla named colour.
  - `abm_formables_italy.txt` now has 5 live entries: ITA_f, DAL_f, TWS_f, TUS_f, VEN_f.
- **`abm_secured_dalmatia` converted from a static country modifier into a Commoners estate privilege (2026-11-16).** The static modifier block was deleted from `main_menu/common/static_modifiers/abm_country_changes.txt` and re-created as a privilege in `in_game/common/estate_privileges/abm_peasants_estate.txt` with the same three effects (`global_monthly_control 0.005`, `navy_galley_power 0.10`, `trade_sea_efficiency 0.05`). Loc moved from `abm_modifiers_l_english.yml` (`STATIC_MODIFIER_NAME_/DESC_`) to `abm_government_l_english.yml` (bare `abm_secured_dalmatia` / `_desc`).
  - **"Commoners" is EU5's `peasants_estate`** (`estate_l_english.yml:8 peasants_estate: "Commoners"`); there is no `commoners_estate`. Privilege files are per estate: burghers, clergy, cossacks, dhimmi, nobles, peasants, tribes.
  - **Grant-only pattern**: `potential = { has_variable = abm_secured_dalmatia }` - vanilla's own way of hiding event-granted privileges (`austere_monastic_life`, `commons_petition`). The decision therefore does `set_variable = { name = abm_secured_dalmatia value = 1 }` **before** `grant_estate_privilege = estate_privilege:abm_secured_dalmatia`, or the potential would still be false at grant time.
  - **`can_revoke = { always = no }`** (vanilla precedent `nobles_estate.txt:1208`, `burghers_estate.txt:577`): the decision is `only_once` and the Dalmatian advances are gated on the privilege, so revoking it would permanently lose both.
  - **All 12 Dalmatian advance gates re-pointed**: `has_country_modifier = abm_secured_dalmatia` -> `has_estate_privilege = estate_privilege:abm_secured_dalmatia` in `in_game/common/advances/abm_italy.txt`.
  - Decision `potential` now checks `NOT = { has_variable = abm_secured_dalmatia }` (was `has_country_modifier`), and its desc interpolates `$abm_secured_dalmatia$` instead of `$STATIC_MODIFIER_NAME_abm_secured_dalmatia$`.
  - Note: `abm_italy.txt` no longer carries the "# Ars Belli: the vanilla Dalmatian advances..." header - the host removed it when committing "Tuscany + TWS update".
- **`abm_secured_dalmatia` bonuses gated on holding Dalmatia (2026-11-16).** The privilege is now a single `location_modifier` (`potential_trigger = { area = area:croatia_area }`, `local_monthly_control = 0.002`, `local_monthly_development = 0.001`), so it only touches locations of that area. The country-level `navy_galley_power` / `trade_sea_efficiency` versions were dropped by the host - those two have no location-scope form, so they cannot live in a `location_modifier`. Note `croatia_area` is the only area containing Dalmatia (it also holds `istria_province` and `lika_province` - senj crikvenica kaseg krk otocac pag rijeka), and `local_monthly_development` is **not** a percent modifier (`decimals = 4`), so `0.001` is a flat +0.001/month while `local_monthly_control` (percent = yes) shows `0.002` as +0.2%.
  - **Modifier scope registry (authoritative, 1.4.4): `main_menu/common/modifier_type_definitions/00_modifier_types.txt`** - every modifier key with its `game_data/category` (all 7 / character 47 / country 2006 / internationalorganization 100 / location 423 / province 1 / rebel 1 / religion 3 / unit 70). `local_*` names are registered keys, not a prefix rule. Confirmed there: `navy_galley_power` = **unit**, `trade_sea_efficiency` = **country**, `local_monthly_control`/`global_monthly_control` = separate **location**/**country** keys - i.e. galley power and sea trade efficiency have no location-scope counterpart (the only `local_navy_*` keys are `local_navy_attrition` and `local_navy_levy_size_modifier`).
  - The engine's own readme (`estate_privileges/readme.txt`) documents `country_modifier` as a "scaled and triggered modifier (scale = script for the scale, potential_trigger = trigger for if the modifier applies)", which is the documented way to make a privilege's effect regional.
  - Deliberately NOT added to `potential`: a privilege that is already granted should not depend on a mutable condition there, because the 12 Dalmatian advances gate on `has_estate_privilege` and the decision is `only_once` - losing Dalmatia would then switch the advances off for good. `potential_trigger` degrades gracefully instead (privilege stays, bonuses stop).
  - Trigger shape proven by vanilla: `estate_privileges/nobles_estate.txt:1979` (`lon_principado_de_asturias.on_activate`: `province_definition:east_asturias_province = { every_location_in_province_definition = { limit = { owner = root } ... } }`), and `root` inside a country `potential_trigger` in `auto_modifiers/country.txt:1219` (`is_an_attacker = root`) plus `estate_privileges/clergy_estate.txt:95`.
  - `navy_galley_power` and `trade_sea_efficiency` have no `local_*` variants (only `local_monthly_control` exists), so the bonuses cannot be moved into `location_modifier` - `country_modifier` + `potential_trigger` is the only option. `abm_secured_dalmatia_desc` (loc) was extended to say the payout requires the Dalmatian coast.
  - Area scope is supported but there is **no `dalmatia_area`** in 1.4.4: `grep dalmat map_data/definitions.txt` matches only the two province definitions, which sit in `croatia_area` alongside `istria_province` and `lika_province` (senj crikvenica kaseg krk otocac pag rijeka dinaric_alps9), i.e. `area = area:croatia_area` is a strict superset of Dalmatia. Location-scope `area =` / `province_definition =` are proven triggers (`cabinet_actions/swe_slash_and_burn_new_farms.txt:29-35`, inside a `select_trigger` `visible`), and the country iterators are `any_owned_location` (`decisions/coal_prospecting.txt:20`) / `any_location_in_area` (`area:desert_area = { any_location_in_area = { owner ?= root } }`, `decisions/maghrebi_culture_decisons.txt:48`).
  - Country-scope "owns all of it" triggers exist and render with proper loc: `own_entire_province = province_definition:X` / `own_entire_area = area:X` (`trigger_localization/country_triggers.txt:2659,2667`; used at `advances/country_SCA.txt:27`, `dlc/D009_*/events/d009_mudejar_events.txt:182`). The decision's verbose `NOT = { any_location_in_province_definition = { is_ownable = yes NOT = { owner ?= root } } }` could be replaced with two `own_entire_province` lines if the wasteland caveat turns out to be unnecessary.
- **`new_colonial_nation_founded` reworked into a per-location, once-ever boom (2026-11-16).** The location-category static modifier in `main_menu/common/static_modifiers/abm_country_changes.txt` is now applied only through the scripted effect `abm_new_colonial_nation_boom` (`in_game/common/scripted_effects/abm_scripted_effects.txt`, location scope): it checks `NOT = { has_variable = abm_new_colonial_nation_founded }`, sets that location variable, then `add_location_modifier = { modifier = new_colonial_nation_founded years = 35 }`.
  - New hook file `in_game/common/on_action/abm_colonial_nation_on_actions.txt` defines `on_location_changed_owner` (vanilla `common/on_action/_hardcoded.txt:4859`: root = location, `scope:loser` = previous owner, `scope:winner` = new owner, may be null) -> `if = { limit = { exists = scope:winner  scope:winner = { is_colonial_subject = yes } } abm_new_colonial_nation_boom = yes }`. `is_colonial_subject` is the CN test (`subject_types/colonial_nation.txt:53`, used as `owner ?= { is_colonial_subject = yes }` in `building_types/plantation_buildings.txt:18`).
  - `REPLACE:setup_colonial_nation` no longer loops the capital region: the old `capital.region = { every_location_in_region = { limit = { owner = root } add_location_modifier ... } }` became `every_owned_location = { abm_new_colonial_nation_boom = yes }`, kept as a fallback for the founding transfer in case the owner-change hook does not fire for subject creation.
  - Once-only flag is a **location variable** - legal: `every_location_in_religious_order = { set_variable = { name = recent_order_service value = yes days = 1825 } }` (`generic_actions/religious_orders.txt:261`) and `any_location_in_region = { has_variable = is_a_center_of_renaissance_humanism }` (`events/culture/culture_italian.txt:385`). No `days =` on our variable, so it never expires.
  - Open finding: `build_gravel_road_cost_modifier` / `build_paved_road_cost_modifier` / `build_modern_road_cost_modifier` in that same static modifier block are **country** modifiers per `00_modifier_types.txt` (lines 979/987/995) while the block is `game_data = { category = location }`, so they are inert where the privilege/modifier is applied per location; there is no location-scope road-cost key (only `local_road_building_time`).
- **Colonial boom hardened (2026-11-16, same session).** Three fixes after a review of the rework:
  - `build_gravel_road_cost_modifier` / `build_paved_road_cost_modifier` / `build_modern_road_cost_modifier` were **removed** from `REPLACE:new_colonial_nation_founded` (`main_menu/common/static_modifiers/abm_country_changes.txt`): they are `category = country` in `00_modifier_types.txt` while the block is `category = location`, so the engine logs `Modifier type 'X' is only allowed in (country) but only (location) is accepted here` and drops them. There is no location-scope road-cost key; `local_road_building_time` is the only road lever available to a location modifier.
  - `on_location_changed_owner` in `in_game/common/on_action/abm_colonial_nation_on_actions.txt` gained an `else_if` that runs `remove_location_modifier = new_colonial_nation_founded` when the location still carries it and the new owner is not a colonial subject - so the boom stops benefiting whoever captures the land from the CN. The location variable is untouched, so "once ever" still holds. Keys proven: `has_location_modifier` (`trigger_localization/location_triggers.txt:146`), `remove_location_modifier` (`effect_localization/location_effects.txt:22`, bare form per `scripted_effects/location_effects.txt:208`). Both branches guard with `exists = scope:winner` because the hook's winner can be null.
  - Deleted `in_game/common/scripted_effects/abm_country_effects.txt` - it was 16 lines, every line commented, documenting the dead `add_country_modifier` version of the same setup.
  - Residual unknown: no vanilla hook exists for "location colonized" other than `on_location_changed_owner`, so a CN colonizing directly must go through it - unconfirmed without a game run. All CN land-gain paths found in vanilla do go through an owner change (`colonial_charter_goals/form_colonial_nation.txt:59-64` creation, `colonial_charter_goals/expand_subject.txt:87` `change_location_owner`).
- **Colonial boom: growth bonus split off for frontier settlements only (2026-11-16).** `local_population_growth = 0.020` was removed from `REPLACE:new_colonial_nation_founded` and now lives in a new location-category static modifier `abm_colonial_frontier_boom` (same file, loc key `STATIC_MODIFIER_NAME_abm_colonial_frontier_boom: "Frontier Settlement"` in `main_menu/localization/english/abm_modifiers_l_english.yml`).
  - `abm_new_colonial_nation_boom` applies it through a nested `if = { limit = { population < 5 } ... }`: a province that already has people on it must not get +2% growth (it compounds), while a raw settlement should.
  - **`population` is in thousands** (`loading_screen/common/defines/00_defines.txt:1178`: "Location.GetTotalPopulation(), which is itself scaled (1 unit = 1000 actual pops)"; same as the workspace note `EU5 Location Mechanics (1.4.4).md:14`), so the gate `population < 5` = **under 5,000 people**, and `local_population_growth` is **per year**, not per month (`yearly = local_population_growth + global_population_growth`).
  - **The number 5 is doubly vanilla-anchored**: it is the town-upgrade population threshold (`in_game/common/location_ranks/00_default.txt:175` `population >= 5`; city = 30, megalopolis = 400) and the sparse-province test in `cabinet_actions/swe_slash_and_burn_new_farms.txt:27` (`province_average_population < 5`). `population` is a general trigger with loc (`trigger_localization/common_triggers.txt:293`) and is written without a scope prefix in location scope.
  - Scale check from the 1337 setup (`main_menu/setup/1337/06_pops.txt`, sum of pop `size` per location): worldwide median 8.2, p25 2.0, p75 22.0; Venice 115.6, Paris 254.4, tenochtitlan 87.0; 39% of all locations are under 5. **American locations** (every location of the `america` continent in `map_data/definitions.txt:5881-9124`): median 0.28, p90 1.46, max 5.74 - i.e. 978 of 979 are under 5, so the gate never excludes a real colony, only developed Old World towns.
  - If the other frontier-flavoured keys (output bonuses, `local_pop_promotion_speed_modifier`, `local_road_building_time`) should also be frontier-only, move them into `abm_colonial_frontier_boom` - it is the designated frontier bundle.
- **Colonial growth surge now capped by capacity, not by settlement age (2026-11-16).** The `population < 5` gate was replaced with a 50%-of-capacity ceiling, which self-normalises regardless of who owns the location. `abm_colonial_frontier_boom` was renamed **`abm_colonial_growth_boom`** (id, loc key `STATIC_MODIFIER_NAME_abm_colonial_growth_boom: "Settlement Growth"`, comments all updated).
  - Grant side: `abm_new_colonial_nation_boom` adds it only when `location_population_percentage < 0.5`, always for 35 years.
  - Lapse side: a static modifier cannot carry a condition, so `in_game/common/on_action/abm_colonial_nation_on_actions.txt` hooks `monthly_country_pulse` (project convention: `on_actions = { <helper> }`) with `abm_colonial_boom_growth_ceiling`: `trigger = { is_colonial_subject = yes }` (deliberately NO `any_owned_location` pre-check - the `every_owned_location` loop with its `limit` is already one cheap pass and the pre-check would double it) then `every_owned_location = { limit = { has_location_modifier = abm_colonial_growth_boom  location_population_percentage >= 0.5 } remove_location_modifier = abm_colonial_growth_boom }`. The CN guard keeps the monthly cost off every other country.
  - `location_population_percentage` is a location trigger meaning "has X% of [population_capacity]" (`trigger_localization/location_triggers.txt:308`, text `triggers_l_english.yml:2277`), used bare in location scope (`building_types/rural_buildings.txt:750`).
  - The `on_location_changed_owner` else_if now strips **both** the base modifier and the growth modifier when the land leaves a colonial subject.
  - **Cost, measured against the map:** `map_data/definitions.txt` gives the `america` continent 889 province definitions over 1,367 locations, avg 1.5 locations per province - so a colonial nation is 1-3 locations and the whole colonisable New World is the hard bound (~1,400 location visits a month, realistically a few hundred). For non-CN countries the helper costs a single boolean. Vanilla's own `monthly_country_pulse` already runs 17 sub-on_actions plus a random-event pool for every country (`on_action/country_monthly.txt`). If it ever needs to be cheaper, swap `monthly_country_pulse` for `yearly_country_pulse` in the same file - one line, 12x less work.
- **RESOLVED (2026-11-16): EU5 MERGES same-name on_action blocks, it does not replace them.** Vanilla proves it by defining `on_winning_war` / `on_ending_war` twice with complementary children - `_hardcoded.txt:1534` has `effect = { crusade victor ... }` while `religious_order_conquest.txt:5` has `on_actions = { religious_order_conquest_offer_pulse }`; vanilla's religious-order conquest feature needs both, so the blocks must combine. Same for `on_royal_marriage` and `on_truce_broken` (`diplomatic_reactions.txt:72,95` vs `_hardcoded.txt:6137,6481`). Safe to add `on_actions` children to `monthly_country_pulse` from mod files.
- **Steppe RGO decisions now demand full province ownership (2026-11-16).** New country-scope scripted trigger `abm_owns_entire_province` in `in_game/common/scripted_triggers/abm_pontic_steppe.txt`:
  ```
  abm_owns_entire_province = { $PROVINCE$ = { NOT = { any_location_in_province = { is_ownable = yes NOT = { owner ?= $COUNTRY$ } } } } }
  ```
  Both parameters are explicit (`PROVINCE = province:X COUNTRY = root`) on purpose - the file's own `abm_steppe_pastoral_location` already passes `$COUNTRY$` rather than relying on `root` inside a nested scope block. `$PROVINCE$ = { ... }` with a scope parameter as block root is the pattern already documented in this file; `PROVINCE = this` works too (vanilla `events/DHE/flavor_ver.txt:82`).
  - Applied to all 14 province decisions: 11 in `decisions/abm_steppe_minerals_decisions.txt` (replacing the partial `owns = location:a/b` runs) and 3 in `decisions/abm_pontic_steppe_decisions.txt` (or_qapi 1 location, cafa 3 of 5, gothia 4 of 5).
  - Also folded into `abm_steppe_pastoral_province` (province scope), so `abm_reorganize_pastoral_rgo_decision`, `abm_establish_timber_working_decision` and the generic action `abm_steppe_rgo_conversion` all require the whole province; that trigger previously only required every **owned** land location to be the country's.
  - `abm_settle_the_steppe` (cabinet action, `in_game/common/cabinet_actions/abm_settle_the_steppe.txt`) got the same rule: the inlined `NOT = { any_location_in_province = { has_owner = yes is_land = yes NOT = { owner ?= scope:actor } } }` in `select_trigger.visible` was replaced by `abm_owns_entire_province = { PROVINCE = this COUNTRY = scope:actor }`, and `allow`'s province-scope `owner ?= root` (that idiom is unverified for a province scope - only locations carry `owner`) by the same trigger with `COUNTRY = root`.
  - Coverage audit: `grep -rln change_raw_material in_game/` returns only the two decision files, `abm_steppe_rgo_conversion.txt` and the paste-in template `events/DHE/abm_flavor_abtau.txt` (per-location event, not province-scoped) - so **all** RGO-changing content is covered. The remaining steppes `any_location_in_province` uses are goods tests (lumber), not ownership ones.
  - Not converted on purpose: `abm_hanseatic_federation_decision` (deliberate graduated 70% thresholds per area/province) and `abm_gazaria_culture_choice` (`owns = theodoro/lusta`) - the latter is a candidate for the same treatment if the host asks.
  - Every one of the 14 provinces lies in `region:steppes_region` and is 100% owned by an owner at 1337 (checked against `main_menu/setup/1337/10_countries_*.txt`), so nothing is blocked: the only locations without an owner are `crimean_mountains` (`topography = mountain_wasteland`), `lake_sasyk_syvash` (`lakes`), `syvash_lagoon`, `syvash_sand_bar` (`flatland_wasteland`) - all excluded by `is_ownable = yes`. `province:X_province` is a real prefix (`peace_treaties/scaligeri_peace_treaties.txt:119`), distinct from `province_definition:`.

## Google Sheets: advance-list spreadsheet (2026-11-17)
- Doc "ABM France + Germany + Baltics + russia": `https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1D65i5OMrR_DFL5NInMsP0TT4ntmldJUk79HEISX97ks/edit`
  - Tabs/gids (renamed by user 2026-11-17, gids unchanged): `abm_germany`=1853901682 (tab 1 = the only sheet we work on, 360 rows), `OLD1`=1703281304 (327 rows, old Sheet1), `OLD2`=2001994915 (273 rows, old abm_german), `Sheet2`=1651186037, `MUS->RUS`=830316332.
  - Columns: `tag/culture`, `region`, `Modded`, `tier`, `age`, `advance_name`, `modifier - NEW`, `modifier - OLD`, `eu4_ideas`.
- Sharing is "anyone with the link can EDIT" -> agent edits **anonymously**; browser shows a "Zaloguj się" link, ignore it, Insert/Format menus are enabled = editor mode.
- **READ (no browser needed):** `curl "https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/<ID>/gviz/tq?tqx=out:csv&sheet=<TabName>"` -> whole tab as CSV. `export?format=csv&gid=<gid>` also works.
- **Local mirror:** `python3 .tools/scripts/sheets_sync.py [TabName] [--out path]` refreshes `.tools/sheets/abm_germany.csv` (360 rows, default tab). Always run it after an edit session and diff against the previous copy to confirm only intended cells changed.
- **WRITE (browser only):** the Sheets API is NOT usable (gcloud account is a work account, token refresh fails, `sheets.googleapis.com` returns 403). Use the browser page:
  1. Dismiss any `[role=alertdialog]` "Wystąpił problem" (click its `OK`). While open it swallows all input AND makes navigation silently fail.
  2. Navigate: click `#t-name-box` -> `selectText()` -> `keyboard.type('D17')` -> `Enter` -> wait -> **verify** `#t-name-box` value == target, else abort. Never use `fill()`: it can submit a mangled name (-> "Nazwa nadana temu zakresowi jest nieprawidłowa") and leave the selection on a stale cell.
  3. Edit: click `#t-formula-bar-input .cell-input` -> `page.evaluate(() => document.execCommand('selectAll'))` -> `keyboard.type(value)` -> verify `#t-formula-bar-input` innerText == value -> `Enter`.
  4. Verify by re-reading the CSV and diffing against a saved baseline; wait for "Zapisano na Dysku" first (an immediate CSV read still shows the pre-commit value).
- Gotchas: Cmd/Meta shortcuts do NOT reach the page (Cmd+A / Cmd+V dead -> no clipboard bulk paste, hence `execCommand('selectAll')`); typing right after name-box navigation can go into the name box instead of the grid; the formula bar always edits whichever cell is *currently active*, so confirm identity (name box == target, formula bar == known old value) before committing, then `Escape` to discard a bad edit.

### `abm_germany` column G ("modifier - NEW") normalised to canonical keys (2026-11-17)
- Column G now holds the SHEET'S INTENT written with real EU5 modifier keys: prose entries like `<cabinet seat = 1>` / `<herretic conversion speed = 0.10>` were translated to `government_size = 1` / `global_heretic_pop_conversion_speed_modifier = 0.10` (typos fixed, `small|medium|large|mild` magnitudes expanded to the game's named tokens, e.g. `tax_income_efficiency = medium_tax_income_efficiency_bonus`). 65 prose cells rewritten this way; the 39 cells that already held a real key were aligned to the game files. Total 104 cells differ from the original.
- Rules kept: rows whose `advance_name` starts with `-` untouched; advances granting unlock effects (`unlock_unit/building/policy/government_reform/chivalric_order`, `can_hire_privateers`) untouched; cells containing a `don't touch` note (rows 144, 146) untouched.
- Validation: 151 distinct keys used, all present in vanilla advance files / the modifier CSVs; named tokens match vanilla usage (`large_permanent_target_satisfaction` only appears in societal_values, still valid).
- Judgement calls where no exact key existed: `burghers trade` -> `global_trades_per_burgher`, `urban prosperity` -> `global_non_rural_monthly_prosperity`, `global alcohol output` -> `global_liquor_output_modifier`, `global estate max tax = medium` -> `global_estate_max_tax = 0.1` (numeric-only key), `merc maint cost mod = -0.20` -> `mercenary_maintenance_efficiency = 0.20` (EU4 cost sign flipped).
- CONSEQUENCE: 66 rows in column G now differ from what `abm_german.txt` / vanilla actually implements (they document intent). Column G is a design column, not a mirror of the code - do not "re-sync" it without asking.
- Diff of all 104 changed rows lives in the session files dir (`sheet_modifier_changes.csv`).

### `abm_germany` name-collision marks: `--abm_xxx` (2026-11-17)
- Column F convention: `abm_xxx` / `-abm_xxx` becomes **`--abm_xxx`** when the SAME tag/culture already has a vanilla advance named `xxx` (that is, the ABM advance duplicates a name the tag can already get). 17 rows marked.
- Detection that works: for each `-*abm_<base>` row, look up `<base>` in `in_game/common/advances/*.txt` then `.tools/eu5/advances/*.txt` and compare the row's tag/culture against the base advance's `potential`. Tag gating is not always `has_or_had_tag`: vanilla also uses `culture = culture:lorrain`/`merged_culture_group_contains_culture` (LOR rows 39/41) and `is_swiss_for_advances = yes` (SWI rows 323-326) - a `has_or_had_tag`-only check misses those 6.
- Marked rows: 39, 41 (LOR), 104, 105, 109, 110 (BRA), 127, 128, 129, 131 (PRU), 180 (HSA), 273, 276 (BOH), 323, 324, 325, 326 (SWI).
- Left with a single dash on purpose: row 106 `-abm_divided_estates` (its vanilla counterpart is named `divide_estates`, so not the same name) and row 145 `-goose_step` (a vanilla advance, not `abm_`).

### `abm_german.txt` synced from the sheet: 17 disabled + 34 re-aged (2026-11-17)
- **`--` sheet mark = disabled advance.** 17 ABM advances whose name collides with a vanilla advance of the same name for the same tag are now fully commented out in `in_game/common/advances/abm_german.txt`, each preceded by `# ABM 2026-11-17: disabled - duplicates a vanilla advance of the same name for the same tag (sheet mark "--<name>").` Commenting = prefix every line of the block with `#` (blank lines become `#` too); already-commented inner lines are left alone. Active advances in the file went 260 -> 243.
- Disabled: `abm_lor_between_great_powers`, `abm_lor_three_bishoprics`, `abm_northern_march`, `abm_kammergericht`, `abm_iron_tooth`, `abm_margrave_council`, `abm_teutonic_legacy`, `abm_soldier_king`, `abm_army_professionalism`, `abm_goose_step`, `abm_the_hansa_bank`, `abm_wagenburg`, `abm_kutnohorian_deal`, `abm_swiss_mercenaries`, `abm_swiss_tolerance`, `abm_the_swiss_confederation`, `abm_alpine_defensiveness`.
- **Ages synced to the sheet** (sheet is the source of truth): 34 advances re-aged, all in `abm_german.txt`, mostly the tag groups LOT, HAN, SHL, WES, PRU, german_ideas and GMA shifting one age later (`age_1 -> age_2` ... `age_4 -> age_5`); e.g. `abm_wes_ideas_tradition_1` age_1_traditions -> age_2_renaissance, `abm_zunft` age_2 -> age_3. The 35th mismatch (`abm_soldier_king`) was skipped because that advance is disabled.
- Safety checks done: nothing outside the loc file references the 17 names (no `requires`, no GUI/JS); every surviving block is byte-identical apart from its `age =` line; disabled blocks survive verbatim inside comments; braces balanced; BOM + CRLF preserved (the file is CRLF - a plain LF rewrite produces a whole-file diff).
- Localization entries for the 17 disabled advances were left in place (harmless orphan keys).

### `abm_german.txt` requires fixed to same-age anchors (2026-11-17)
- Rule (verified against the game): **vanilla has zero cross-age prerequisites** - an advance of age N always requires another advance of age N (`requires` is the tree link inside the age, not across ages). `abm_german.txt` had 77 advances requiring an earlier age.
- Fix = move each advance one rung up the file's own anchor ladder, so `requires` is always same-age:
  age_1 `feudalism_advance` | age_2 `renaissance_thought` | age_3 `paved_road_advance` (admin/economy) or `military_traditions` (military) | age_4 `global_trade_advance` or `supply_depot_advance_age_4_reformation` | age_5 `absolutist_court` | age_6 `enlightened_court` (85 vanilla advances use it; also used by abm_anatolia/balkans/crescent).
- Applied: feudalism->renaissance_thought x14, legalism->renaissance_thought x17 (both age_2), legalism->paved_road_advance x6 and renaissance_thought->paved_road_advance x5 (age_3), paved_road_advance->global_trade_advance x5 (age_4), global_trade_advance->absolutist_court x4 (age_5), imperial_ambitions->enlightened_court x26 (age_6 ambitions; `imperial_ambitions` is age_4).
- Post-state: 243/243 advances have a resolvable, same-age prerequisite; all anchors are generic vanilla advances with no `potential` block, so every German tag can take them. Nothing else in the file changed.
- Note: `abm_france.txt` and `abm_iberia.txt` still carry the same age_6 bug (`*_ideas_ambition` requires `imperial_ambitions`, age_4).

### Requires policy: generic, same-age prerequisites only (2026-11-17)
- **Rule (user decision):** every `requires` must point at an advance of the SAME age that is available to ANY nation. No `potential`, `government`, `religion`, `culture`, `has_or_had_tag`, `has_variable`, `has_reform` gate - neither on the target nor anywhere in its own `requires` chain. Focus-pick advances (`has_variable = abm_focus_*`) are never eligible.
- Implemented in `.tools/scripts/update_advance_names_icons_requires2.py` (the maintained tool; it also does icons):
  * `parse_advance_block` now records nested block headers, and every reference entry gets `gates` (blocks + fields; `potential` is listed in both because it is often written inline: `potential = { has_or_had_tag = X }`).
  * `Reference._mark_generic()` computes `generic` = ungated AND every ancestor ungated; `pick_requires()` only considers generic candidates; new `requirement_ok(key, age)` validates a curated value.
  * `AGE_REQUIRES[3]` fixed: `artillery` was `unlock_chambered_cannon_advance` (age 4) and `cavalry` was `horse_riding_advance` (age 1) - now `fort_limit_3_advance` / `pike_square`.
  * `GENERIC_FALLBACK` (per age: feudalism_advance / renaissance_thought / paved_road_advance / global_trade_advance / absolutist_court / enlightened_court) is used when the curated requirement is stale or no generic same-age candidate exists.
  * An existing value is KEPT when it already satisfies the rules, so re-runs are no-ops (verified: second run reports 0 changes).
  * `abm_focus_picks_*.txt` are skipped by default - they are generated by `focus_picks/generate_focus_picks.js` and their chains are intra-branch (same focus gate) by design.
- Applied 2026-11-17: 253 prerequisites re-pointed across 38 files (105 cross-age + 148 gated). Post-state verified by an independent scanner: 2,713 prerequisites, 0 dead, 0 cross-age, 0 gated outside the focus-pick files; icons untouched (`--requires`); BOM/CRLF preserved.
- Pre-existing bug found (NOT introduced): `in_game/common/advances/abm_china_west.txt` has 163 `{` vs 162 `}` at HEAD - one block is unterminated.

### Requires + icons: basic-only prerequisites, one icon per set (2026-11-17, final policy)
- **Prerequisites = BASIC advances only.** For each age the tool derives the "basic" set at load time = generic roots of that age (no `requires` of their own, no `potential`/government/religion/culture/tag/focus gate, and no gated ancestor). Counts today: age1=6, age2=5, age3=11, age4=13, age5=14, age6=13. `pick_requires()` draws ONLY from that set, preferring the same thematic type, then least-used, then the curated/age-default value, then most-referenced root; no hash rotation any more (that is what used to scatter prerequisites). `requirement_ok()` = "is a basic advance of this age".
- Because `AGE_REQUIRES` / `MOD_MAP` values are only *preferences*, they are silently ignored when not basic; e.g. `military_traditions` (government-gated) can never be written again.
- **Icons: one per advance set, hard rule.** Group = `potential_signature` (the potential block) - an "idea set" of one tag/culture. `used_by_group` is now a `Counter` per group (a plain set collapsed two advances already sharing an icon and made both look unique - that bug hid every pre-existing duplicate). Seeded from the file before transforming; the block's own icon does not block itself; `choose_icon()` hard-excludes icons already used by the other advances of the set. Existing icons that are already unique are left alone, so `--icons` is surgical (no global restyle) unless `--force`.
- Applied 2026-11-17: 2,258 prerequisites re-pointed to basic roots (every non-focus advance now requires a root of its own age - 2,563 checked, 0 violations), and 23 duplicate icons fixed + 34 missing icons added; 0 duplicate sets remain outside the generated focus-pick files. Both modes are idempotent (re-run reports 0 changes).
- Still open: `abm_focus_picks_economic.txt` (11 sets) and `abm_focus_picks_military.txt` (9 sets) still contain duplicate icons - they are generated from `.tools/scripts/focus_picks/focus_data.js` (66 authored `icon:` values), so fix them in the data + regenerate, not here.

### Requires must also match the theme (2026-11-17)
- Added theme-aware basic pools: `Reference.basic_type[age][type]` = the generic ROOTS of that age+type, or - when an age has no root of that type - its depth-1 generic advances (`Reference.depth`). `Reference.pool(age, type)` returns the same-theme pool and, only when that theme has nothing in the age, walks `TYPE_FALLBACK` (naval->military, estate->administration, diplomacy->administration, subject->diplomacy, ...).
- `pick_requires` / `requirement_ok(key, age, type_)` / the keep-rule are all type-aware now, so a prerequisite is always a basic advance of the advance's own age AND theme where that theme exists.
- Result after applying: 2,563 prerequisites, 0 violations; **90.2% are the exact same theme**, the other 9.8% are the nearest related theme because the age has no basic advance of that theme (diplomacy->administration x128, naval->military x54, estate->administration x56, subject x8). Both `--requires` and `--icons` remain idempotent.

### Theme matching, round 2: classifier fixes (2026-11-17)
- **`unlock_unit` no longer defaults to military.** `classify_by_effects` now inspects the unlocked unit's name: galley/ship/cog/carrack/caravel/galleon/frigate/fluyt/xebec/dromond/dhow/junk/barge/transport/privateer/indiaman/threedecker/twodecker/brig/sloop/pinnace/merchantman/galleass/galiot/gunboat/corvette/clipper/schooner/hoy/hulk/crayer/balinger/nau/carvel -> naval. This is what made `abm_mys_ideas_tradition_2` (land general training) require `unlock_traditional_galley_advance`.
- **Pools are now "shallowest available" per (age, theme)** instead of "roots or depth-1": `Reference.generic_by_type[age][type]` keeps every generic advance with its root distance, and `pool()` returns only the smallest distance tier for that theme. So an age with no military root gets its shallowest military advance (e.g. age_1 military -> `power_projection_advance_1`, age_6 military -> `conscription_advance`/`levee_en_masse_advance`) instead of borrowing another theme.
- Result: 2,563 prerequisites, 0 violations, **98.2% exact same theme**; the only remaining related-theme matches are 41 estate->administration (ages 4-6 have no generic estate advance at all) and 4 subject->diplomacy. `--requires` and `--icons` still idempotent.
- Icons are deduplicated but NOT re-themed by default (a non-duplicate icon is kept). `--icons --force` re-picks them from the advance's type; that is a ~2k-icon pass, run only on request.

## 2026-11-17 - update_advance_names_icons_requires2.py refactor (1516 -> 1152 lines)
De-bloating pass; behavior verified unchanged after every splice (`--requires --dry-run` and `--icons --dry-run` both report 0 changes, `ast.parse` clean).
- **Removed the curated tables**: `MOD_MAP` (~258 lines of per-modifier icon/requires/category), `ICON_POOLS`, `DEFAULT_MAP`, `category_family()`, `lookup_mod_map()`. They were fully redundant once classification became thematic (removing them caused 0 churn).
- **`MOD_FAMILY` survived** as a compact `family|key key key` block (183 keys, 11 families) parsed into a dict - it only decides the *theme*, not icons/prerequisites.
- **`AGE_REQUIRES` kept** (compact `age trade=x naval=y ...` lines into a dict). It is NOT redundant: dropping it and deriving the anchor from the reference table produces 387 requires changes.
- **`strip_line`** is now a 3-line regex (`"[^"]*"|#.*`), **`find_top_level_blocks`** ~12 lines with an early-exit key regex, **`_load_advance_files`** reads each reference file once (icons + advances in the same pass, previously 2-3 reads).
- **`choose_icon`/`classify_entry`/`mod_family`/`get_age_require`** collapsed to one-liners where possible; CLI options are generated from a `CLI_FLAGS` spec table; module docstring rewritten to describe the actual policy (the old one still advertised the curated mapping).
- **Fixed a latent bug** in `choose_icon`: the "no valid icon candidates" ValueError referenced an undefined `family` name, so that branch raised NameError instead of the intended message.
- Round-trip tested on a copy of `abm_india_deccan.txt`: broken `requires` restored byte-identically; a deleted `icon` line re-inserted right after `age`; a duplicated icon inside a `potential` group replaced. BOM + CRLF preserved in all cases. Note `--force` deliberately re-picks icons, so it is not a round-trip test.
- Still open (unchanged): 20 duplicate icon sets inside the generated `abm_focus_picks_economic/military.txt`, which come from `.tools/scripts/focus_picks/focus_data.js`; `abm_china_west.txt` has a pre-existing brace imbalance (163 `{` vs 162 `}`) that also exists at HEAD.

## 2026-11-17 - custom unit advances mirror their stock unit's prerequisites
The user's rule: an advance that unlocks a custom `abm_*` unit must require what the vanilla advance for the REGULAR unit of the same type and age requires, so both sit on the same row of the tech tree ("siblings, not children" - the requirement is copied, not the stock advance itself).
- Slot of a custom unit comes from its own definition: `copy_from = a_age_3_discovery_heavy_infantry` (or `category` + `age`), i.e. `age_3_discovery` + `heavy_infantry`. `unit_slots()` in the tool builds that map from `in_game/common/unit_types/*.txt` (note the `COPY_FROM_RE` must not use `\w+` for the age part, or `heavy_infantry` gets swallowed).
- `STOCK_UNIT_REQUIRES` maps `(age, category)` -> the prerequisite of the vanilla advance unlocking that slot, taken from `.tools/eu5/advances/2_army_unlocks.txt`: e.g. age 3 heavy infantry = `unlock_halberdiers_advance` -> `training_fields_advance`; age 4 = `unlock_pikemen_advance` -> `barracks_advance`; age 5 = `unlock_musketeers_advance` -> `unlock_hunters_advance`; age 6 = `unlock_fusiliers_advance` -> `conscription_center_advance`. All 20 targets are generic, ungated, same-age advances (verified against the reference table).
- This is the single documented exception to the "basic root only" policy; it applies to every advance with an `unlock_unit` whose unit resolves to a slot (stock or custom), and it is idempotent.
- Applied to 45 advances in `abm_units_arabia/india/italy/steppes.txt` (only `requires =` lines changed; BOM + CRLF preserved, braces balanced). `REPLACE:genoese_galley` is skipped: `n_genoese_galley` has no `copy_from`/`category`, so it has no slot.

## 2026-11-17 - German formables: three-area tier 3 tiles (WES, SWA, FKN)
User rule: a *regional* German formable is `level = 3` and spans **three areas**, and the potentials of
different tiles are allowed to cross. Only the three below follow it so far; SHL (holstein_area), HAN
(lower_saxony_area) and POM (pomerania_area) are still vanilla single-area level 2.
- `WES_f`: westphalia_area + rhineland_area + hesse_area = **120 locations**, `required_locations_fraction = 0.6` (72). The user set `level = 3` and emptied `allow` (dropped the vanilla "KOL gone / age 6" gate).
- `SWA_f`: swabia_area + upper_rhine_area + alsace_area = **80 locations**, fraction 0.6 (48). Was one area at 0.8.
- `FKN_f`: franconia_area + upper_rhine_area + hesse_area = **103 locations**, fraction 0.6 (62). Was `level = 2`, one area.
- Shared shape: `potential` = `culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:german_group }` plus an OR of `original_capital.area = area:<each of the three>`. `original_capital.area` is vanilla-attested (`in_game/common/advances/region_north_america.txt:30`); every German culture is in `german_group` (`culture_groups/00_culture_groups.txt:90`), so the area list is the real gate.
- Overlap is deliberate: upper_rhine_area is in both SWA and FKN, hesse_area in both FKN and WES.
- Region sizes (parsed from `map_data/definitions.txt`): north_german_region 419 locations / 14 areas, south_german_region 380 / 16. Fractions were set by hand from those totals, not from vanilla.
- Checker 0 errors on the file and on the whole `formable_countries/` folder. In-game confirm still needed (new campaign) for the three requirement lists.
- Same day, follow-up: `HAN_f` and `FKN_f` converted to the same three-area tier 3 shape.
  - `HAN_f`: `level = 2 -> 3`, areas lower_saxony_area + holstein_area + mecklenburg_area = **82 locations**, fraction 0.6 (~49).
  - `FKN_f`: the user replaced the area set - now franconia_area + swabia_area + bavaria_area = **118 locations** (~71 at 0.6), and the `original_capital.area` OR was changed to match. swabia_area is therefore shared by SWA and FKN.
  - `HAN_f` kept its vanilla `allow = { owns = location:hanover }`; note that this now also gates a Holstein/Mecklenburg-rooted contender.
  - Still single-area level 2: SHL (holstein_area), POM (pomerania_area at fraction 1.0 with its own area capital gate).
  - Hansa research (same day): EU5 has exactly one Hansa tag - `HSA: "Hansa"` / `HSA_ADJ: "Hanseatic"` (country_names_l_english.yml:69) - and no Hansa international organization (nothing in `in_game/common/international_organizations/`). EU4 also uses `HSA` (`HSA_ideas` at `.tools/eu4/ideas/00_country_ideas.txt:2697`) and additionally has LUB/HAM/BRE/RIG with idea sets. In this mod HSA owns lubeck+hamburg+bremen at start and LUB/HAM/BRM are landless tags holding `our_cores_conquered_by_others` on their own city.
